[1] MInfoVisQA: A Multilingual Benchmark for Information-Rich Visual Question Answering cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Changjiang Jiang, Jiang Zhou, Xinbin Yuan, Fan Zhang, Tao Feng

TL;DR: 论文提出 MInfoVisQA，一个面向信息丰富可视化（InfoVis）的多语言视觉问答基准，包含 24 种语言、128 个种子实例扩展出的 8,960 个 QA 配置。作者通过分离数值-结构数据与文本标签并程序化同步翻译，构建语言平行的视觉与问答对，并在语言对齐 QA（LQA）和跨语言 QA（XQA）两种设置下评测 25 个多模态大模型。

Details

Motivation: 多模态大模型在视觉语言任务上表现优异，但当表面语言变化时，它们对信息丰富可视化的理解是否保持一致尚不清楚，论文旨在量化这一跨语言鲁棒性问题。

Result: 实验显示聚合准确率会掩盖显著的跨语言鲁棒性差距：两个平均 LQA 准确率均约 80.4% 的模型，其跨语言性能波动分别达 7.8 和 20.3 个百分点；在固定英语 QA 下，全部 25 个模型使用非英语视觉时得分均下降。

Insight: 创新点在于构建语言平行的 InfoVis 视觉与 QA 基准，并通过解耦数值-结构信息与文本、同步翻译标签来保证跨语言可比性；同时引入 LQA/XQA 两种评测设置，揭示聚合指标掩盖的跨语言敏感性，强调需要跨语言鲁棒性指标。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in vision-language tasks; yet, whether they maintain consistent understanding of information-rich visuals (InfoVis) when the surface language changes remains unclear. To quantify this capability, we introduce MInfoVisQA, a parallel benchmark with 8,960 QA configurations derived from 128 seed instances across 24 languages. We construct seed-matched, language-parallel visuals and QA pairs by disentangling numerical-structural data from text and programmatically synchronizing translated labels. We evaluate 25 MLLMs under two settings: Language-Aligned QA (LQA, matching visual-text languages) and Cross-Language QA (XQA, fixed QA language with varying visual languages). The results show that aggregate accuracy masks substantial robustness gaps: two models with comparable mean LQA accuracy (80.4%) exhibit performance spreads of 7.8 and 20.3 percentage points across languages. Moreover, all 25 models score lower with non-English visuals under fixed English QA, underscoring the need for cross-language sensitivity metrics.

[2] Limite 1B - Violetto: Technical Report cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Mario Prignano, Gabriele Cirillo, Alessio Morosini, Luca Cerovaz, Alessandro Bartolocci

TL;DR: 论文提出 Limite 1B - Violetto，一个 10 亿参数、面向高吞吐数学推理的专用模型。它从零开始在 2370 亿 token 的数学中心语料上训练，支持最长 131k 上下文，并通过知识蒸馏、定向监督微调和 RLVR 课程学习进行后训练。模型定位为单轮数学求解器和大型系统中的快速并行组件，而非通用助手。

Details

Motivation: 出发点是在控制训练与推理成本的同时，提升数学推理能力与吞吐效率，并探索小参数模型在数学场景中替代或补充大模型的可能性。

Result: 在 AIME 2026 上达到 94.01%，在 BeyondAIME 上达到 74.25%，在其评测套件中可与参数量大得多的模型相比。

Insight: 创新点在于数学专用语料从零训练、架构与训练配方协同设计、蒸馏+SFT+RLVR 的后训练课程，以及明确面向高吞吐单轮数学求解的轻指令微调定位，并开源模型、checkpoint 和优化推理实现。

Abstract: We present Limite 1B - Violetto, a one-billion-parameter model designed for high-throughput mathematical reasoning. Limite is trained from scratch on 237 billion tokens from a carefully curated, mathematics-centric corpus and supports context lengths of up to 131k tokens. Its architecture and training recipe were developed together to maximize reasoning capability while keeping training and inference costs low. Post-training combines knowledge distillation, targeted supervised fine-tuning, and RLVR on a curated curriculum. Limite achieves 94.01% on AIME 2026 and 74.25% on BeyondAIME, placing it among models many times its size on our evaluation suite. The model is deliberately only lightly instruction-tuned: it is intended primarily as a single-turn mathematical solver and as a fast, parallel component of larger systems, rather than as a general-purpose assistant. We release the model, its base and value checkpoints, and an optimized serving implementation under the Apache 2.0 license.

[3] Token Commitment as a Control Surface for Steering Masked Diffusion Generation cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Ambroise Aigueperse, Hugo Buurmeijer, Carmen Amo ALonso, Marco Pavone

TL;DR: 论文将掩码扩散模型解码时的“token承诺”策略视为一种扩散特有的控制面，拆分为每一步提交哪些token以及提交多少token两个决策，并据此提出反事实目标感知承诺机制和校准自适应预算机制，用于在推理阶段引导生成结果朝向指定目标。方法在LLaDA系列的语言与视觉语言任务、多个目标、两种激活引导方法及无引导对照下验证，并在Dream-7B上复现。

Details

Motivation: 现有推理时引导方法多修改激活、logits或去噪轨迹，却把哪些预测被最终承诺、以多快速度承诺当作固定解码细节；论文希望利用这一被忽视的承诺策略来更可控地引导掩码扩散生成，并解决目标引导下质量—目标权衡的问题。

Result: 在LLaDA系列的多配置实验中，两种机制均带来经验上单调的目标质量权衡；自适应调度在平均去噪开销匹配时，相对无评分器的随机调度可恢复到有竞争力的工作点。方法还可用于NLI蕴涵（对话摘要）和CLIPScore（图像描述）等任务成功代理目标，并在Dream-7B上复现。

Insight: 创新点在于把token承诺策略明确建模为可解释的控制面，并分离“承诺哪些token”和“承诺多少token”；客观看，其价值在于不依赖单一可复用引导方向，只需对临时补全计算标量分数即可适用，为掩码扩散推理控制提供了新的通用接口。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models repeatedly predict provisional tokens before a sampler decides which predictions become permanent. Existing inference-time steering methods primarily modify activations, logits, or denoising trajectories, while treating this commitment policy as a fixed decoding detail. We instead study token commitment as a diffusion-specific control surface and separate it into two decisions: which tokens to commit and how many tokens to commit at each step. For the first, we introduce counterfactual target-aware commitment, which ranks unresolved positions by a mixture of model confidence and leave-one-token-out target contribution. For the second, we introduce calibrated adaptive budgets driven by target progress and confidence. Across language and vision-language settings in the LLaDA family, multiple targets, two activation-steering methods, and an unsteered control, both mechanisms yield empirically monotonic target-quality trade-offs in every configuration we evaluate. Adaptive schedules also recover competitive operating points relative to scorer-free stochastic schedules matched in average denoising cost. Because both mechanisms require only a scalar score evaluated on a provisional completion, they apply unchanged when the target is an automatic proxy for task success rather than an output attribute: NLI entailment for dialogue summarization and CLIPScore for captioning, neither of which corresponds to a single reusable steering direction. We reproduce the behavior of both mechanisms on a second masked-diffusion family, Dream-7B, for one control target and one task-success target. These results characterize commitment–which provisional predictions become irreversible, and how rapidly–as an interpretable control surface for steering masked diffusion generation under the conditions we test.

[4] TUSI: A Citation-Grounded Graph-Based Benchmark cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Marzieh Karimi, Mehran Safayani, Alireza Basiri

TL;DR: 论文提出 TUSI-Bench，一个面向波斯语法律领域 RAG 系统评估的引用 grounded 基准。它基于包含 7,261 条法律条文的多层法律知识框架构建，并整合法典、法律评注、学说文献和最高法院统一裁决，同时构建法律知识图谱以支持结构化检索。基准包含开放式、需显式法律引用的 TUSI-QA（1,999 题）和源自伊朗律师考试的四选一 TUSI-Bar（约 500 题），并提出基于图的检索基线。

Details

Motivation: 现有波斯语法律 RAG 基准存在真实性、grounding 和法理推理覆盖不足的问题：或由法条合成、缺乏自然信息需求，或来自非正式法律问答且未显式链接权威法律文件，难以严格评估有依据的法律推理。

Result: 实验表明，引入法律知识图谱中的图关系可使检索召回率相较标准基线最高提升 8 个百分点；论文还提供了标准化的波斯语法律 grounded 推理评测资源。

Insight: 创新点在于构建多层法律知识框架与法律知识图谱，并设计同时覆盖开放式引用问答和律师考试选择题的互补评测任务；客观看，其价值主要是将结构化法律表示、权威引用约束和 RAG 检索评测结合到波斯语法律场景中。

Abstract: Evaluating retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in Persian law remains challenging because benchmarks lack realism, grounding, and coverage of legal reasoning. Existing resources are either synthetically generated from statutory texts, lacking natural information needs, or derived from informal legal question–answer sources without explicit linkage to authoritative legal documents, making them unsuitable for rigorous evaluation of grounded legal reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce TUSI-Bench, a structured Persian legal benchmark designed for the evaluation of RAG-based systems. TUSI-Bench is built upon a multi-layered legal knowledge framework containing 7,261 legal articles and integrating codified statutes, legal commentaries, doctrinal writings, and Supreme Court unification rulings. We also construct a legal knowledge graph linking articles and concepts for structure-aware retrieval. Based on this foundation, TUSI-Bench provides two complementary evaluation tasks: (i) TUSI-QA, an open-ended question answering benchmark containing 1,999 questions that requires grounded responses with explicit legal citations, and (ii) TUSI-Bar, a multiple-choice benchmark containing approximately 500 four-option questions derived from Iranian Bar examination questions. Furthermore, we propose a graph-based retrieval baseline that leverages relational structure within the legal knowledge graph. Experimental results demonstrate that incorporating graph-based relations improves retrieval recall by up to 8 percentage points compared to standard baselines, highlighting the importance of structured legal representations for RAG systems. The proposed benchmark provides a standardized resource for evaluating grounded legal reasoning in Persian and enables future research on structured legal intelligence.

[5] Latent Fact Composition under Next-Token Training cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Tianyi Zhang, Feiying Chen

TL;DR: 论文研究在下一词元训练下，Transformer能否完成中间实体未在两跳训练样本中出现的潜在两跳推理。作者构建了损失可精确计算的合成语料，发现标准Transformer、循环Transformer和DiscoLoop几乎失败，于是提出Baton系列循环Transformer，将中间实体作为词元嵌入传入第二个循环。仅训练预测答案时，Baton三个变体中有两个几乎完美；但训练预测每个词元时，准确率先升后降。

Details

Motivation: 现有受控两跳推理研究通常只训练模型预测答案，而语言模型实际按每个词元训练。论文关注逐词元训练是否能让模型学会组合潜在事实，尤其是中间实体不出现在两跳训练样本中的泛化场景。

Result: 在合成语料上，标准Transformer、循环Transformer和DiscoLoop几乎无法回答此类两跳问题；Baton在仅预测答案时两个变体接近完美。逐词元训练Baton时准确率先升后降，移除下一词元损失梯度中两个依赖特定训练集的项可提高峰值但无法消除下降，下降出现在第二跳且模型仍记得两个事实。

Insight: 创新点在于提出Baton循环Transformer，将中间实体以词元嵌入形式传给第二循环，并构建损失项可精确计算的合成语料来受控分析潜在事实组合。客观看，论文还将模型成败归因于三个设计条件并通过单条件实验验证，同时对下一词元损失梯度进行分解，揭示逐词元训练下两跳组合的退化机制。

Abstract: Controlled studies of latent two-hop reasoning train transformers to predict only the answer, whereas language models are trained to predict every token. We ask whether a model trained on every token learns to answer two-hop questions whose intermediate entity appears in no two-hop training example. We build a synthetic corpus with such questions. On this corpus every term of the loss can be computed exactly. A looped transformer, a standard transformer and DiscoLoop answer almost none of these questions. We therefore introduce the Baton family of looped transformers. They pass the intermediate entity to the second loop as a token embedding. When trained to predict only the answer, two of its three variants answer these questions almost perfectly. We then relate the success and failure of each model to three design conditions. Experiments that change one condition at a time agree with this hypothesis. When Baton is trained on every token, however, its accuracy on these questions rises and then declines. We further split the gradient of the next-token loss into three terms. Removing the two terms that depend on the particular training set raises the peak accuracy but does not remove the decline. The decline arises in the second hop while the model still recalls both facts.

[6] DiagTrace: An Interactive Benchmark for Clinical Decision Making cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Yufan Wang, Rui Yang, Yi Lin, Chunhua Weng, Yifan Peng

TL;DR: 论文提出 DiagTrace，一个基于 PubMed Central 病例报告构建的交互式临床决策基准，用于评估大语言模型能否在信息不完整时通过主动询问逐步获取证据，并重建真实诊断轨迹。基准包含 8 个专科的 510 个病例，其中 225 个用于评估 4 个 LLM 在临床病程对齐、交互质量和效率方面的表现。

Details

Motivation: 现有医学问答和诊断推理评测通常一次性提供全部患者信息，只评价最终诊断或理由，忽视了真实诊断中需要从有限信息中逐步获取证据的过程。论文旨在弥补这一评测缺口。

Result: 论文未报告具体数值，但在 225 个病例上评估 4 个 LLM 后发现，模型在证据覆盖度、与来源一致的顺序性以及交互轮次使用之间存在权衡。

Insight: 创新点在于构建来源可追溯的交互式诊断轨迹评测，将病例报告转化为初始表现、事件库和临床病程，并同时评价轨迹重建、交互质量与效率，而不仅看最终诊断正确性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on medical question answering and diagnostic reasoning benchmarks. However, existing evaluations typically provide all patient information upfront and assess only the final diagnosis or rationale. This setup ignores the need to acquire diagnostic evidence incrementally from incomplete information in real-world diagnosis. To bridge this gap, we introduce DiagTrace, a source-grounded benchmark for evaluating whether LLMs can reconstruct the documented diagnostic trajectory through interactive information seeking. Each benchmark instance is built from a PubMed Central case report and includes an initial presentation, an event bank, a clinical course, and diagnostic references. DiagTrace contains 510 cases across eight specialties, with 225 used to evaluate four LLMs on clinical-course alignment, interaction quality, and efficiency. Results reveal trade-offs in coverage, source-consistent ordering, and turn use.

[7] RLDE: A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Interpretable Iterative Document Editing cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Nikhil Parkar, M. Marimuthu, Riya Joshi, Karthikeyan N

TL;DR: 论文提出RLDE，一个面向长文档迭代修订的端到端强化学习框架，将文档编辑建模为无参考马尔可夫决策过程，在NOOP/INSERT/DELETE/REPLACE有限动作空间下，结合指针网络进行token级动态片段选择，并调用冻结的BART-base生成上下文感知建议，通过PPO优化可读性、困惑度和语法等多目标奖励。

Details

Motivation: 长文档质量、清晰度和流畅度提升通常依赖人工迭代修订，而现有自动系统多为黑盒生成或监督学习，难以解释且未捕捉修订的序列性；论文旨在实现可解释、无配对修订目标、低成本的自动化迭代文档编辑。

Result: 在BEA-2019数据集上所有指标均取得统计显著提升（p<0.01），Flesch阅读易读性从49.8升至59.4，困惑度从63.7降至39.8，并保留93%语义信息；相比GPT-4和LLaMA-2等CoT提示工程模型，RLDE以很低推理成本取得高性能，人工评价意义保留4.9/5、编辑质量4.4/5。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点包括无参考MDP建模、有限动作空间下的指针网络动态片段选择、冻结BART-base生成建议、PPO多目标奖励优化，以及基于SBERT的语义保留惩罚以缓解删除退化；客观看，其可解释迭代编辑流程与低计算成本是主要可借鉴之处。

Abstract: Improving the quality, clarity, and flow of long documents remains a challenging problem in NLP, as human editing typically occurs through iterative refinement rather than a single-pass rewrite. Most existing automated systems act as black-box generators or rely on supervised learning, limiting interpretability and failing to capture the sequential nature of revisions. In this paper, we propose RLDE, an automated end-to-end metric-driven RL framework that formulates document revision as a reference-free MDP problem (unsupervised with respect to paired document-revision targets). Working with a limited action space (NOOP, INSERT, DELETE, REPLACE), RLDE leverages Pointer Networks for dynamic span selection at the token level and sends generative actions to the frozen BART-base model to obtain context-aware suggestions. PPO agents learn to optimize a multi-objective reward function based on readability, perplexity, and grammar metrics with the help of a novel SBERT-based Semantic Preservation Penalty constraint to avoid deletion degeneracy. Evaluations on the BEA-2019 dataset showed statistically significant results (p < 0.01) across all metrics, where Flesch Reading Ease increased from 49.8 to 59.4, perplexity decreased from 63.7 to 39.8, while preserving 93% of semantic information. When compared to Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompt-engineered models like GPT-4 and LLaMA-2, RLDE shows high performance at a very low computational inference cost. Moreover, the quality of edits generated by RLDE has been verified via human evaluations, scoring 4.9/5 for meaning preservation and 4.4/5 for edit quality.

[8] What MoE Routing Controls: Causal Interventions on Expert Trajectories cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Mukund Athmaram, Manoj Rao, Liang Ma

TL;DR: 论文研究MoE语言模型中专家路由对模型行为的因果影响：通过在生成时给目标专家的router logit加固定偏置，将token强制引导到由概念或角色高频使用的专家路径上。作者在Qwen3-30B-A3B上区分“格式目标”和“概念目标”，发现格式路径会改变生成文本，而概念路径虽能使下一token概率偏向该概念（甚至跨语言构建路径时也成立），却对最终文本影响很小。论文进一步分析格式自强化、概念强度差异及中层集中等现象。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注MoE模型中token跨层被路由到一系列专家时，这些专家路径究竟控制了什么行为，以及强制token走特定专家路径是否会对模型输出产生因果影响。其出发点是区分路由对输出格式与内容概念的调控作用。

Result: 在Qwen3-30B-A3B上的干预实验表明：引导格式路径会改变模型写出的文本；引导概念路径会使下一token概率向目标概念偏移，但几乎不改变生成文本。结果还显示religion和money概念受引导最强，效应集中在中间层，且格式一旦启动会自我强化；摘要未提及标准benchmark或SOTA比较。

Insight: 创新点在于用固定router logit偏置对MoE专家轨迹做因果干预，并系统对比格式目标与概念目标、跨语言概念路径及部分生成阶段干预。客观看，其可借鉴之处是将“路由控制”拆分为格式自强化机制与概念概率偏移但难传导到文本的差异，并从层分布、概念类别和embedding/词频角度解释效应异质性。

Abstract: In mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models, a token is routed to a sequence of experts across layers. We investigate how forcing tokens onto chosen expert paths influences model behavior. We build a target path from the experts that a concept’s or role’s tokens most often use, and we add a fixed bias to each target expert’s router logit during generation to steer the token toward this expert path. We study two kinds of targets. Format targets concern the form of the output, and concept targets concern its topic. In Qwen3-30B-A3B, we show that steering a format path changes the text the model writes. We also show that steering a concept path shifts the model’s next-token probabilities toward the concept, even when the path is built in another language, yet this shift has little effect on the generated text. We investigate why formats reach the text but concepts do not by steering only part of the generation, and find evidence that a format, once started, reinforces itself. We show that the concepts religion and money steer most strongly, and that the effect is concentrated in the middle layers. We attempt to explain why the strength of the effect varies by concept through an analysis of embedding distinctiveness and word frequency.

[9] From Imperfective Paradox to Moorean Absurdity: Single-Chain Reasoning Lock-In and Multi-Candidate Verbalization Across Aspectual and Pragmatic Ambiguity cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

An-Sheng Jhang

TL;DR: 论文围绕LLM在体貌歧义与语用歧义下的蕴含和连贯判断，提出并检验单链推理过早锁定假设，在Qwen3-8B上开展4个预注册形式实验，共16,640个形式单元。实验比较Direct-CoT、NLI-CoT、Multi-Candidate Verbalization等提示与读出协议，并在中英文、Moorean句子和跨模型消融中观察判断差异。结论强调可观察的提示-读出协议效应，而内部过早锁定仍作为假设保留。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注LLM判断事件是否仅发生、是否达到终点、是否满足计划或多重语用标准时，可能受单链推理过早锁定影响。其出发点是区分提示设计与读出方式对蕴含/连贯判断的因果作用。

Abstract: Natural language entailment and coherence judgments often depend on whether an event merely occurred, reached a telic culmination, satisfied a plan, or was evaluated under multiple pragmatic standards. All code, stimuli, preregistered analysis plans, and verification tools are available at https://github.com/asj252/llm-judgment-studies.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have renewed interest in Page Stream Segmentation (PSS), splitting a multi-page file into its constituent documents. Yet published LLM methods still frame the task as boundary classification over one or a few neighboring pages, and commercial document-splitting APIs do not disclose their methods. We reimagine PSS for LLMs as a sequential process over the whole stream and propose RICoT (Recurrent Interleaved Chain-of-Thought), a prompting framework that works through the pages as a reader would: within a sliding window it reasons about each page and commits its boundary decision before moving on, and across windows it carries forward a compact record of what it has read, keeping per-call cost bounded. We also introduce FinancePSS-205, a benchmark of 205 real-world, de-identified financial page streams, to be released publicly. Abstract: The ability to generate multimodal reports integrating fluent narratives with faithful visualizations from tabular data, has become a cornerstone capability for modern vision-language models in practical domains such as finance, healthcare, and policy making. However, existing benchmarks are limited by single-modality focus, simplistic metrics, novelty evaluation, and visualization quality. We address these limitations with MM-ReportBench, a multimodal report evaluation benchmark covering 386 tasks across 185 real-world tables. Each task is paired with an expert-curated reference report featuring standardized chapter structures and verifiable key insight points, constructed through a hybrid pipeline of multimodal reports generation based on Monte Carlo Tree Search and rigorous human quality control. For robust evaluation, we design a dual-judge evaluation framework: Text Judge Model assesses textual quality along structural completeness, grounded insight verification with numerical fact-checking against source tables, and novelty analysis that distinguishes genuine analytical discovery from trivial paraphrasing; Visualization Judge Model evaluates generated visualizations across data fidelity, expressiveness, aesthetics, and technical correctness. Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across various sectors. In this paper, we introduce AfriNORM, a scenario-based, multiple-choice question dataset designed to measure and diagnose the normative reasoning limitations of LLMs when applied to African sociocultural contexts. [13] Understanding Cross-Modal Inconsistency through Representation Subspaces in Omni Models cs.CLPDF

Seonghyeon Cho, Chanjun Park

TL;DR: 论文研究统一语言骨干的Omni模型在音频、视觉、文本语义等价输入下产生跨模态答案不一致的问题。作者从答案位置表示中估计低维Answer Subspace，将答案相关成分与其余表示分离，发现预测不一致的信息主要位于Answer Subspace之外，并集中于可复现的二维表示。干预实验进一步表明，扰动Answer Subspace会显著改变预测，而扰动该不一致表示几乎无影响；仅对单一模态的Answer Subspace施加扰动可诱导跨模态分歧，对两个模态施加相同扰动则基本保持一致。

Details

Motivation: 现有表示层面分析未区分“能预测是否发生分歧的信息”与“扰动后会实际影响模型答案的表示”，因此需要厘清Omni模型跨模态不一致的来源及其与答案表示的关系。

Result: 在3个Omni模型和2个多模态推理任务上，预测跨模态不一致的信息主要位于Answer Subspace之外，并集中于一个可复现的二维表示，且不能由固定模态偏移解释。干预结果显示，扰动Answer Subspace会显著改变预测，而扰动分歧表示或同维随机子空间几乎不改变预测；仅扰动单一模态的Answer Subspace可诱导分歧，对两模态施加相同扰动则基本保持一致。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于区分表示的线性可解码性与行为有效性，并通过Answer Subspace分解揭示跨模态分歧可由模态特定答案表示失配诱导。其可借鉴之处是：预测分歧的表示不一定是因果/行为上有效的表示，分析模型不一致性时需结合匹配干预来验证表示的因果作用。

Abstract: Omni Models process audio, vision, and text within a unified language backbone, yet often produce different answers for semantically equivalent inputs across modalities. Existing representation-level analyses do not distinguish between information that predicts whether disagreement will occur and the representation whose perturbation affects the model’s answer. In this paper, we estimate a low-dimensional Answer Subspace from answer-position representations and use it to separate answer-related components from the remaining representation. Across three Omni Models and two multimodal reasoning tasks, information predictive of cross-modal inconsistency lies mainly outside the Answer Subspace, concentrating in a reproducible two-dimensional representation that is not explained by a fixed modality offset. Matched interventions reveal distinct behavioral roles: perturbing the Answer Subspace substantially changes the model’s predictions, whereas perturbing the disagreement representation, like a random subspace of the same dimensionality, leaves them almost unchanged. Our key finding is that cross-modal disagreement can be induced by misaligning modality-specific answer representations: perturbing the Answer Subspace for only one modality induces disagreement, whereas applying the same perturbation to both largely preserves agreement. Thus, the representation that predicts disagreement is distinct from the answer-level representation through which disagreement can be induced, showing that linear decodability does not imply behavioral efficacy.

Motivation: typed decision models以结构化接口返回动作和选项级概率分布，具有低推理成本和低延迟优势，但其在交通预定义备选问题中的实际有效性、鲁棒性和效率尚缺乏系统验证。论文旨在回答这类模型何时适用于交通决策，并强调输入设计和任务特性的影响。

Abstract: Typed decision models combine natural-language understanding with a structured interface that returns a selected action and an option-level probability distribution without generating lengthy textual responses. Their low inference cost and response latency make them attractive for transportation problems with predefined alternatives. This paper investigates their effectiveness, robustness, and efficiency in two representative transportation tasks: individual travel-mode choice prediction and sequential traffic signal control. We systematically evaluate TypeSafe Jev and GPT-6 Luna Decisions against conventional transportation methods, reinforcement learning algorithms, and four chat-based large language models. Experiments on the Swissmetro dataset show that typed decision models achieve lower prediction accuracy than traditional discrete choice models and the strongest chat-based baseline, while their performance varies substantially with input representation and question framing. Details

Motivation: 现有非英语 LLM 基准多为翻译、选择题或难以自动评分，缺少语言特定、开放词表、人工策展且可确定性评分的荷兰语评测。论文旨在构建更具挑战性且可复现的荷兰语词谜基准，并提供可推广到其他语言的构建方法。

Each puzzle gives three clues that describe different senses or uses of the same Dutch word. The clues are individually ambiguous, requiring the solver to combine evidence across multiple clues. We construct the benchmark from Dutch Wikipedia disambiguation pages, followed by automatic filtering and extensive manual curation. [16] TPHC: Improving Hyper-Connections with Tensor-Parallel Grouped Updates cs.CLPDF

Xinying Hu, You Wu, Haoyi Wu, Jianchao Tan, Kefeng Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出TPHC，将张量并行（TP）分片与Hyper-Connections（HC）的并行残差流结合：为每个TP分片路由单独的残差流混合，并在写回时保持各分片输出分离，使不同流可接收不同混合输出。该方法在不增加注意力或FFN子层参数与FLOPs的情况下增强残差流更新的表达力，并原生支持TP下的流分片。

Details

Motivation: 现有manifold-constrained HC（mHC）在流数超过四个后收益有限，主要原因是每个子层只产生单一输出并写入所有流，限制了各流差异化更新。论文旨在提升多残差流之间的独立性，使HC在更多流数下仍有效。

Result: 在8B模型、四流设置下，TPHC训练时间仅比mHC多9%；流数从4增至16时训练时间仅增19%。在1.1B模型、100B tokens训练下，相比匹配流数N∈{4,8,16}的mHC，验证损失降低0.7-1.0%，六个常识推理benchmark平均零样本准确率提升0.3-0.7个百分点。

Insight: 创新点在于将TP分片与HC流进行细粒度耦合：每个TP分片使用独立的残差流混合路由，写回阶段保留分片输出分离，从而实现更具表达力的流更新。其可借鉴之处是以路由和写回机制提升并行流独立性，同时避免增加注意力/FFN子层的参数和FLOPs，并保持TP原生支持。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) expand the residual stream of a Transformer into $N$ parallel streams, and manifold-constrained HC (mHC) stabilizes them at scale. However, mHC benefits little from more than four streams, partly because each sublayer writes a single output to all streams, limiting how differently they can be updated. In this work, we propose TPHC, which integrates tensor-parallel (TP) shards with HC streams to promote greater independence across streams. Specifically, we route a separate mixture of the residual streams to each TP shard and keep the individual shard outputs separate during write-back, so each stream can receive a different mixture of those outputs. This design makes the residual-stream updates more expressive without adding parameters or FLOPs to the attention or FFN sublayers. With native support for stream sharding under TP, TPHC requires only 9% more training time than mHC on an 8B model when both use four streams. Increasing the number of streams from four to sixteen raises its training time by only 19%. On a 1.1B model trained on 100B tokens, TPHC reduces validation loss by 0.7-1.0% and raises average zero-shot accuracy across six commonsense reasoning benchmarks by 0.3-0.7 percentage points relative to mHC at matched stream counts $N\in{4,8,16}$. Our code is available at https://github.com/wuyou2002/TPHC.

[17] Natural Language Programming: Rethinking Language, Memory, and Cognition in the Era of Generative AI cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Giovanni Adorni, Daniele Grosso

TL;DR: 论文提出自然语言编程（NLPg）作为传统NLP在生成式AI时代的理论与认识论扩展，将prompt视为外化目标、约束、验证标准和中间推理状态的动态认知制品，而不仅是技术交互手段。作者基于分布式认知、扩展心智、元认知和对话学习理论提出“对话工作记忆”，并将BTMA、SKGE、RMR等记忆架构映射到情景、语义和元认知层，以解释长程对话一致性。最后讨论了自然语言同时作为沟通媒介、可执行规范、认知脚手架和分布式记忆架构的后符号、后界面范式。

Details

Motivation: 传统NLP主要把语言当作分析、分类、翻译或生成的计算对象，而LLM使自然语言 increasingly 成为可执行的认知媒介。论文希望重新理解prompt在人类推理、问题求解和人机混合工作流调节中的作用，并弥补仅关注语言理解的传统NLP视角。

Result: 摘要未报告定量实验、benchmark或SOTA结果，论文主要是理论性、概念性和认识论层面的框架提出与现有记忆架构的映射分析。

Insight: 创新点在于将prompt提升为动态认知人工制品和对话式认知编程，提出“对话工作记忆”以解释prompt和对话状态作为临时外部记忆结构的作用，并系统映射情景、语义、元认知记忆层。客观看，其可借鉴之处是以后符号/后界面视角统一自然语言的沟通、规范、认知脚手架和分布式记忆功能。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence is transforming the role of natural language in computational systems. In classical Natural Language Processing (NLP), language was treated as an object of computation: something to analyse, classify, translate, or generate. Large Language Models (LLMs) challenge this perspective, enabling natural language to function increasingly as an executable cognitive medium through which humans structure reasoning, orchestrate problem solving, and regulate hybrid human/AI workflows. This paper introduces Natural Language Programming (NLPg) as a theoretical and epistemological extension of traditional NLP. Rather than focusing on language understanding alone, NLPg conceptualises prompts as dynamic cognitive artefacts that externalise goals, constraints, assumptions, validation criteria, and intermediate reasoning states. Prompting is thus not merely a technical interaction technique, but a form of dialogic cognitive programming operating within distributed human/AI memory architectures. Building on theories of distributed cognition, extended mind, metacognition, and dialogic learning, the paper proposes the notion of Dialogic Working Memory, describing how prompts and conversational states function as temporary external memory structures supporting reasoning continuity and reflective regulation. We further map existing memory architectures (BTMA, SKGE, RMR) onto this framework, illustrating how episodic, semantic, and metacognitive layers support long-horizon conversational coherence. Finally, implications for computational linguistics and AI/mediated knowledge systems are discussed, suggesting a post symbolic and post interface paradigm in which natural language operates simultaneously as communication medium, executable specification, cognitive scaffold, and distributed memory architecture.

[18] EgoLink: Egocentric Language-Vision Interactive Network Knowledge Challenge Summary cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Renhe Sun, Yueying Feng, Bohan Yu, Tong Niu, Yunqi Liu

TL;DR: 本文总结了ACM Multimedia 2026 EgoLink挑战赛，该挑战赛面向第一人称多模态智能，评估模型能否结合第一人称感知、社会推理和交互行动。比赛包含两条赛道：Track 1聚焦第一人称视频中的社会推理选择题问答，Track 2聚焦通过对话、工具使用和闭环执行完成服务任务的交互智能体。论文还介绍了Track 1的多阶段数据构建流程、任务设计、评测指标、排行榜和代表性方案。

Details

Motivation: 现有基准多关注可见物体和活动，EgoLink旨在评估模型理解情绪与社会线索、解析视觉 grounded 指代，并将用户请求转化为可验证工具中介动作的能力，检验感知、推理和决策在离线问答与状态改变交互中是否保持耦合。

Result: Track 1最佳系统达到0.9263准确率；Track 2最佳系统达到0.844联合成功率和0.916微平均准确率。评测结合客观答案评分与基于过程和结果的评估，可同时分析最终正确性和执行质量。

Insight: 创新点在于构建连接第一人称社会推理与交互式工具执行的统一多模态挑战，并强调过程验证和证据 grounded 推理。参赛方案体现的共同经验包括候选验证、选项鲁棒推理、场景感知规划、工具规范化以及回答或行动前的状态感知纠错。

Abstract: The EgoLink Challenge at ACM Multimedia 2026 benchmarks egocentric multimodal intelligence linking first-person perception, social reasoning, and interactive action. Unlike benchmarks centered on visible objects and activities, EgoLink evaluates whether models can interpret emotional and social cues, resolve visually grounded references, and convert user requests into verified tool-mediated actions. Its two tracks cover social reasoning in egocentric video and interactive agents that complete service tasks through dialogue, tool use, and closed-loop execution. Together, they test whether perception, reasoning, and decision-making remain coupled under both offline question answering and state-changing interaction. We highlight a multi-stage Track 1 data pipeline for multiple-choice question (MCQ) generation, chain-of-thought generation, automatic quality grading, and video-contribution filtering. We summarize the task design, evaluation metrics, rules, leaderboards, and representative solutions. The benchmark combines objective answer scoring with process- and result-based evaluation, enabling analysis of both final correctness and execution quality. The best Track 1 system achieved 0.9263 accuracy, while the best Track 2 system achieved 0.844 joint success and 0.916 micro accuracy. Participant reports reveal a shared principle: strong systems make intermediate evidence explicit through candidate verification, option-robust reasoning, scenario-aware planning, tool canonicalization, and state-aware correction before answering or acting. These findings highlight the importance of process verification and evidence-grounded reasoning for reliable egocentric multimodal intelligence.

Haiyu Yang, Heidi Lesscher, Enhong Liu, Miel Hostens

TL;DR: 论文发布了一个用于群养奶牛犊游戏行为研究的多农场视频与标注数据集，包含荷兰两个农场的四个摄像头约一周记录，共1,173个视频文件、651小时视频；其中8个观察日由训练有素的观察者用18项行为谱连续编码104小时，90小时覆盖栏内所有犊牛，并提供110万解码帧、每帧时间戳OCR、逐犊牛分割轨迹和帧级游戏标签。

Details

Motivation: 游戏行为可作为奶牛犊正向福利指标，但公开可用的群养犊牛视频及个体行为标注十分稀缺，亟需数据支持犊牛行为福利研究和自动行为识别系统开发评估。

Result: 论文主要贡献是发布数据集而非提出新模型；数据规模为651小时视频、104小时连续行为标注、90小时全犊牛编码及110万带分割轨迹和帧级游戏标签的解码帧，未提及benchmark或SOTA结果。

Abstract: Play is used as an indicator of positive welfare in dairy calves, but openly available video of group-housed calves with individual behaviour annotations is scarce. We describe video from four camera deployments of about one week each in group pens of dairy calves on two farms in the Netherlands: a research and teaching dairy farm (2024 and 2025) and a commercial dairy farm (2024). The recordings comprise 1,173 video files spanning 651 h. For eight observation days, trained observers coded calf behaviour continuously in Noldus The Observer XT with an 18-item ethogram of play and other behaviours, covering 104 h of video, 90 h of which have every calf in the pen coded. The release also contains 1.1 million decoded frames from seven annotated sessions, a per-frame timestamp read by optical character recognition from the camera clock burned into each frame, per-calf segmentation tracks and per-calf frame-level play labels. The data support studies of calf behaviour and welfare and the development and evaluation of automated behaviour recognition.

[20] Every Camera Grows Up: Camera-Height Regression in Interactive Video World Models cs.CVPDF

Sheng Lu, Guangpin Tao, Tong Lu, Ying Tai

TL;DR: 论文研究交互式视频世界模型作为具身智能体模拟器时的一个系统性缺陷：起始图像决定了相机高度（如地面机器人0.15 m、俯拍摄像头2.2 m），但随着动作轨迹推进、生成内容取代起始视角，相机高度会不可控地漂移到人类眼高，作者称之为“相机高度回归”。为此作者构建了EyeHeight基准（6个渲染高度0.15–2.2 m加4个真实标定机位，配无尺度多视角高度估计），系统评测了6个开源权重模型，并分析了成因、提出了缓解手段。

Details

Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型要忠实充当具身智能体的模拟器，就必须在相机移动过程中保持起始视角所固定的相机高度；然而现有模型在生成新视角时会悄悄丢失这一物理约束，导致模拟器在几何上失真，而这一问题此前未被系统研究和量化。

Result: 6个模型在180°转身行走的rollout中全部丢失初始高度：从0.15 m初始化出发，仅0–20%的片段终点落在起始高度的1.3倍以内，推断视点收敛到1.1–1.5 m的人类高度，而真值渲染误差仅百分之几；回归随旋转角度增大，且在起始帧仍在上下文时即已出现，SANA-WM中360°回转可大幅逆转。无引导采样加高度均衡微调可提升高度保持能力，但损失5个MUSIQ点。

Insight: 论文的创新在于将一个隐蔽但影响下游导航策略的失效模式显式定义并量化（camera-height regression），提出配套基准与无尺度高度估计评测方法，并把成因追溯到以人类视角为中心的视频先验（地面视角数据稀少），还揭示了无分类器文本引导在前半去噪阶段反而加剧漂移这一反直觉发现，为世界模型的几何一致性改进提供了可操作方向。

Abstract: Interactive video world models serve as simulators for embodied agents: from a starting image and an action trajectory, they generate the views the agent would see. The starting image fixes how high the camera is, for example 0.15 m for a floor robot or 2.2 m for an overhead camera, and a faithful simulator must keep this height as the camera moves. We show that six recent open-weight models fail to do so: as motion replaces the starting view with generated content, they move the camera toward human eye level, a failure we call camera-height regression. Our benchmark, EyeHeight, pairs six rendered elevations (0.15 m to 2.2 m) and four calibrated real-world rig heights with scale-free multi-view height estimation. All six models lose their initial height during a $180^\circ$ turn-and-walk rollout: initialized at 0.15 m, only 0-20% of clips end within $1.3\times$ of the starting height as inferred viewpoints converge to human heights of 1.1 m to 1.5 m, whereas ground-truth renders remain within a few percent. In Matrix-Game models, homography-tracked floor motion stays consistent with constant elevation during rotation even as newly outpainted room geometry yields a higher reconstructed viewpoint. Regression grows with rotation angle, begins while the start frame remains in context, and largely reverses in SANA-WM when a $360^\circ$ turn restores the initial view. We trace this pull to human-centric video priors in which floor-level views are nearly absent: in SANA-WM, textual height cues and dense floor-disparity maps do not prevent it, whereas classifier-free text guidance during the first half of denoising strengthens it. Combining unguided sampling with height-balanced fine-tuning recovers more retention but costs 5 MUSIQ points, a loss fixed during those same early steps. Viewpoint drift alone shifts the paths predicted by visual navigation policies.

[21] Sketch, Don’t Paint: Allocating Test-Time Compute in Generative Visual Reasoning cs.CVPDF

Sheng Lu, Guangpin Tao, Tong Lu, Ying Tai

TL;DR: 论文研究生成式视觉推理中的测试时计算分配问题，即多模态模型何时应渲染视觉思考、以及每个视觉思考需要多少扩散去噪步数。作者发现视觉思考可分为因果性、装饰性和有害性三类：因果性思考在很少去噪步数下已可读，8步草图几乎达到50步完整渲染的准确率；装饰性思考可省略，有害性思考会降低准确率。基于此，论文提出自适应选择直接回答、纯文本思考或视觉思考，并在适合画图时采用8步草图多次采样投票的sketch-and-vote方法。

Details

Motivation: 现有交错式多模态推理常通过扩散生成像素或视频作为视觉思考，但每次都执行完整去噪流程，推理成本很高。论文旨在回答两个问题：何时值得渲染视觉思考，以及每个视觉思考需要多少去噪步数，从而避免“总是画图”带来的次优计算分配。

Result: 在3个交错推理模型的14组模型-基准对上，直接回答在9组中胜出；用每组10个标注样例选择直接、纯文本或视觉层级后，平均准确率从60.7%提升到65.1%。在画图有效的任务上，sketch-and-vote用更少去噪步数超过完整渲染，在VSP空间规划上提升8.6个点，在两个迷宫任务上分别提升9.6和22.1个点。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于将视觉推理的测试时计算显式拆分为“是否渲染视觉思考”和“渲染多少去噪步数”两个轴，并通过控制变量揭示因果性、装饰性和有害性三种视觉思考机制。客观上看，可借鉴之处是早停式草图生成加上独立噪声多采样投票，以及按模型和任务自适应路由不同推理模态，而非默认使用完整视觉渲染。

Abstract: Multimodal models increasingly reason by synthesizing pixels, sketching maze paths within interleaved chains of thought or rolling out video solutions frame by frame. Each visual thought runs the full diffusion schedule inherited from photorealistic synthesis, so iterative denoising dominates inference cost. We study how to allocate test-time compute along two axes: when to render visual thoughts rather than thinking in text or answering directly, and how many denoising steps each thought requires. Controlled interventions that vary only how the thought is rendered uncover three regimes. Causal thoughts carry the plan and become legible early: an 8-step sketch nearly matches a 50-step render in accuracy, commits to the same plan as often as two independent full renders agree, and in video models mostly fails by stalling short of the goal, so the rollout abstains. Decorative thoughts can be omitted without loss, whereas harmful thoughts lower accuracy. Because the regime depends on both model and task, always drawing is suboptimal. Across 14 model-benchmark pairs of three interleaved reasoners, answering directly, without any thinking, wins 9 pairs, and choosing among direct, text-only, and visual tiers from ten labeled examples per pair raises mean accuracy from 60.7% (always drawing) to 65.1%. Where drawing pays, we propose sketch-and-vote, which samples 8-step sketches (a step count chosen without labels) from independent noises until one answer reaches three votes. With this one setting, sketch-and-vote beats full rendering with fewer denoising steps on an image reasoner and two video reasoners, by 8.6 points on VSP spatial planning and by 9.6 and 22.1 points on mazes.

[22] Can a World Model Predict a Deepfake Video? A Consistency Margin Prior for Deepfake Detection cs.CVPDF

Pradip Gupta, Shubham Mandloi, Ravi Kumar Satzoda, Sujeeth Bharadwaj, Yassine Benajiba

TL;DR: 论文研究预测式视频世界模型能否为Deepfake检测提供跨数据集更可泛化的信号。方法基于V-JEPA 2.1，冻结编码器并用rank-16 LoRA适配预测器，将预测输出与冻结编码器目标之间的残差作为“一致性边距先验”，再用轻量Latent Consistency Head分离残差幅值与方向进行分类；并通过标准与反向边距目标对比，验证边距方向对跨域迁移的影响。

Details

Motivation: 现有Deepfake检测器常依赖生成器特定伪影，在训练数据集上表现好，但跨数据集测试时性能明显下降。论文希望利用世界模型对自然视频的预测残差，获得比生成器伪影更可泛化的检测信号。

Result: 完整模型仅增加8.25M可训练参数，占总参数334M的2.47%；仅在FF++上训练时，在Celeb-DFv2上达到AUC 90.5，并在多个跨数据集基准上表现良好。对比实验显示反向边距设置平均具有更大的跨数据集泛化差距，支持边距方向影响跨域迁移的结论。

Insight: 创新点在于将视频预测模型的一致性残差作为Deepfake检测先验，并显式分离残差幅值与方向；同时以低秩LoRA适配冻结世界模型，实现参数高效且面向跨数据集泛化的检测。消融表明分离幅值/方向、减少数据集特定变化和使用低秩投影有助于跨域泛化。

Abstract: Deepfake detectors often perform well on the dataset they are trained on, but their performance drops when tested on videos from different datasets. In this paper, we study whether a predictive video model can provide a signal that generalizes better across datasets. Instead of relying only on generator-specific artifacts, as many existing detectors do, we use a model trained to predict natural video and measure the difference between what it predicts and what it observes in a video. Using V-JEPA 2.1, we keep the encoder frozen and adapt the predictor using rank-16 LoRA. We define the difference between the predictor output and the frozen encoder target as the prediction residual. We hypothesize that manipulated clips produce, on average, larger prediction residuals than authentic clips, and use this as our consistency-margin prior. We process these residuals using a lightweight Latent Consistency Head (LCH), which separates residual magnitude from direction for training and classification. To test whether the direction of the consistency margin affects cross-dataset generalization, we compare standard and reversed margin objectives while keeping the remaining training setup fixed. We match the two settings on FF++ validation performance before evaluating them on unseen data. The reversed-margin setting exhibits a larger cross dataset generalization gap on average, providing evidence that the direction of the consistency margin affects cross-dataset transfer. Our complete model adds only 8.25M trainable parameters, which is 2.47% of the total 334M parameters. Trained only on FF++, it achieves an AUC of 90.5 on Celeb-DFv2 and performs well across several other cross-dataset benchmarks. Our ablations show that separating residual magnitude from direction, reducing dataset-specific variation, and using a low-rank projection improve cross-dataset generalization in our experiments.

[23] AR-WAM: efficient streaming world-action modeling for autonomous driving cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.ROPDF

Baiting Luo, Shatadal Mishra, Ahmadreza Moradipari, Nejib Ammar

TL;DR: 论文提出AR-WAM，一种面向自动驾驶的高效流式世界-动作模型，将自回归时序建模与单次前向的未来条件相结合，避免重复编码历史和迭代视频生成。模型利用因果视频骨干增量更新视觉上下文并复用历史计算，同时用近期观测得到的未来特征条件化轨迹生成，并通过强化学习直接优化驾驶奖励。

Details

Motivation: 自动驾驶既需要预测交通演化，又需要快速响应新观测；现有视频基础模型虽具备预测能力，但重复历史编码和迭代视频生成导致计算成本高，难以用于实时驾驶。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v1上，仅使用单目前视相机时，监督训练后达到91.5 PDMS，强化学习后达到94.0 PDMS，优于对比的强端到端世界-动作模型；在连续驾驶动态设置中，监督AR-WAM跨决策保留视觉历史并优于强世界-动作基线。

Insight: 创新点在于因果视频骨干支持的增量视觉上下文更新与计算复用、单次前向获得未来特征以条件化轨迹生成从而避免迭代视频生成，以及用强化学习直接优化驾驶奖励。客观看，其可借鉴之处是面向流式自动驾驶将高效世界建模与动作生成耦合，在保持预测能力的同时降低推理成本并提升动态场景泛化。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires anticipating how traffic will evolve while responding quickly to new observations. Video foundation models offer the first ability, but repeated history encoding and iterative video generation make them costly to use for driving. We present AR-WAM, an efficient streaming world-action model that combines autoregressive temporal modeling with single-pass future conditioning. Because its video backbone is causal, AR-WAM updates its visual context incrementally, reusing past computation and conditioning future prediction on observed motion. We further hypothesize that future features computed from recent observations can improve generalization in dynamic traffic without iterative video generation. AR-WAM therefore obtains these features in one forward pass and uses them to condition trajectory generation, and reinforcement learning then improves the policy through direct optimization of driving rewards. With a single front camera, AR-WAM achieves 91.5 PDMS after supervised training and 94.0 after reinforcement learning on NAVSIM-v1, outperforming the compared strong end-to-end world-action models. In a dynamic setting of continuous driving, supervised AR-WAM keeps its visual history across decisions and outperforms strong world-action baselines.

[24] REEL: Brain-Guided Video Generation and Selection by Reranking Encoder Embeddings with Linear Decoding from fMRI data cs.CVPDF

Fabrizio Spera, Tommaso Boccato, Moein Khajehnejad, Nicola Toschi, Matteo Ferrante

TL;DR: 论文提出REEL框架，用线性语义解码器从fMRI预测视频生成器嵌入，并由预训练视频生成器产生多个候选视频，再通过神经编码流重排序选择与脑信号最匹配的视频。方法在CC2017和BMD数据集上验证，表明“提议-重排”策略能提升视频脑解码准确率。

Details

Motivation: 静态图像脑解码已取得显著进展，但视频fMRI解码受BOLD响应慢与视频刺激快速变化之间的时间失配制约。论文希望以更简单且互补的策略提升视频脑解码与选择性能。

Result: 在CC2017三名参与者上，重排序将平均Video 50-way准确率从0.2359提升到0.2434；在BMD十名参与者上从0.3653提升到0.3920，优于仅使用单个生成候选。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新点是贝叶斯启发的propose-and-rerank策略，将线性语义解码、预训练视频生成器与多神经编码流重排结合。客观来看，其可借鉴之处在于不依赖更复杂的生成管线，而是通过候选重排缓解fMRI与视频之间的时间失配，可作为现有复杂流程的补充方向。

Abstract: Visual brain decoding from functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data has made remarkable progress in recent years, driven by increasingly powerful artificial intelligence (AI) models and the growing availability of large-scale public datasets. However, despite the impressive advances achieved in static image decoding, the reconstruction of videos from fMRI signals remains challenging due to the temporal mismatch between rapidly changing video stimuli and the slow dynamics of the blood-oxygen-level-dependent (BOLD) response. We evaluate a Bayesian-inspired proposal-and-rerank strategy on CC2017 and the BOLD Moments Dataset (BMD), using a linear semantic decoder, a pretrained video generator, and three neural encoding streams with ridge readouts and learned pooling in two streams. We refer to this framework as REEL (Reranking Encoder Embeddings with Linear decoding). Compared with a single generated candidate, reranking increases mean Video 50-way accuracy from 0.2359 to 0.2434 across three CC2017 participants and from 0.3653 to 0.3920 across ten BMD participants. These findings suggest an alternative and conceptually simpler strategy for improving video brain decoding, highlighting the potential of our approach as a complementary direction to increasingly complex pipelines.

Abstract: Bird’s-Eye-View (BEV)-conditioned large language models (LLMs) enable language-interactive driving, but onboard deployment requires efficient perception and continuous planning alongside autoregressive language generation. [26] Does the Model Use What It Cites? Counterfactual Tests of Grounded Reasoning Chains in Small Video-Language Models cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Jugal Gajjar, Kamalasankari Subramaniakuppusamy

TL;DR: 论文提出用反事实干预检验小型视频-语言模型的有据推理链：遮挡模型自己引用的帧内区域，并与同帧同尺寸的非引用区域遮挡作配对比较，得到无需标注的“引用差距”（cite gap）。在2-3B模型上，CLEVRER和NExT-QA的cite gap分别为0.089和0.003，说明不同数据监督下引用区域对答案的因果影响差异很大。

Details

Motivation: 现有有据推理链通常只检查引用框是否可定位或与问题相关，却不能回答答案是否真正依赖所引用区域；论文要检验模型是否“用其所引”。

Result: 遮挡引用框使正确答案翻转的比例在CLEVRER上约为对照的2.2倍、NExT-QA上约1.1倍；遮挡引用实体在所有帧中的位置约为对照的1.9倍。引用框能通过grounding检查，但NExT-QA上答案对引用区域的依赖很弱。

Insight: 创新在于用配对反事实遮挡构造无标注因果检验，并揭示引用可验证且相关并不等于答案依赖它；评估有据推理应移除链中命名的实体或区域，而不仅是检查其指向位置。

Abstract: A grounded reasoning chain cites a frame span and a box for every step, so a reader can check where the model looked. Whether the answer depends on the cited region is a different question. We test it by intervention: the regions a model cited in its own chain are blacked out, and the share of correct answers that flip is compared with blacking out boxes of the same size elsewhere on the same frames. The paired difference, the cite gap, needs no annotation. We apply it to 2-3B video-language models trained on exact CLEVRER chains and on pseudo-labelled NExT-QA chains. With the same weights, the cite gap is 0.089 on CLEVRER and 0.003 on NExT-QA. Pooled over models, masking the cited boxes flips 2.2 times as many correct answers as the control on CLEVRER and 1.1 times as many on NExT-QA, also among the answers that depend on the video at all, while the boxes pass a grounding check on both. Masking the cited entity on every frame, instead of the cited box, flips 1.9 times as many answers as its control. The NExT-QA chains were supervised to cite two frames of entities that stay visible on most frames, so this outcome is what the supervision predicts; the point is that the usual check on grounded chains cannot see it. A citation can be verifiable and relevant without the answer depending on it, and a test of grounded reasoning should remove what a chain names, not only where it points.

Abstract: Worldwide neuroimaging data could strengthen diagnostic foundation models, but privacy rules often prevent centralizing brain MRI scans. Each site keeps exact class prototypes for local prediction and can share privacy-protected class summaries: norm-bounded sums and counts perturbed once with calibrated noise. Zhixu Du, Kuo Yang, Jingwei Sun, Hai “Helen” Li, Yiran Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出BATMoS，一种面向视频扩散模型推理的预算感知时序策略混合方法，通过在不同扩散时刻选择性地复用缓存引导而非每次都执行完整无分类器引导，以减少去噪器评估。方法用基于幅值的时间规则分配引导复用，并由单个整数控制计算预算，无需额外路由器训练或逐预算阈值搜索。

Details

Motivation: 视频扩散模型的推理预算效果取决于去噪轨迹上的计算分配；同等预算下不同配置的VBench总分可相差4.80，说明需要显式设计分配。关键观察是引导评估在不同扩散时刻价值不等，选择性复用引导有望在保持生成质量的同时减少计算。

Result: 在CogVideoX-2B上每配置4720个视频、覆盖VBench全部16个维度评估，BATMoS在8种分配策略中总分最高；相比完整CFG减少20%分支评估，总分仅低0.10。结合特征复用可将有效分支评估从80降至62，总分下降0.11。

Insight: 创新点在于按时间路由新鲜CFG与缓存引导，用低幅值块复用引导、其余保留新鲜引导的简单时序规则，并以单个整数控制预算，避免路由器训练和逐预算阈值搜索；客观看，其价值在于把时序引导分配做成轻量、可组合且实用的推理加速机制。

Abstract: The effectiveness of a video diffusion model’s inference budget depends on where computation is spent along the denoising trajectory. Across eight equal-budget inference configurations, VBench total scores span 4.80 points, highlighting the importance of explicit allocation design. Our key insight is that guidance evaluations are not equally valuable across diffusion time: retaining the sampling updates while selectively reusing guidance can preserve generation quality with fewer denoiser evaluations. We introduce BATMoS (Budget Aware Temporal Mixture of Strategies), which routes between fresh classifier-free guidance and cached guidance using a single pretrained denoiser. A magnitude-based temporal rule assigns guidance reuse to low-magnitude blocks and reserves fresh guidance for the remaining blocks, with a single integer controlling the computation budget. The resulting schedule requires no additional router training or per-budget threshold search. Evaluated on CogVideoX-2B with 4,720 videos per configuration across all sixteen official VBench dimensions, BATMoS achieves the highest total score among the eight allocation policies and comes within 0.10 points of full classifier-free guidance using 20% fewer branch evaluations. BATMoS also composes with feature reuse, reducing computation from 80 to 62 effective branch evaluations with a 0.11-point decrease in total score. These findings make temporal guidance allocation a practical mechanism for extracting more generation quality from a fixed inference budget.

[32] When AUC Meets Generative Reconstruction: A Unified Principle for Balanced Visual Representation Learning cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Boyu Han, Qianqian Xu, Shilong Bao, Zhiyong Yang, Qingming Huang

TL;DR: 论文提出AUCGR与pAUCGR统一表示学习范式，将生成重建重新表述为生成输出流形上的判别排序问题，通过AUC/pAUC约束匹配重建输出得分高于不匹配输出，从而在保持感知重建监督的同时嵌入判别信息。该方法即插即用、无需修改现有生成重建框架，并给出理论分析与泛化保证，实验验证其可同时提升判别准确性和细粒度感知表示。

Details

Motivation: 面向MLLM视觉编码器，现有对比学习与生成重建存在类间判别和类内变异/感知保真之间的权衡，简单复合损失会因共享潜在空间中的梯度干扰导致优化冲突和不稳定。

Result: 摘要仅给出定性结论：广泛实验验证该方法同时提升了判别准确性和细粒度感知表示；未提及具体benchmark、定量数值或SOTA对比。

Insight: 创新点在于把生成重建转化为生成输出流形内的判别排序问题，用AUC/pAUC排序匹配与不匹配重建输出，从目标层面缓解复合损失的竞争与不稳定，并保持即插即用；pAUCGR进一步聚焦可靠特征子空间中的不匹配比较以缓解监督偏差，且提供与AUC/pAUC优化的理论联系和泛化保证。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) require visual encoders that extract fine-grained visual features while constructing semantically disentangled and discriminative representations. Conventional methodologies encounter a fundamental trade-off: contrastive learning enhances inter-class discrimination by suppressing intra-class variability, whereas generative reconstruction emphasizes perceptual fidelity but lacks explicit comparative supervision. The naive aggregation of these objectives via composite loss functions often induces optimization conflicts and instability due to gradient interference within the shared latent space. To resolve this, we introduce a unified representation-learning paradigm that reformulates generative reconstruction as a discriminative ranking problem within the generative output manifold. Our approach maintains perceptual reconstruction as a supervisory signal and integrates a ranking-based discriminative constraint by systematically ordering matched and mismatched reconstruction outputs. This formulation embeds discriminative information seamlessly into the generative process, alleviating the risk of loss competition. Specifically, we propose AUC Generative Reconstruction (AUCGR), which optimizes the area under the ROC curve (AUC) by ensuring higher scores for matched pairs than for mismatched pairs, and Partial AUC Generative Reconstruction (pAUCGR), which addresses supervision bias by focusing on mismatched comparisons within reliable feature subspaces. The proposed objectives are designed to be plug-and-play, requiring no architectural modifications to existing generative reconstruction frameworks. Theoretical analysis establishes rigorous connections to AUC/pAUC optimization and provides generalization guarantees. Extensive experiments validate that our method advances both discriminative accuracy and fine-grained perceptual representation.

[33] Diagnosing and Correcting Skin-Tone Color Casts in Ultra-Lightweight Photo Retouching Networks cs.CVPDF

Choulhyouc Lee

TL;DR: 论文诊断并纠正超轻量照片修饰网络在合成自监督训练下产生的肤色偏绿问题：模型会低估退化去除的皮肤色度，甚至在干净输入上引入色相偏移；作者提出零额外参数的逐像素、皮肤掩码、非对称绿色过量损失，在不改变域内PSNR/SSIM的情况下显著降低肤色偏色，并可导出35 KB TFLite模型。

Details

Motivation: 合成自监督（退化高质量图像再恢复）是无需配对数据的常用训练配方，但全帧PSNR/SSIM可能掩盖局部肤色偏色；论文关注超轻量修饰网络为何遗留或引入肤色偏绿并如何纠正。

Result: 在代表6825参数直方图条件网络上，偏色肖像从52/70降至1/70；FFHQ上偏色比例68.1%→2.2%，CelebA-HQ 64.4%→0.8%；FiveK域内PSNR/SSIM基本不变（+0.13 dB,+0.004），但全帧CIEDE2000略升（8.77→9.69），近中性皮肤有品红过校正。

Insight: 创新点在于揭示全帧指标和全局均值损失会忽略或恶化局部肤色偏色，并提出沿绿-品红符号轴、仅作用于皮肤区域的非对称逐像素损失；客观看，零参数、跨三种超轻架构有效且可部署是主要价值，但并非提升整体颜色保真度。

Abstract: Color casts are expected to come from the scene or camera, and a retouching model removes them. We report a case where the model leaves one: an ultra-lightweight retouching network renders human skin greener than the original because it under-restores the skin chroma that a degradation removes and, even on clean inputs, adds a small hue shift of its own. A common paired-data-free recipe, synthetic self-supervision (degrade high-quality images, learn to restore them), silently produces this cast while whole-frame PSNR/SSIM stay essentially unchanged. In a representative 6,825-parameter histogram-conditioned network, the cast appears in 52 of 70 portraits under a ground-truth-anchored, white-balance-free protocol. Both a histogram-conditioned and a CNN-conditioned network develop the cast under synthetic self-supervision, yet both are near-neutral in-domain when trained on MIT-Adobe FiveK expert pairs. Image-level objectives miss it: a global-mean green loss worsens the cast (52/70 to 57/70). With zero added parameters, our per-pixel, skin-masked, asymmetric green-excess loss reduces the cast to 1/70 across stratified skin tones (68.1% to 2.2% over the 70,000-image FFHQ corpus; 64.4% to 0.8% on held-out CelebA-HQ photos). In-domain FiveK PSNR/SSIM are unchanged (+0.13 dB, +0.004), at the cost of a small whole-frame CIEDE2000 increase (8.77 to 9.69) and a magenta over-correction where skin is nearly neutral (skin Delta a* -1.5 to +4.4 and CIEDE2000 5.6 to 6.4 on clean inputs): the loss targets the signed green-magenta axis, not total skin-color fidelity. The same loss suppresses the cast across three ultra-light architectures. A whole-frame green metric correlates with skin-region CIEDE2000 at the method level (Spearman rho = 0.89). The final network exports to a 35 KB TFLite model.

[34] RetiDual-Select: Reliability-Aware Specialist-Generalist Routing for Retinal OCT under Image-Quality Shift cs.CVPDF

Zihao Han, Philippe De Wilde

TL;DR: 论文提出 RetiDual-Select，将视网膜专科模型 RETFound 与医学视觉语言模型 MedSigLIP 的图像编码器结合，并把候选融合、路由安全与预测复核拆开：以校准 PoE 为静态参考，小门控提出候选，验证集冻结的 harm guard 决定是否替换，独立评分用于复核。方法在干净数据上保留静态 PoE 的高精度，仅在图像质量造成可靠性机会时启用自适应路由，并在合成质量扰动下验证其安全收益与边界。

Details

Motivation: 自适应融合能修复静态集成错误，但也可能推翻正确决策；论文关注视网膜 OCT 在图像质量漂移下如何安全地自适应路由，避免有害替换并在不可靠时转人工复核。

Result: 静态 PoE 在三个数据集上取得最高或并列最高干净准确率；在2%可接受错误目标下，Kermany-4接受94.3%图像且选择性准确率99.89%，OCT-C8接受92.4%且98.65%。15种预设合成条件下自适应增益主要集中在严重对比度降低，路由机会约扩大9倍，百分位重缩放基本消除该效应；冻结 guard 将 OCT-C8 最差条件损失从1.39降至0.36个百分点。

Insight: 宣称创新在于可靠性感知的专科-通用路由框架，将候选融合、安全替换和复核机制解耦，并用验证集冻结护栏控制自适应风险。客观看，关键贡献是以校准 PoE 为安全基线、仅在门控跟随更可靠分支且存在质量导致的机会时才自适应，从而避免自适应融合推翻正确决策；但结论受合成扰动限制，未证明对自然采集或扫描仪漂移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Adaptive fusion can repair a static ensemble’s errors, but can also overturn correct decisions. RetiDual-Select combines retinal specialist RETFound with the image encoder of the medical vision-language model MedSigLIP, separating candidate fusion, routing safety and predictive review. Calibrated product-of-experts (PoE) fusion is the static reference, a small gate proposes an alternative for each image, a validation-frozen harm guard decides whether that candidate may replace the reference, and a separate score prioritizes review. Static PoE reached the highest or joint-highest clean accuracy on all three datasets. At a clean 2% accepted-error target the review policy accepted 94.3% of Kermany-4 images at 99.89% selective accuracy, and 92.4% of OCT-C8 at 98.65%. Both branches were correct on 95.0% and 91.8% of clean images; only 4.4% and 6.2% had exactly one correct branch. Across 15 prespecified synthetic conditions, adaptive gains were concentrated in severe contrast reduction, where this routing opportunity widened roughly ninefold; percentile rescaling largely removed the effect, linking it to compressed dynamic range. In post-hoc validation-only analyses, gain was higher when the gate followed the more reliable branch, and a frozen guard cut the worst-condition OCT-C8 loss from 1.39 to 0.36 percentage points. Adaptive routing was useful when image quality created a reliability opportunity and the gate followed it; otherwise the system retained Static PoE or deferred for review. The findings are bounded by the synthetic perturbations evaluated and do not establish robustness to natural acquisition or scanner shift.

[35] Text-Guided Modality Correction for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection cs.CVPDF

Yu Quan, Dong Zhang, Ping Gong, Peng Huang, JinHui Tang

TL;DR: 论文提出文本引导模态校正网络 TMCNet，用于开放词汇目标检测。该方法将文本语义引入区域特征聚合、跨模态对齐和多尺度解码，以增强区域与类别语义的对应关系，并融合不同层级的互补特征。在 COCO-OVD 和 LVIS-OVD 上取得较强性能，且可扩展到开放词汇分割任务。

Details

Motivation: 现有开放词汇目标检测方法对目标区域的文本指导不足，跨模态对齐策略有限，且未充分利用多层级互补特征，从而限制了对未见类别和不同尺度目标的检测能力。

Result: 论文在 COCO-OVD 和 LVIS-OVD 两个公开数据集上验证了 TMCNet 的较强开放词汇检测性能，并表明仅使用简单文本查询作为引导即可有效；扩展到开放词汇分割任务也能带来性能提升。摘要未给出具体数值或明确 SOTA 结论。

Insight: 创新点在于将文本引导贯穿区域特征提取、跨模态对齐和多尺度解码全过程：用文本语义增强查询以引导区域聚合，通过视觉-文本交互强化区域与类别语义对应，并用渐进式文本校正解码器融合深层语义与浅层空间细节；同时展示了跨任务的扩展性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection (OVD) is essential for detecting objects beyond the categories seen during training. Establishing vision-language associations is crucial for recognizing unseen categories, while integrating complementary visual features across levels supports detection at different scales. However, existing methods often provide insufficient textual guidance for object regions, rely on limited cross-modal alignment strategies, and underuse complementary features across levels, limiting their ability to detect unseen categories and objects of varying scales. To address these limitations, we propose a Text-Guided Modality Correction Network (TMCNet) that incorporates textual guidance into region feature extraction, cross-modal alignment, and multi-scale decoding to strengthen region-category semantic correspondence and promote complementary feature fusion across levels. First, a text-guided modality encoder incorporates textual semantics into queries to guide regional feature aggregation, reducing semantic ambiguity in object representations. Second, a cross-modal alignment module strengthens the correspondence between region representations and category semantics through interactions between visual and textual features. Finally, a progressive text correction decoder combines a top-down pathway with lateral connections to progressively fuse text-guided deep semantic features with shallow visual features at the corresponding scales, preserving both semantic information and spatial detail for objects of different sizes. Experiments on two public datasets, COCO-OVD and LVIS-OVD, demonstrate that TMCNet achieves strong performance in open-vocabulary object detection using simple text queries as guidance. Extending TMCNet to open-vocabulary segmentation also yields performance gains, demonstrating its cross-task applicability and extensibility.

[36] CEVA: Complementary Evidence Verification via Frame-Level Reorganization for Short-Video Fake News Detection cs.CVPDF

Wenshen Yang, Junfeng Hao, Jing Li, Decheng Liu, Yangkunfei

TL;DR: 论文提出CEVA，用于短视频假新闻检测，核心是在多模态融合前按帧的证据角色进行帧级重组：DBCR模块将显式对齐帧与候选互补上下文帧分开，并在重组证据上做跨模态融合与双向对比正则化。该方法在FakeSV和FakeTT上取得对比方法中最佳总体结果，且DBCR在固定帧预算和骨干网络下优于均匀、随机和相似度选择，并能提升两类结构不同的检测器。

Details

Motivation: 短视频新闻多模态交织且场景变化，现有检测器要么均匀池化采样帧，要么按文本-帧相似度选帧，忽略了与文本对齐的帧可能只是重复误导性声明，而较少主导的上下文帧反而可能提供反驳证据。

Result: 在FakeSV和FakeTT两个基准上，CEVA在对比方法中取得最佳总体结果；在固定帧预算和骨干网络下，DBCR优于均匀、随机及基于相似度的帧选择，并提升两个结构不同的检测器。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新点是首个在融合前显式按证据角色组织帧的短视频假新闻检测框架；客观看，其可借鉴之处在于将帧选择从同质池化/文本相似度转为“显式对齐证据+互补上下文证据”的分工重组，并用双向对比正则化强化跨模态融合，且该预融合证据组织模块具有可迁移性。

Abstract: Short-video platforms are now a common way for the public to follow unfolding events. Their rapid, visually persuasive format also makes fake news easier to spread. Unlike text-image posts, short-video news interleaves multiple modalities and changing scenes, making it difficult to identify which frames verify a claim. Existing detectors either pool sampled frames homogeneously or select them by text-frame similarity. Both overlook that aligned frames may repeat a misleading claim, whereas less dominant context may challenge it. We present CEVA (Complementary Evidence Verification Approach), to our knowledge the first short-video fake news detection framework to explicitly organize frames by their evidential roles before multimodal fusion. Its Dual-Branch Complementary Reorganization (DBCR) module separates explicit-alignment frames from candidate complementary-context frames, and cross-modal fusion with bidirectional contrastive regularization operates on the reorganized evidence. On FakeSV and FakeTT, CEVA achieves the best overall results among the compared methods. Under fixed frame budgets and backbones, DBCR outperforms uniform, random, and similarity-based selection and improves two structurally different detectors. Together, these results establish pre-fusion evidence organization as an effective and transferable component of short-video fake news detection.

[37] EgoHandWAM: Probing the Role of Hand Motion in Egocentric World Action Modeling cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Kailin Wang, Haoxiang Jie, Yaoyuan Yan, Zhiyou Heng, Zhaosong Li

TL;DR: 论文提出 EgoHandWAM，利用当前图像和近期手部状态预测第一人称场景中的三维手腕/指尖轨迹及未来视觉特征，并在 EgoDex 大规模数据上系统探究手部运动对世界动作建模的贡献。实验显示手部历史对视觉预测有可测增益，但自动预测轨迹帮助有限，且将人类示例作为辅助用于机器人任务未带来稳定提升。

Details

Motivation: 探究在第一人称视觉中，手部运动究竟能为“接下来会看到什么”提供多少预测信息，以及这种信息是否可辅助世界动作建模和机器人任务。

Result: 在 EgoDex 留出集上，仅动作模型将 2 秒手腕终点误差从 60.26 mm 降至 56.48 mm，但低估长距离伸手；视觉头利用记录手史相对复制当前特征降低 smooth L1 误差 1.32%，打乱手史后增益消失。四任务探针中加入记录的未来位移和有效性掩码使视觉特征误差降低 3.93%，仅掩码或预测轨迹帮助很小；RoboTwin 两个仿真任务中，用对齐人类示例替换辅助机器人样本在三个随机种子下成功率无一致提升。

Insight: 创新点在于对第一人称世界动作建模中手部运动作用进行系统探测：联合预测 3D 手部轨迹与未来视觉特征，并通过打乱历史、掩码/位移探针和机器人迁移实验区分记录手部信息与预测轨迹的实际价值。客观看，其贡献更偏向诊断性实证而非性能 SOTA，揭示了手部运动的有限但有条件有效的预测信号。

Abstract: How much does hand motion tell us about what a first-person camera will see next? EgoHandWAM forecasts three-dimensional wrist and fingertip trajectories and a future visual feature from the current image and recent hand states. We train on 751,065 EgoDex windows using a frozen DINOv2 encoder. On held-out development sessions, the action-only model reduces two-second wrist endpoint error from 60.26 to 56.48~mm relative to a stationary forecast. It still underestimates long reaches. Adding future-feature supervision gives nearly identical trajectory accuracy. With the recorded hand history, the visual head reduces smooth L1 error by 1.32% relative to copying the current feature. Shuffling histories across sessions erases this gain. In a separate four-task probe, adding recorded future displacements to observed context and future tracking-validity masks reduces visual-feature error by 3.93%. Masks alone give little improvement. A trajectory predicted from the same observations adds little. On two simulated RoboTwin tasks, substituting aligned human examples for an auxiliary batch of robot examples gives no consistent increase in task success across three training seeds.

[38] Unsupervised Multi-modal Medical Image Registration via Invertible Translation cs.CVPDF

Mengjie Guo, Jinming Duan

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无监督多模态医学图像配准方法 INNReg，先通过图像到图像翻译网络将多模态图像转换为单模态图像，再用配准网络基于翻译结果对齐原始多模态图像。为保证翻译前后几何一致性，引入基于动态深度可分离卷积局部注意力的可逆神经网络，并设计基于归一化互信息的 barrier 损失约束配准网络。

Details

Motivation: 多模态医学图像配准对图像引导治疗很重要，但不同模态间空间关系复杂且常未知，跨模态差异给配准带来显著挑战。论文希望借助可逆翻译降低模态差异，同时保持几何一致性以提升无监督配准精度。

Result: 在 MRI T1/T2 和 MRI/CT 两个公开多模态医学图像数据集上进行实验，摘要宣称 INNReg 展现出优越性能，但未给出具体定量指标或明确 SOTA 结论。

Insight: 创新点在于将多模态配准转化为翻译后的单模态配准，并用可逆神经网络约束翻译的几何一致性；同时引入动态深度可分离卷积局部注意力和基于归一化互信息的 barrier 损失。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是联合建模跨模态翻译与配准，并通过可逆性和信息度量约束减少翻译伪影对配准的干扰。

Abstract: In medical imaging, the alignment of multi-modal images plays a critical role in providing comprehensive information for image-guided therapies. Despite its importance, multi-modal image registration poses significant challenges due to the complex and often unknown spatial relationships between different image modalities. To address this, we introduce a novel unsupervised translation-based multi-modal registration method, termed Invertible Neural Network-based Registration (INNReg). INNReg consists of an image-to-image translation network that converts multi-modal images into mono-modal counterparts and a registration network that uses the translated mono-modal images to align the multi-modal images. Specifically, to ensure the preservation of geometric consistency after image translation, we introduce an Invertible Neural Network (INN) that leverages a dynamic depthwise convolution-based local attention mechanism. Additionally, we design a novel barrier loss function based on Normalized Mutual Information to impose constraints on the registration network, which enhances the registration accuracy. The superior performance of INNReg is demonstrated through experiments on two public multi-modal medical image datasets, including MRI T1/T2 and MRI/CT pairs. The code is available at https://github.com/MeggieGuo/INNReg

[39] HandRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement for Hand Pose Estimation cs.CVPDF

Zhekai Wang, Luqing Luo, Wenhao Yao, Junjie Lu, Huanan Zhu

TL;DR: 论文提出 HandRSI，一个面向第一视角 3D 手部姿态估计的递归自我改进框架。它结合编码智能体自动优化可执行推理流程与估计器的选择性蒸馏，在无真值帧上监督手部存在性和时序一致性，并迭代接受能提升十项基准指标综合表现且不显著回退的改进。方法从 ACE-Ego-Hand 出发，在 HOT3D 和 ARCTIC 上显著降低 MPJPE-p 和抖动，并提供多种速度配置。

Details

Motivation: 现有第一视角 3D 手部姿态估计器在图像边缘漏检、遮挡时定位错误和抖动明显；传统上依赖手写推理后处理与微调两步分别调优，但二者相互依赖、难以联合优化。

Result: 从 ACE-Ego-Hand 开始，五轮改进在 HOT3D 上降低 MPJPE-p 35%、ARCTIC 上降低 11%，抖动约减半；HOT3D 上 MPJPE-p 比同真值微调的 ACE-Ego-Hand 低 16%，统一协议下在两项基准十项指标上优于九个复跑已发表方法，并得到 20.7 至 41.1 FPS 的 Lite/Medium/Heavy 系统。

Insight: 创新点在于将编码智能体驱动的推理流程递归优化与估计器选择性蒸馏闭环结合，并以十项指标的复合提升且无显著回退作为改动接受准则，同时在新估计器上重新验证流程；客观看，其可借鉴之处是自动搜索推理后处理与模型自训练/蒸馏的协同迭代机制。

Abstract: Egocentric 3D hand pose estimators still miss hands at the image border, misplace occluded hands and jitter. Such failures are usually fixed by hand-written inference refinements and by fine-tuning, two interdependent steps that are tuned separately. We present HandRSI, a recursive self-improvement framework for a hand pose system that consists of a video estimator and its executable inference harness. In each round, coding agents refine the harness, and a change is accepted only if it improves a composite of all ten benchmark metrics on held-out data without significantly regressing any of them. The accepted harness then guides the estimator through selective distillation, which supervises hand presence and temporal consistency on frames without ground truth, while ground truth supervises pose. Since the updated estimator may change which refinements remain useful, the harness is re-validated on it. Starting from the released ACE-Ego-Hand model, five rounds reduce MPJPE-p by 35% on HOT3D and 11% on ARCTIC and roughly halve jitter. On HOT3D, HandRSI’s MPJPE-p is 16% lower than that of ACE-Ego-Hand fine-tuned on the same ground truth. Under a unified protocol, it outperforms nine re-run published methods on all ten metrics of both benchmarks. Hypervolume selection on the accuracy-speed front yields Lite, Medium and Heavy systems running at 20.7 to 41.1 FPS. This report gives the full method, every stage of every round and the complete protocol. Code, evaluator, harnesses and weights are public.

[40] CueClash: Frontier Vision-Language Models Judge 3-D Relations Close to the Picture Plane cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.ROPDF

Soham Dan

TL;DR: 论文提出CueClash基准，用渲染场景分离视觉语言模型判断3-D关系时依赖的2-D图像线索与地面布局线索，并评估Claude Opus 5.5、GPT-5.5和Gemini 3.6 Flash等前沿模型。实验发现模型在2-D线索误导时准确率明显下降，但给定地面坐标和关系定义时几乎全对，说明主要失败在从图像到布局的转换；模型生成的俯视地图保留深度顺序但压缩深度，且在CV-Bench上也受可靠但误导的2-D线索影响。

Details

Motivation: 照片中2-D线索与3-D地面布局通常一致，使VLM可能走捷径而未真正理解3-D关系。论文希望分离这两类线索，定位模型判断3-D关系时的具体失败环节。

Result: 在CueClash上，低推理努力下shortcut gap为+18至+37个百分点，剔除两个混杂关系后为+7至+29；给定地面坐标时准确率达99%至100%。三个模型在CV-Bench中遇到可靠但误导的2-D线索时也掉点，且两个开源模型中布局信息虽到达语言模型，但其地图丢失较多信息。

Insight: 创新点在于构建可控渲染基准，使每种关系的2-D线索恰好一半正确，并用shortcut gap及俯视地图分析诊断VLM的3-D关系判断。客观看，论文将失败归因于“图像到布局”的转换，并揭示模型地图存在深度压缩和类图像复制现象，为多模态空间推理评测提供了可借鉴范式。

Abstract: A vision-language model (VLM) can judge 3-D relations from the picture or from the ground layout. In photographs the two usually agree. We separate them with CueClash, rendered scenes in which the 2-D cue for each of five relations is right on exactly half the statements. The shortcut gap is accuracy when the cue is right minus accuracy when it is wrong. For Claude Opus 5.5, GPT-5.5 and Gemini 3.6 Flash at low reasoning effort it is +18 to +37 percentage points. It is +7 to +29 without two relations whose cue-wrong items are confounded with borderline cases or occlusion. Given ground coordinates and each relation’s definition, they answer 99 to 100% correctly. Pixel measurements with camera depths remove most of the gap for objects fitting through openings. The failure lies in the step from the picture to the layout. The maps that the models draw of the scene from above mostly keep the depth order but, like the picture, shorten depth. GPT-5.5’s maps stay close to a copy of the picture. Opus 5.5’s and Gemini’s maps undo about 0.6 of this shortening. Gemini’s then shrink all depths by a further uniform factor. Opus 5.5 follows the picture mainly on fits, and its maps correct depth about as much as human observers of real scenes do. The three models also lose accuracy on CV-Bench photographs where a reliable 2-D cue misleads. In two open models the layout information reaches the language model, but their maps lose much of it.

Abstract: Reconstructing an articulated object with its 3D movable parts, joint parameters and unseen articulation states from an uncalibrated monocular video is essential for robot manipulation and interactive digital twins. Existing per-scene optimisation methods require minutes to hours and external depth or camera poses, whereas feed-forward 3D foundation models reconstruct dense point maps in one pass without segmenting movable parts or inferring joints. Even on these monocular point maps, regressing or fitting continuous joints directly fails on casual videos, since cross-frame attention blurs moving part boundaries in deep layers, underestimates motion along the viewing ray and leaves continuous axes ill-conditioned under small opening angles. To address these challenges, we propose PhyVGGT, a single-pass framework on a frozen 3D foundation model that anchors moving-part matching and closed-form joint estimation to the undistorted geometry of the stationary base. First, our Part and Motion Decoder fuses intermediate per-frame tokens with global tokens and references part queries to a static-base query to segment 3D parts, align point maps on the static base and predict learned Bingham priors. Second, our Kinematic Inference Layer fits an orthogonal Manhattan frame to static surface normals, selects the joint axis in closed form under a Bingham posterior combining pairwise motion scatter with the learned priors, and solves a linear system for the hinge pivot to drive unseen states. Trained solely on synthetic data, PhyVGGT achieves state-of-the-art monocular performance across five benchmarks, outperforming FAMOS and monocular ArtiPoint, surpassing posed RGB-D ArtiPoint in median error, and running in 6.8 s per 48-frame video against hours of per-scene optimisation in Articulat3D. TL;DR: 论文提出 Decoding Without a Decoder（DWD），让微调后的扩散 Transformer 通过线性预测头在最后一步直接输出像素，从而移除潜空间扩散模型中的解码器。方法仅微调最后几个 Transformer block，并用像素质量损失训练 DWD 头，同时锚定原去噪头以保持采样轨迹。该方法在图像和视频潜生成器中降低推理成本并保持或提升像素质量，也可用于改进无解码器的像素扩散模型。

Details

Motivation: 潜扩散模型依赖解码器将去噪后的 latent 映射到像素空间，增加了模型复杂度和推理成本。论文关注如何在不破坏原有采样轨迹的情况下去掉解码器，并直接在最后一步预测高质量像素。

Result: 在 Wan 视频生成上，DWD 明显减少细节失真和闪烁，将 warping error 降低 23%；在像素流模型文本到图像生成中提升纹理细节，并将 ImageNet FID 从 1.55 降到 1.43，达到像素流模型中的新 SOTA。整体上，DWD 在移除解码器的同时保持甚至提升像素质量并降低推理成本。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models rely on a decoder to map denoised latents to pixels, adding complexity and inference cost. We introduce Decoding Without a Decoder (DWD), which lets a finetuned diffusion transformer produce final pixels directly through a linear head. Unlike previous methods, where the denoising output leads to final pixels, DWD separates the two roles: the original denoising head continues to drive sampling, then the new DWD head predicts the pixels at the final step. We extend pretrained latent models with DWD efficiently by finetuning only the last few transformer blocks, training the DWD head with pixel quality losses while anchoring the denoising head to the pretrained model to preserve the sample trajectory. Across latent image and video generators, DWD removes the decoder and reduces inference cost while maintaining or even improving pixel quality. On Wan video generation, it visibly reduces detail distortion and flickering, lowering warping error by 23%. Remarkably, the same separation also improves pixel diffusion models that do not even use a decoder: a final-step DWD head produces substantially better texture detail in text-to-image generation, and improves ImageNet FID from 1.55 to 1.43, a new state of the art among pixel flow models.

Abstract: Video understanding with multiple multimodal language models requires a communication interface that can transfer complementary visual observations. We study Cross-Modal StateKV Injection, a provider–carrier framework that communicates through key–value (KV) states at visual-token positions. Provider models observe complementary video frames; their visual KV states are aligned and projected into the carrier representation space and integrated through gated residual updates. Details

Motivation: 现有视频世界模型缺乏显式的惯性状态演化机制，运动与结构连续性只能隐式体现在预测中；自回归推演时反复重建会累积误差并导致预测动力学不稳定。

Result: 论文在机器人操作、3D游戏仿真和开放世界导航等多个领域进行实验，表明 InertialWorld 的自回归预测性能与强视频世界模型基线相比具有竞争力，但摘要未明确给出具体指标或宣称达到SOTA。

Abstract: The physical world has inertia: it evolves from what already exists rather than being recreated at every instant. However, many existing video world models lack an explicit mechanism for inertial state evolution, leaving the continuity of motion and structure implicit in video prediction. During autoregressive rollouts, repeated reconstruction can accumulate errors and destabilize the predicted dynamics. To address this limitation, we introduce InertialWorld, an Inertial Video World Model that makes inertia a structural prior for autoregressive prediction: existing scene evidence is carried across time rather than reconstructed at every step. Central to this formulation is Inertial Memory, which turns memory from passive predictive context into an evolving video state with two complementary views: a dense Eulerian grid preserves scene-aligned evidence, while persistent Lagrangian carriers bind local content to learned canonical coordinates. At each step, the model transports the carrier content according to action-conditioned motion, predicts the complete next video state from the transported carriers and dense grid, and uses a learned gate to combine transported and newly predicted content into the memory for the next step. Extensive experiments across multiple domains, including robot manipulation, 3D game simulation, and open-world navigation, demonstrate that InertialWorld achieves competitive autoregressive prediction performance against strong video world model baselines.
