[49] M$^3$-Test: Coverage Gaps in Joint-Perturbation Testing of Visual Question Answering cs.CVPDF

Enhui Wan, Xiaowan Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出 M3-Test，一种面向视觉问答（VQA）联合扰动测试的覆盖审计方法：将每个干净输入与单模态变体以及完整的 5x5 家族、4x4 强度联合库对齐，使缩减后的测试套件可在相同请求上重放。基于四个视觉语言模型的 260 万条响应，论文揭示通过单模态测试的问题仍存在联合失败覆盖缺口，并提供请求级风险排序与可复用评估。

Details

Motivation: 标准鲁棒性报告对条件取平均，无法指出哪些已接受请求在图像与问题联合变化时仍然脆弱；论文关注“单模态分别通过但联合扰动失败”的覆盖缺口。

Result: 在四个视觉语言模型、260 万条响应上，Qwen 中通过全部 40 项单模态测试的问题仍有 26%–29% 在联合测试中失败，而联合记录失败比例低于 0.6%；通过 25 个最大强度角点后仍有 11%–14% 的已接受问题在其他位置失败。失败复现率约 90%（匹配通过控制翻转率 0.3%–0.6%），最脆弱失败记录再检查支持率 80%–89%；风险排序在 25 次额外查询内发现约 80% 受影响问题，优于最大优先的 65%–68% 和成对覆盖阵列的 53%–57%。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点是对齐可重放的联合扰动覆盖审计，将通过的鲁棒性套件转化为请求级“仍未测试风险”账本，并证明缺口不能完全由执行噪声或评分错误解释。客观来看，其价值在于把鲁棒性评估从平均指标推进到可定位、可复用的测试分配与回归审查。

Abstract: A visual question answering (VQA) model can pass every image-only and question-only test yet fail when the same changes occur together. Standard robustness reports average over conditions and therefore cannot say which accepted requests remain vulnerable. We introduce M3-Test, a coverage audit that aligns each clean input with its single-modality variants and a complete 5x5 family, 4x4 strength joint library, so that any reduced test suite can be replayed on the same requests. Across 2.6 million responses from four vision-language models, joint failures affect 26-29% of the Qwen questions that pass all 40 single-modality tests, although fewer than 0.6% of their joint records fail. Passing the 25 maximum-strength corners does not close this gap: 11-14% of the accepted questions still fail elsewhere in the library. Re-executing the archived failures reproduces about 90% of them, against a 0.3-0.6% flip rate among matched passing controls, and re-checking the most fragile failure records supports 80-89% of them. On the evidence examined, neither execution noise nor grading error can fully account for the gap. The same aligned grids make previous evaluations reusable. On held-out images, a risk order learned from other models discovers about 80% of affected Qwen questions within 25 additional queries, compared with 65-68% for maximum-first and 53-57% for pairwise covering arrays. M3-Test thus turns a passing robustness suite into a request-level account of what remains untested, supporting acceptance, regression review and test allocation.

[50] PURE: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Multi-Granularity Multimodal Identity Matching cs.CVPDF

Hengyu You, Xintong Wang, Jin Cao, Liang Ding

TL;DR: 论文提出 PURE，一个面向多粒度多模态身份匹配的诊断性基准，用于判断两条记录是否指代同一身份，并区分基础级匹配与变体级匹配。该基准基于电商真实匹配对构建，包含 1,457 道多选题、14 个任务，覆盖感知、理解、推理和基于证据的判断四个能力维度，并系统评测了十个多模态大模型。

Details

Motivation: 传统身份匹配只看最终准确率，无法区分模型在不同粒度、不同能力上的表现，也难以解释模型究竟在哪里出错。论文希望用诊断性基准揭示聚合准确率所掩盖的能力差异，尤其是在真实电商场景中基础级相同但变体级不同（如颜色、尺寸不同）的匹配问题。

Result: 在十个近期多模态大模型上，PURE 总体得分范围为 47.1% 到 86.0%；理解维度平均最高，判断维度最低，模型在视觉差异定位和变体级规则判断上较弱。模态消融显示多模态证据总体有益，但模型会错误识别匹配记录之间的冲突；直接匹配中，多数模型在基础级更可靠识别匹配对，而变体级则相反。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于将身份匹配拆解为多粒度与多能力维度的诊断性评测，而不是仅报告总体匹配准确率；其客观价值在于用真实电商数据和多选题任务定位模型在感知、理解、推理及证据判断上的具体薄弱环节，并揭示基础级与变体级匹配中的不对称表现。

Abstract: Identity matching asks whether two records denote the same identity, yet the answer depends on the target granularity. Two records may match at the base level but not at the variant level: they share an underlying design while differing in attributes such as color or size. Final matching accuracy does not separately characterize the capabilities involved. To study this problem, we use e-commerce as a challenging real-world domain. We introduce PURE, a diagnostic benchmark for multi-granularity multimodal identity matching covering four capability dimensions: Perception, Understanding, Reasoning, and Evidence-grounded Judgment. The benchmark comprises 1,457 multiple-choice questions across 14 tasks, derived from real-world matching pairs and verified by two domain experts. Across ten recent MLLMs, overall scores range from 47.1% to 86.0%. Understanding receives the highest mean score and Judgment the lowest, with weaknesses in visual difference localization and variant-level rule judgment. Modality ablations show that models benefit from multimodal evidence but can incorrectly identify conflicts between matching records. In direct matching, most models recognize matching pairs more reliably than non-matching pairs at the base level, whereas the reverse holds at the variant level. PURE therefore reveals what aggregate matching accuracy hides: not only whether models judge a pair correctly, but where they struggle to interpret evidence and apply matching criteria.

Details

Motivation: 现有视觉语言基准大多符合人类常识，模型可能利用数据集先验和统计共现取得高分，难以判断其预测是否真正基于图像内容。论文旨在检验并推动忠实的视觉理解。

Result: 在ContraSense上，当前MLLM表现显著落后于人类：最强闭源模型准确率仅约60%，人类达到96.04%，表明模型在反直觉场景下仍难以依赖视觉证据推理。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新点是构建反直觉、低统计偏差的基准，以迫使模型依赖视觉证据。客观看，其价值在于暴露常识先验对现有评测的污染，并提供可公开使用的诊断性评测集。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive performance on a wide range of vision-language tasks. However, it remains unclear whether their predictions are grounded in the visual content itself or are largely driven by statistical co-occurrence patterns and commonsense correlations learned from large-scale image-text corpora. Existing vision-language benchmarks predominantly consist of samples that conform to human commonsense, allowing models to achieve strong performance by exploiting dataset priors rather than genuinely understanding image content. To address this limitation, we present ContraSense, a systematically constructed benchmark for evaluating faithful vision-language understanding. ContraSense consists of carefully curated counter-intuitive image-text pairs that deliberately violate common expectations, substantially reducing the effectiveness of statistical biases and requiring models to rely on visual evidence for reasoning. We conduct comprehensive evaluations on a diverse set of representative open- and closed-source MLLMs. The results reveal a substantial performance gap between current MLLMs and humans: even the strongest closed-source model achieves only about 60% accuracy, compared with 96.04% for human participants. These findings demonstrate that current MLLMs still struggle with faithful visual understanding and highlight the need for more robust multimodal reasoning grounded in image content. The ContraSense benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/LingLin00/ContraSense.

[52] CoBoVLA: Decoupling Language from Action with Discrete Codes for Robust and Interpretable Robot Manipulation cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Mehul Arora, Dhruv Agarwal, Ambuj Agrawal

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoBoVLA（CodeBookVLA），一个 250M 参数的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过可学习的离散可解释码将语言理解、规划和动作控制解耦：文本编码器将指令映射为共享步骤码，视觉规划器再生成动作码序列，由与语言无关的执行器执行。该设计提升了对指令改写的鲁棒性，并支持按阶段定位失败，同时可在资源受限硬件上实时控制。

Details

Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在 LIBERO 上成功率较高，但对指令改写非常敏感，性能可下降 22-52 个点；由于多数 VLA 联合学习语言理解与动作生成，难以判断失败来自误解指令还是执行错误。论文希望解耦语言与控制，以提升鲁棒性和可诊断性。

Result: CoBoVLA 在 LIBERO-Goal 上达到 91.4% 成功率，高于匹配协议下 SmolVLA 的 82.8%，控制步延迟 17 ms；在 LIBERO-Para 的 4,092 条未见改写上达到 81.1%，匹配 episodes 上 78.5%，显著高于 SmolVLA LoRA 的 36.5%；无两步演示时完成 48.8% 的自由形式两步任务。分析显示指令正确编码时成功率 91%，否则降至 27.2%。

Insight: 创新点在于用离散共享步骤码和动作码显式解耦语言理解、规划与控制，使失败可追溯到具体阶段，并只需编码一次指令即可支持轻量实时控制。客观看，其模块化离散码瓶颈兼顾了解释性、鲁棒性和部署效率，是较有借鉴价值的设计。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve high success rates on the LIBERO benchmark but remain sensitive to paraphrasing. Existing VLAs ranging from 0.6B to 7.5B parameters experience a 22-52 point performance drop on LIBERO-Para while SmolVLA exhibits an even larger decline, from 83% to 8%. Because most VLAs jointly learn language understanding and action generation, it is difficult to determine whether a failure originates from misunderstanding an instruction or executing it incorrectly. To address these challenges, we introduce CoBoVLA, or CodeBookVLA, a 250M-parameter VLA that decouples language understanding, planning, and control through learned discrete interpretable codes. A text encoder maps the instructions to shared step codes, which a visual planner then converts into action code sequences executed by a language-independent actor. This design improves robustness to instruction paraphrasing while allowing failures to be traced to individual stages. It also enables efficient real-time control on resource-constrained hardware by encoding instructions only once and using compact discrete representations to keep each component lightweight. CoBoVLA achieves 91.4% success on LIBERO-Goal, compared with 82.8% for SmolVLA, under a matched protocol, with 17 ms per control step latency. On LIBERO-Para, it achieves 81.1% across 4,092 unseen paraphrases and 78.5% on matched episodes, compared with 36.5% for SmolVLA LoRA fine-tuned on paraphrases. Without two-step demonstrations, CoBoVLA completes 48.8% of 160 free-form two-step tasks. Finally, success reaches 91% when instructions are correctly encoded but falls to 27.2% otherwise, identifying language understanding as the main source of remaining failures. Together, these results demonstrate that decoupling language understanding and control can improve instruction robustness while making VLA failures easier to diagnose.

Jongbhin Woo, Youngjoon Jang, Joon Son Chung

TL;DR: 论文提出 SignAligner，一个基于提示条件的视觉-语言时间跨度生成器：输入手语视频和字幕句子或目标词查询，输出对应的起止帧区间。模型先在字幕跨度和手语定位得到的词跨度上联合训练，再用基于时间重叠的奖励进行精炼，从而统一实现字幕对齐和词跨度生成。

Details

Motivation: 大规模手语语料需要字幕跨度和词跨度两种时间标签，但如何利用已有的词级手语定位数据联合训练字幕对齐仍缺乏探索。论文希望用一个共享生成器同时支持字幕对齐和词级定位，为语料构建和人工审核提供帮助。

Result: 在 BOBSL 字幕测试集上达到 66.68 F1@0.50，为无外部手语视频资源对比系统中的最佳；重叠奖励精炼优于同一联合训练模型的交叉熵继续训练，且联合词跨度训练能提升字幕对齐。词查询实验中模型能跟随查询词而非固定视频位置，在词远离中心时显著优于仅依赖位置的预测。

Insight: 创新点在于双粒度跨度联合训练与重叠奖励精炼的统一框架，使同一生成器兼具字幕对齐和词跨度生成能力；客观上看，其可借鉴之处是利用易获得的词级定位数据辅助字幕级对齐，并用基于时间 IoU 的奖励直接优化定位质量。

Abstract: Large sign-language corpora benefit from temporal labels at two scales: subtitle spans for subtitle alignment and word spans for sign spotting. Sign spottings already locate words in time at corpus scale, but training subtitle alignment jointly on subtitle and word spans remains underexplored. We present SignAligner, a prompt-conditioned visual-language span generator that maps a sign-language video and a subtitle-sentence or target-word query to a textual start/end frame interval. It is first trained jointly on subtitle spans and word spans from sign spottings, then refined with overlap-based rewards while retaining auxiliary word examples. On the subtitle test set of the British Broadcasting Corporation-Oxford British Sign Language (BOBSL) corpus, the refined model reaches 66.68 F1@0.50 (F1 with a temporal intersection-over-union threshold of one half), the best among compared systems without external sign-video resources. This overlap-reward refinement outperforms cross-entropy continuation from the same jointly trained model. Joint training with word spans improves subtitle alignment in a pair of our generators matched in code, labels and maximum update budget. The advantage remains after both models of the pair are refined with overlap rewards. For word queries, the refined model follows the queried word, not a fixed position in the video: when the word’s sign lies away from the video centre, it is far more accurate than predictions that use position alone. Together, these findings support one shared span generator refined with overlap rewards, aligning subtitles and producing word spans for human review.

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate promising capabilities in 2D visual understanding, yet remain limited in 3D spatial reasoning. Recent studies explore integrating 3D reconstruction models into MLLMs. However, these approaches either use a single geometric granularity across all tasks or adopt a fixed multi-level fusion scheme, regardless of task-specific reasoning requirements. Through systematic experiments on multi-level geometric fusion, we find that spatial tasks at different cognitive levels (e.g., distance estimation vs. route planning) require geometric representations of different granularities, revealing that fixed fusion cannot accommodate diverse spatial reasoning requirements. To bridge this gap, we propose SpatialMind, a task-adaptive framework for dynamically integrating multi-level geometric representations. SpatialMind employs GeoFus, a multi-layer geometric fusion module built around a gating network jointly conditioned on textual instructions, visual semantics, and geometric representations. This network adaptively modulates the contributions of shallow, intermediate, and deep geometric features across the layers of the LLM decoder. Furthermore, we introduce a negative-entropy regularization loss to mitigate gate collapse. Gate visualizations indicate that GeoFus adapts geometric granularity to task requirements. Experiments demonstrate that SpatialMind achieves competitive performance across spatial benchmarks, attaining a state-of-the-art score of 72.3 on VSI-Bench. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/zhaosb-star/SpatialMind.

Abstract: E-commerce advertising video creation requires both high-level creative planning and reliable multi-modal production. CutAgent comprises two collaborative agents, $\textbf{GeneAgent}$ and $\textbf{VideoAgent}$, connected by a structured creative gene that bridges product understanding and video production. GeneAgent maps multi-modal product inputs—textual attributes together with product images—to creative genes that specify narrative units and production rules. To train GeneAgent, we construct $\textbf{CutAgent-70K}$ with approximately 70K samples of product attributes/images, reverse-extracted creative genes, cleaned clip pools, and real-world CTR, and apply high-CTR supervised fine-tuning followed by CTR-guided GRPO. [56] As Student Rollouts Continue, Visual Backdoors Keep Growing cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Zane Fu, Yanzhao Zheng, Jinze Liu, Ziting Qin, Qianshan Wei

TL;DR: 论文研究了On-Policy Distillation (OPD)中视觉后门的传播风险。传统观点认为OPD对后门传播有内在抵抗力，但作者通过实验和理论分析发现，OPD中后门传播存在阈值效应：低毒化率下后门很少出现，但超过临界阈值后攻击成功率迅速接近100%。传播由教师分配的异常概率质量持续积累驱动，而非显式恶意响应。基于此，作者提出了一种零显式触发器注入的新型触发器设计，揭示了OPD中被忽视的后门传播机制和安全风险。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注OPD这一新范式下后门传播的风险。传统知识蒸馏中后门可传播，但OPD因其在线监督方式和初始学生良性响应，看似有抵抗力。作者旨在探究OPD中是否真的存在后门传播风险及其机制。

Result: 实验跨多个模型家族和数据集，结合理论分析，发现OPD中后门传播具有阈值效应：低毒化率下后门很少出现，但超过临界阈值后攻击成功率迅速接近100%。传播由教师异常概率质量的积累驱动，而非显式恶意响应。作者还提出零显式触发器注入的触发器设计，实现了后门传播。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点包括：挑战OPD有抵抗力的直觉，揭示OPD中后门传播的阈值现象和驱动机制（异常概率质量积累），并提出零显式触发器注入实现后门传播。客观分析认为，创新之处在于首次系统研究OPD中的后门传播，发现了与离线蒸馏不同的传播特性（阈值效应），并揭示了不依赖显式恶意响应的传播机制，这对VLM安全有重要启示。

Abstract: Conventional knowledge distillation can facilitate the propagation of backdoors from teacher models to student models. However, how this risk manifests in On-Policy Distillation (OPD) remains insufficiently studied. OPD has recently emerged as an important post-training paradigm for Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Unlike traditional offline distillation, OPD does not require teacher-generated responses. Instead, it uses the teacher’s probability distributions to supervise response trajectories generated online by the student. Since the initial student model typically generates benign responses and receives no explicit supervision from malicious target responses, OPD appears to offer some inherent resistance to backdoor propagation. In this paper, we challenge this intuition and reveal a more subtle risk of visual backdoor propagation in OPD. Through experiments across multiple model families and datasets, together with theoretical analysis, we identify three main findings. First, unlike the relatively gradual propagation observed in traditional offline distillation, backdoors rarely emerge in OPD at low poisoning ratios. However, the attack success rate rapidly approaches 100% once a critical threshold is exceeded. Second, backdoor propagation does not depend on explicit malicious responses. Instead, it is driven by the continuous accumulation of anomalous probability mass assigned by the teacher. Finally, based on these findings, we propose a novel trigger design that enables visual backdoor propagation with zero explicit trigger injection. These findings further reveal an overlooked mechanism of backdoor propagation in OPD and highlight its potential security risks.

[57] Sample-Aligned Decoders for Latent Generative Models cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Yu Lei, Yi Li, Hongcheng Gao, Yipeng Wei, Liang Li

TL;DR: 论文指出潜空间生成模型的解码器只见过编码器输出的真实潜变量，从未见过测试时生成的潜变量，导致生成器的残差误差被直接渲染进最终图像。作者提出样本对齐解码器（SAD），这是一个仅微调解码器、冻结生成器的后训练框架：在冻结的基础模型特征空间中将解码后的生成潜变量与无配对真实图像对齐，同时用编码器锚定约束防止解码器遗忘原潜空间。

Details

Motivation: 现有补救方案要么重训生成器、要么增加采样开销、要么假设生成器误差类似对真实潜变量的扰动，但作者发现该假设不成立——生成潜变量虽同样位于编码器潜流形上，却服从不同分布。因此应让解码器适配生成潜变量的分布，但这带来两个挑战：生成潜变量没有配对真值图像（无配对监督），以及脱离配对目标后解码器会偏离原潜空间（潜空间漂移）。

Result: 在ImageNet上，SAD跨三种tokenizer提升了四个SOTA生成器，例如将REPA-E的FID-50K从1.15降至0.98，且与判别器引导互补，并可扩展到文生图生成；推理阶段无额外开销。

Insight: 创新点在于只对冻结生成器的解码器做后训练、无推理成本，用冻结基础模型特征空间中的无配对分布对齐加编码器锚定来同时解决无监督与潜空间漂移问题；作者还从理论上分析了该对齐仅在生成潜变量上有效、且校正强度与生成器产生该样本的相对频率成正比，这一视角对潜空间生成模型的误差归因具有借鉴意义。

Abstract: Latent generative models synthesize images by decoding generated latents, yet their decoder is trained only to reconstruct encoder latents and never sees the latents it decodes at test time, so the residual errors of the generator are rendered directly into the final image. Existing remedies retrain the generator, add cost at every sampling step, or make the decoder robust to perturbed real latents, assuming that generator errors resemble such perturbations. We find that they do not. Generated latents lie on the encoder latent manifold as closely as real ones, yet follow a different distribution. The decoder should therefore be adapted to the distribution of generated latents, which raises two challenges: (1) unpaired supervision, since a generated latent has no ground-truth image, and (2) latent-space drift, since a decoder freed from paired targets forgets the latent space it must decode. We propose Sample-Aligned Decoders (SAD), a post-training framework that fine-tunes only the decoder of a frozen generator at no inference cost. Generated-Sample Distribution Alignment matches decoded generated latents to unpaired real images in a frozen foundation-model feature space, and Encoder Anchor Preservation keeps the decoder close to the original one on encoder latents. Our analysis shows that the alignment is effective only on generated latents, where it corrects each sample in proportion to how much more often the generator produces such latents than the encoder; on encoder latents it can only work against the anchor. SAD improves four state-of-the-art ImageNet generators across three tokenizers, e.g., FID-50K from 1.15 to 0.98 for REPA-E, complements discriminator guidance, and extends to text-to-image generation. Our code and models are available at https://torpedo2003.github.io/SAD/.

Details

Motivation: 手工从参考视频重建3D blockout成本高，限制了创作者进行预演和视频生成条件构建，也阻碍配对训练数据的大规模生产；因此需要自动、可编辑且度量一致的重建方法与评测基准。

Abstract: Animated 3D blockouts support previsualization and provide structural conditions for video generation, but manually reconstructing them from reference videos is costly. This limits both their use by creators and the production of paired training data. Yanchao Li, Jiaqing Xie, Ben Gao, Yusu Zhang, Beilun Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出 PRUDENT 方法用于视觉 token 剪枝：先以约一半的预算回答所有问题，再根据一个小型 VLM 在完整图像上的回答与剪枝后答案是否分歧，来决定将节省下来的 token 重新分配给哪些问题（以两倍预算重答）。该方法将视觉 token 剪枝从“优化 token 排序”扩展为“识别哪些剪枝答案不可信并重新分配预算”。

Details

Motivation: 现有剪枝方法对所有问题使用相同预算，只关注提升 token 排序质量；但被剪掉的 token 所包含的证据在保留 token 上无法体现，因此难以判断哪些剪枝答案不可靠。论文希望利用这些不易察觉的失败信号来动态分配 token 预算。

Result: 在 18 个 benchmark 上，在相同平均视觉 token 预算下优于 8 种现有剪枝方法；预算越紧，领先幅度越大；在 5 种 InternVL 规模及另外两个模型家族上均取得最高平均表现；小型 VLM 的分歧还能标记出大多数剪枝失败案例。

Insight: 创新点在于把“小型 VLM 在完整图像下的分歧”作为剪枝答案可靠性的判别信号，实现问题级的自适应预算重分配，而不是单纯改进 token 排序；这为视觉 token 剪枝提供了一个与排序正交的新维度，且分歧检测器可替换、具备通用性。

Abstract: Visual token pruning speeds up large vision-language models (VLMs) by keeping only a part of their visual tokens. Most existing methods improve the ranking of the tokens and then answer every question once with the same budget. With strong rankings, pruning hurts only a few questions, and signals computed on the kept tokens see little of what it removed. We find that, on most of these questions, a small VLM given the full image disagrees with the pruned answer, since it still sees the missing evidence. We propose PRUDENT, which first answers with about half the budget and spends the saved tokens on re-answering the disagreeing questions at twice the budget. We show when such re-answering pays and why the small VLM should read the full image. On 18 benchmarks, PRUDENT outperforms eight existing pruning methods at the same average budget of visual tokens. Its lead widens as the budget tightens, and holds across five InternVL sizes. PRUDENT also has the highest average in two more model families. The disagreement of the small VLM flags most pruning failures of all eight methods, and re-answering on it also improves the two strongest with their own rankings. Other checkers give similar gains, and the extra tokens go where the first pass hurts the answer. These results suggest that identifying which pruned answers are trustworthy provides an additional lever for visual token pruning beyond the ranking of tokens. Code and data are available at https://github.com/LiZaiyuan0619/PRUDENT.

Abstract: Reward models for instruction-guided video editing can favor temporal consistency at the expense of motion. We study this failure, termed \emph{motion collapse}, using controlled motion-suppression variants of real edits. On VE-Bench, the evaluated composite reward prefers a motion-suppressed candidate in $97%$ of cases. Learned video rewards and a VLM judge also struggle with mild suppression, while latent optimization and reward-guided fine-tuning exploit this weakness to increase reward as motion declines. A linear probe of our base reward backbone performs near chance on mild suppression, motivating an explicit inter-frame dynamics input. We propose a motion-aware reward model that combines an RGB frame-difference stream with preference training against motion-suppressed and fabricated-motion negatives. A distillation anchor preserves alignment with human quality judgments. On the VE-Bench motion ladder, our model reduces the rate of selecting motion-suppressed candidates to $0%$ while achieving a Spearman correlation of $0.57$ with human quality ratings. It also detects unseen suppression variants and preserves motion during reward-guided fine-tuning on two editing pipelines. We present a causal, training-free pipeline that adds two temporal components to a frozen MR. Illuminate image prior. Temporal input fusion recursively averages motion-compensated low-light frames before diffusion inversion. Late-start offset propagation runs the full sampling trajectory only on key frames. Each non-key frame inverts the low-noise part of its own trajectory and adds an enhancement offset warped from the latest key frame. At ZVRD’s own resolution the pipeline is $261\times$ faster than ZVRD and scores better on all five metrics we report. Measurements inside the model show why, and a linear-Gaussian model of inversion explains them. Fusion removes frame-to-frame noise before inversion amplifies it. A flow error perturbs the transported offset ten times less than it perturbs a transported state. The perceptual gain does not transfer to low resolution: at $346 \times 260$, LPIPS is worse by 0.028. At 0.65 frames per second, the pipeline is an offline method.

[65] Plausible Videos Can Lead to Poor Decisions: Diagnosing and CURE-ing World Models for Planning cs.CVPDF

Raphael C. Kim, Michele Santacatterina, Jingsen Zhu, Ramin Zabih

TL;DR: 论文指出，动作条件世界模型即使能生成与训练数据一致、视觉上逼真的视频，也可能导致较差的规划决策。作者提出 CURE（Certify、Upweight、Rollout、Explore），通过证书评估世界模型是否可信，并据此选择重加权训练数据去偏、测试时增加采样或主动探索收集缺失支撑区域的数据。

Details

Motivation: 现有世界模型常以视频生成质量或感知真实性衡量，但视觉上合理的视频并不保证规划决策可靠；论文关注如何判断世界模型何时可用于规划，以及不可信时应采取什么补救措施。

Result: 在 PhyWorld、MuJoCo 机械臂控制和 Language-Table 推物等仿真及真实视频任务中，证书可信的决策遗憾更低，例如 Language-Table 仿真为 0.06 对 0.38、MuJoCo 接触任务为 0.07 对 0.38；相比最佳悲观决策规则降低 10-25% 遗憾，相比世界-动作模型、搜索和策略学习在相同数据下降低 41-64%。

Insight: 创新点在于用证书从决策质量而非感知质量评估世界模型可信度，并给出 Certify、Upweight、Rollout、Explore 的闭环处理流程；客观看，其价值在于将世界模型评估与规划支撑覆盖联系起来，强调数据支撑不足时的主动探索和测试时采样。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models are increasingly used as world models for planning, yet a world model that makes plausible, realistic-looking videos that are consistent with the training data can produce poor decisions. We address two key questions: (1) when can we trust a world model for planning, and (2) what should we do when we cannot? We introduce CURE (Certify, Upweight, Rollout, Explore), a certificate-guided technique that assesses whether a world model can be trusted for planning and determines what to do next: whether to upweight the training data to debias the model; roll out more samples at test time; or explore by collecting new data where support (how well the training data cover the planner’s decisions) is missing. We deploy CURE across multiple domains, spanning simulated physics based on PhyWorld, MuJoCo arm control, and Language-Table pushing in simulation and on real videos. The decisions our certificate trusts have lower regret than those it does not (e.g., 0.06 vs. 0.38 on Language-Table sim and 0.07 vs. 0.38 on MuJoCo contact), whereas video perceptual quality does not identify good decisions. By focusing on decision quality rather than perceptual quality, CURE lowers regret over the best pessimistic decision rule by 10-25% across support levels, and over world-action models, search and policy learning by 41-64%, given the same data.

[66] Lost in Read-out: Locating the Image Quality Gap in Vision-Language Models with a Closed-Form Edit cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文探讨视觉语言模型（VLM）作为零样本图像质量评估器时，其判断与人类意见仅中等一致的原因：究竟是模型没有看出图像质量，还是未能把质量说清楚。作者通过逐层探针分析五个 VLM，发现图像质量信息在视觉编码器和最后一层语言层中可线性解码，且远好于模型最终答案所体现的水平；主要损失发生在最终的读出环节（read-out）。随后作者通过闭式编辑单个末层神经元进行干预验证，在 KonIQ 数据集与未参与拟合的提示词下，恢复了大部分本可被探针捕捉到的质量信息，同时保持感知问答能力不受影响。

Details

Motivation: VLM 被广泛用作零样本图像质量评估，但其好坏判断与人类主观意见只有中等相关性；论文想厘清这一差距究竟源于模型的感知能力不足，还是源于最终答案的输出环节，并尝试低成本地修复该差距。

Result: 在五个 VLM 上的逐层探针显示质量信息远高于答案所揭示的水平；闭式编辑单个末层神经元在未参与拟合的提示下恢复了 86–100% 的读出头额外信息（KonIQ），且不损害感知问答；但在最大两个模型上未达迁移标准、当读出头本身低于答案时会降低分数，且编辑仅从语言模型中部开始才有效，对是/否或打分式问题不迁移，遇到情感句还会反转好–坏对比。

Insight: 创新点在于提出“看得见却说不出”的定位框架，用逐层线性探针区分感知与读出，并以单神经元闭式编辑作为干预验证因果；客观看，其价值在于揭示 VLM 质量评估的瓶颈在最终读出层以及颜色/锐度偏好偏差，并提供一种轻量、可控且不破坏其他能力的修复手段，同时明确指出该方法的适用边界。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are widely used as zero-shot image quality assessors, yet their good-vs-poor answers agree only moderately with human opinion. We ask whether these models fail to see quality or fail to say it. Layer-wise probes on five VLMs point to the second: quality is linearly decodable from the vision encoder and still from the last language layer, far better than the answer reveals, and about a hundred labels recover most of it. The loss lies mainly in the final read-out, which partly reads something else: relative to human opinion, it over-rewards colorfulness and under-rewards sharpness on all five models. We test this by intervention: if the read-out is at fault, a closed-form edit of a single last-layer neuron, fitted to many prompts at once, should close the gap and no more. Both hold. Under prompts never used for fitting, the edit recovers 86–100% of what a ridge head on the last-layer state, fitted under the same prompts, finds beyond the answer on KonIQ. Where that gap is small, it gains little, too little for our transfer criterion on the two largest models; where the head itself is below the answer, it lowers the score; and it works only when written from the middle of the language model on. Unlike unconstrained fine-tuning, the edit leaves perception question answering intact. It does not carry over to yes-or-no or rating questions, and it reverses the good–poor contrast after sentiment-laden sentences. Code will be made available.

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide a unified interface for language-conditioned visual perception. In pixel-to-sequence grounding, autoregressive coordinate generation requires multiple sequential decoding steps per box, increasing inference cost with the number of instances. Pixel-to-embedding methods reduce this overhead but lack explicit spatial encoding when using a shared special token. We introduce SemGeo, a token-efficient visual grounding framework that represents each instance with a single grid token for coarse localization. A geometric decoder reuses visual features and hidden states to recover precise discrete box coordinates in parallel. To train proposal generation and box localization together, we introduce Gaussian soft labels that encode coordinate distances during supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Reinforcement post-training further combines box-set rewards with coordinate supervision on model-generated proposals. This stage trains the language model and geometric decoder on their own predictions to mitigate the mismatch between teacher-forced training and inference. Across eight benchmarks spanning object detection, referring object detection, and document layout grounding, SemGeo achieves the highest F1@mIou among compared MLLMs on six benchmarks. It also delivers $2.07\times$ and $1.25\times$ the mean box-generation throughput of representative grounding baselines under vLLM and Transformers, respectively, while achieving higher accuracy in both comparisons.

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative denoising. Few-step distillation reduces the number of model evaluations, but its coarse updates can move the student away from the teacher’s trajectory. We propose Anchored Path Matching (APM), which supervises endpoint and short-transition predictions from states reached by the student. From each state, we continue the teacher to zero noise and use its endpoint and first intermediate state as targets. Modality-routed adapters learn to match both targets from the same starting state. When the two gradients conflict, anchor-priority updates preserve the endpoint gradient and remove the opposing component of the local gradient within each modality. One checkpoint supports 1-, 2-, and 4-step joint generation. The sampling budget can be chosen at deployment without retraining or teacher queries. Abstract: Counting distinct objects in a video is hard when the camera returns to places it has seen. A vision-language model that reads the whole video answers with a few small numbers, and one that reads it in parts counts an object again in every part that shows it. Object trackers need the category before the video is read, or a fixed vocabulary. We observe that a memory for counting does not need to know what it is looking for, only where it has been: objects rarely leave a room between visits, so parts of a video grouped by place share almost no objects, and a place can be recognised before any question is asked. Building on this, we introduce PlaceTally, a question-blind test-time memory model that constructs a place graph for each video. Each video fragment joins the place of its best earlier match or opens a new place, because a strong match is rarely wrong while a true return often matches weakly. A frozen vision-language model counts each place once from at most 128 of its frames, so every read has a bounded input and the memory grows with the number of places; the graph is built 15 times faster than real time. On RevisitCount, our benchmark of real room re-recordings, it scores 62.5% mean relative accuracy on test rooms and 61.2% on held-out rooms, against 51.2% and 44.3% for the same model reading the whole video. Abstract: Modern video moment retrieval systems often propose windows that overlap the target event, yet still select the wrong one. The verifier uses parent-aware interval evidence, structured Qwen3-VL event judgments, VideoLLaMA3 pairwise temporal signals, and dense temporal evidence, but never lets those signals freely reorder the top-K list. Details

Motivation: 人类能感知被遮挡物体的完整内容，而AI难以复现这一能力。现有扩散方法虽将非模态补全从图像扩展到视频，但缺乏辅助先验来指导重度遮挡内容重建，并难以在长序列中保持时序一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明REVEAL在现有benchmark上达到SOTA，并在较长序列上保持高质量的时序一致性；同时提出LAVAT新benchmark用于评测文本引导的视频非模态补全。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点在于统一扩散框架中整合互补的运动与语义先验：文本查询同时用于开放词汇分割和纹理重建语义引导，光流warp掩码提供强形状先验。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是让语言与光流在分割和纹理两阶段分别发挥先验作用，并构建长视频文本描述基准以评测重度遮挡下的时序一致性。

Abstract: Amodal perception enables humans to perceive entire objects even when parts are occluded, a remarkable cognitive skill that artificial intelligence struggles to replicate. Recent diffusion-based methods have extended amodal completion from images to videos, yet they lack auxiliary priors to guide the reconstruction of heavily occluded content and to maintain temporal consistency over long sequences. We present REVEAL (REconstructing VidEo Amodal content via Language and flow guidance), a unified diffusion-based framework that integrates complementary motion and semantic priors for video amodal completion. A user-provided text query serves a dual role: it drives open-vocabulary video segmentation to obtain visible masks, and it provides semantic guidance for texture reconstruction. For amodal segmentation, we introduce optical flow guidance: by warping visible masks from previous frames to the current frame, the flow-warped mask propagates visible content into occluded regions and approximates the object’s current shape, providing a strong shape prior even under simultaneous deformation and occlusion. For texture completion, text guidance constrains the reconstruction of occluded appearance while providing a stable semantic reference that helps maintain visual coherence throughout the reconstruction. We also contribute LAVAT, a new benchmark featuring long video sequences paired with text descriptions, enabling evaluation of text-guided video amodal completion under heavy occlusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that REVEAL achieves state-of-the-art performance on existing benchmarks while maintaining high-quality temporal consistency over extended sequences. TL;DR: 论文提出免训练的AI生成图像检测器SaySee，不在生成图像上训练，而是利用视觉基础模型中已学到的“AI生成”概念：用仅真实性不同的成对文本描述构建生成方向并对图像嵌入投影打分，再用真实图像校正格式、图像类型和内容等造成的分数偏移。

Details

Motivation: 现有检测器在生成图像上训练会学到特定生成器的痕迹，难以泛化到新生成器；同时真实与生成训练图像在格式和内容上的差异会被当作真实性线索，损害鲁棒性和泛化性。

Result: 在覆盖标准基准、真实场景数据和最新生成器的24个评测集上，SaySee配合MoE-ViE取得98.2%平均AUC，比最佳现有检测器高4.2个百分点；在将格式与真实性解耦时仍为所有方法中最佳。

Abstract: Must a detector see generated images in order to catch them? Detection of AI-generated images has long faced two problems. The cues a detector learns from the few generators in its training set carry traces specific to those generators and transfer poorly to new ones, which limits generalization. The real and generated training images also differ in format and content, so the detector takes these differences as evidence of authenticity, and its decisions change with factors that have nothing to do with authenticity, which harms robustness as well as generalization. Both problems stem from training on generated images. A detector never trained on them can largely avoid both at the source. Vision foundation models have already learned the concept of “AI-generated” during pretraining, and text can point to it directly. Building on this, we propose SaySee, a training-free detector: Say builds a generation direction from sets of paired descriptions that differ only in authenticity and scores an image by the projection of its embedding onto it, and See uses real images to correct the score offsets caused by format, image type and content. On 24 evaluation sets that span standard benchmarks, in-the-wild data and the latest generators, SaySee reaches a mean AUC of 98.2% with MoE-ViE, 4.2 points above the best existing detector. When format is decoupled from authenticity, it remains the best of all methods. Code will be released.

Abstract: As multimodal large language models (MLLMs) advance toward understanding and interacting with the physical world, spatial reasoning becomes essential for understanding scene structure and anticipating the effects of physical changes. Existing spatial benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating spatial relations, memory, and viewpoint reasoning, but less systematically assess how models connect physical scene conditions with visual observations. We introduce SpatialCast, a benchmark that uses cast shadows to evaluate this ability. Cast shadows provide observable evidence of a 3D scene. Their image appearance depends on object geometry and pose, illumination, and viewpoint. We examine this relationship through three complementary tasks that assess scene-projection consistency, predict intervention-induced changes, and infer the physical changes behind visual transitions. The benchmark contains 21,000 synthetic questions with separate scene and geometry test partitions, complemented by 200 SpatialCast-Real questions from 20 photographed scenes. Evaluations show that closed-source MLLMs outperform general-purpose open-weight MLLMs and MLLMs designed for spatial reasoning, which remain near chance on the synthetic benchmark, while even strong closed-source models struggle with shadow-length and light-height judgments. We also use SpatialCast training data for GRPO post-training, improving open-weight MLLM performance on the benchmark. Further evaluations show improvements on several external spatial benchmarks, suggesting transfer of the learned capabilities. [75] VSpark: Speculative Decoding for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Suho Yoo, Youngjoon Jang, Hyebin Cho, Taeyeop Lee, Jaewook Jeong

TL;DR: 论文提出 VSpark，用于加速逐 token 生成机器人动作的自回归视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）。它基于投机解码：轻量草稿模型一次提出多个动作 token，VLA 单次前向验证并只接受与其预测一致的 token，从而保持无损解码；针对机器人控制中多个 token 对应近似相同运动、近似正确草稿仍被拒绝的问题，引入校准动作噪声收集 rollout、草稿与 VLA 隐特征对齐、以及拒绝草稿上的后续预测复用三项设计。

Details

Motivation: 自回归 VLA 逐 token 生成动作，推理慢；标准投机解码在机器人控制中加速受限，因为动作 token 存在多对一语义相似性，草稿接近正确动作仍可能因 token 不匹配被拒绝。

Result: 在四个采用不同动作 tokenizer 的 VLA 模型和四个机器人操作基准上，VSpark 实现 1.28x 到 2.10x 加速，同时保持 VLA 解码无损。

Insight: 创新点在于针对动作 token 的语义相似性重新设计投机解码：用校准动作噪声增强草稿覆盖、用隐特征对齐提升草稿与 VLA 的一致性、并复用被拒绝草稿路径上已计算的预测作为后续验证候选。客观看，这些设计将传统 token 级投机解码扩展到动作语义层面，兼顾加速与无损性。

Abstract: Our aim is to accelerate autoregressive vision-language-action models (VLAs), which generate robot actions one token at a time. Speculative decoding makes this possible: a lightweight draft model proposes several tokens at once, and the VLA verifies them in a single pass, accepting only those that match its own predictions, so decoding remains lossless. The speedup, however, is limited in robot control, where many different tokens encode nearly the same motion and a draft close to the correct action is still rejected. To address this, our approach, VSpark, is built upon three design choices: (i) rollouts collected with calibrated action noise, which expose the draft to a wider range of robot states; (ii) feature alignment, which trains the draft to match the VLA’s hidden features rather than its output tokens alone; and (iii) continuation reuse, which keeps the predictions the VLA has already computed along rejected drafts as candidates for the next verification step, instead of discarding them. On four VLAs with different action tokenizers and four manipulation benchmarks, VSpark achieves 1.28x to 2.10x speedup while keeping the VLA’s decoding lossless.

Weiwei Li, Junzhuo Liu, Tong Chu, Hengfu Yu, Yinjie Lei

TL;DR: 论文提出CHORD多视图去偏框架，利用多个表示空间之间的跨视图共识来缓解深度模型对虚假相关的依赖。它聚合各视图中的类内划分作为候选偏差代理，在无偏差监督下对目标表示和分类器去偏，并在视觉、语言、语音和医学影像的七个基准上提升了最差组准确率。

Details

Motivation: 现有去偏方法依赖人工指定的偏差属性或从单一表示空间推断未知偏差，但标注属性可能与模型实际使用的捷径不一致，且单一表示的证据不完整且可能有噪声。论文希望利用不同特征空间对偏差的互补可见性，在无偏差监督下更稳健地去偏。

Result: 在七个跨视觉、语言、语音和医学影像的基准上，CHORD在最差组准确率上取得一致提升；摘要未给出具体数值，也未明确宣称达到SOTA。

Insight: 创新点在于从多视图表示空间构建跨视图共识，并将各视图的类内划分作为候选偏差代理，从而避免依赖人工偏差标注或单一表示推断。客观看，其可借鉴之处是用多表示空间的互补证据增强未知偏差发现与去偏的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep models often rely on spurious correlations that fail on underrepresented groups. Existing methods either correct human-specified bias attributes or infer unknown biases from the learned representation space. However, annotated attributes may differ from the shortcuts actually used by a model, while a single representation provides incomplete and potentially noisy evidence. Our key insight is that a bias obscured in one feature space may be clearer in another. In this work, we propose CHORD (Cross-view Harmonic Outlier Reversal for Debiasing), a multi-view framework that allows samples to “sing in chorus” across diverse representation spaces to achieve robust cross-view consensus. CHORD aggregates aggregates within-class partitions in each view, which is a proxy for specifying candidate biases, to debias the target representation and classifier without bias supervision. Experiments on seven benchmarks across vision, language, speech, and medical imaging show consistent improvements in worst-group accuracy. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/davelee-uestc/chord_debiasing_public .

Abstract: Driving world models forecast what a vehicle’s sensors will record next. We study joint forecasting of RGB, thermal, and LiDAR on real adverse-weather sequences of the DSERT-RoLL dataset. Abstract: We introduce Faithfulness Video Score (FVSCORE), a neuro-symbolic framework for specification-driven video generation evaluation. Existing evaluators rely on holistic MLLM judgments or vision-language metrics, yet aligning their scores with human judgments and tracing them to specific temporal violations remain open challenges. FVSCORE addresses these limitations by compiling each video into explicit propositions capturing prompt requirements and implicit propositions capturing unstated physical, temporal, and cross-modal constraints. It grounds each proposition using specialized perceptual tools and evaluates the resulting confidence traces with soft temporal operators inspired by Linear Temporal Logic. A Significance-Aware Calibration step then aligns verification with human perception by retaining only perceptually significant violations before producing a timestamp- and evidence-grounded report. We conduct extensive experiments across public and complex multi-event benchmarks with the strongest video models to date, demonstrating that FVSCORE outperforms holistic and agent-based evaluators in human correlation while providing highly accurate and interpretable feedback.

Abstract: Recognizing social signals such as interest and hesitation requires interpreting context-dependent behaviors that may co-occur in conversation. We introduce Social Signal Bench, a 47-hour densely annotated benchmark for the evaluation of social signal recognition and engagement, comprising two datasets: Social Signal Bench Video (25 hours), covering ten social signals in audiovisual recordings, and Social Signal Bench Audio (22 hours), covering six signals in audio-only recordings. [80] Know When to Zoom: How Small Is Too Small for Vision-Language Models? cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Siddharth Vohra, Min Xu

TL;DR: 论文研究在线视觉语言模型处理大图时，因服务端缩放或拒收而在模型看到图像前就丢失小细节的问题：8000×6000无人机照片中20像素宽的汽车传到Claude只剩约6像素，OpenAI默认直接拒收。作者用印刷码“视力测试”和601个真实照片小物体问题，量化了4家厂商7个模型在默认设置下可读最小文字尺寸随分辨率的变化（小图≤6像素，7680×4320大图升至10–13像素），并提出分块（tiling）与两步缩放（模型先指出所需区域再放大）两种恢复细节的策略，同时分析各模型的失败模式与何时该触发缩放的规则。

Details

Motivation: 在线VLM服务常在预处理阶段缩小甚至拒收大图，使模型根本看不到小目标细节，用户却无从知晓信息已丢失；论文希望量化“多小算太小”，并找到低成本、可落地的方式把细节找回来。

Result: 将每张图切成最多9块可使Claude和Gemini准确率提升11.1–14.5个百分点，并让GPT-6.1 Sol因避免拒收从50.7%升到83.7%；两步缩放以约40%的图像token在Gemini上追平或超过分块、在Claude上差距仅2.5个点；仅在模型回答不确定后再分块，可用62%–70%的token达到距始终分块1.4个点以内的效果。

Insight: 创新点在于把“细节尺寸（像素）”作为可测量变量，系统揭示服务端预处理带来的隐性信息损失；发现模型自身“不确定”的回答比调用前基于尺寸的检查更可靠的缩放触发器；并揭示Gemini与Claude在细节缺失时失败模式迥异（前者43%–48%自信答错，后者仅5%–9%），最终凝练为agent何时该zoom的五条规则并开源全部数据与代码。

Abstract: Online vision-language models often miss small details in large images, because the service shrinks the image, or rejects it, before the model ever sees it. A car 20 pixels wide in an 8000 by 6000 drone photo reaches Claude about 6 pixels wide, and OpenAI’s default rejects the photo outright. We measure how much detail survives for seven models from four vendors, using an eye exam of printed codes and 601 questions about small objects in real photos. The smallest text a model reads half the time grows from 6 pixels or less on a small image to a predicted 10 to 13 pixels on a 7680 by 4320 photo at default settings. Zooming wins much of the detail back. Splitting each photo into up to nine tiles raises accuracy by 11.1 to 14.5 percentage points for Claude and Gemini, and by avoiding rejections it lifts GPT-6.1 Sol from 50.7% to 83.7%. A two-step zoom, in which the model first names the region it needs, matches or beats tiles on Gemini and comes within 2.5 points of them on Claude, with about 40% of their image tokens. When a detail is lost, Claude and Gemini both say they cannot tell about half the time, but they fail differently in the rest: Gemini gives a confident wrong answer in 43% to 48% of these cases, Claude in only 5% to 9%. A check based on detail size, run before the call, misses most of the gain from tiles on Claude and Gemini, but Claude’s own reply is a reliable trigger: tiling only after an unsure reply comes within 1.4 points of always tiling with 62% to 70% of the image tokens. We turn these findings into five rules for when an agent should zoom, and release the eye exam, every model answer and code at https://siddvoh.com/know-when-to-zoom.

[81] PCRG: Training-Free Pruning and Caching of Reference Tokens for Video Generation cs.CVPDF

Siyuan Wang, Haoran Li, Yi Yue, Tak L. Fung, Quan Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出PCRG，一种无需训练的即插即用方法，用于加速参考到视频的扩散Transformer生成。它利用VAE潜变量中的低层冗余度和VLM条件器中的高层重要性这两个模型已有的信号，将参考token分为剪枝、缓存和保留三类：剪枝token在首步前移出序列且保持RoPE位置不变，缓存token的KV只计算一次并复用，保留token正常处理，从而缩短序列长度而非改变注意力计算方式。

Details

Motivation: 参考token约占全注意力序列一半，且注意力开销随序列长度平方增长，但其中多数token是静态、平滑或与输出无关的，造成大量冗余计算，因此需要在不重新训练的前提下加速参考条件视频生成。

Result: 在已蒸馏至8步并量化为int8的MiniMax-H3上，端到端在V2V-Bench加速1.57倍、公开VACE-Benchmark加速1.49倍，VBench质量仅下降0.24%和0.17%；与TeaCache、Spark、Veda叠加分别达1.86倍、1.82倍、2.15倍，保真度基本不变。

Insight: 创新在于复用模型已计算的VAE低层冗余度与VLM高层重要性信号做三级token分配，并以保持RoPE位置不变的方式剪枝、对缓存token复用KV，实现训练无关、即插即用且可与步缓存和稀疏注意力正交叠加的加速。

Abstract: Reference-to-video diffusion transformers pack the variational autoencoder (VAE) latents of all references and the generated tokens into one full-attention sequence, in which reference tokens make up about half. Because attention cost grows quadratically with sequence length, these tokens take up an even larger share of the attention computation, yet most of them are static, smooth or irrelevant to the output. To accelerate reference-conditioned generation, we propose pruning and caching of reference tokens for video generation (PCRG), a training-free, plug-and-play method that uses two signals the model already computes to assign each reference token to one of three tiers, namely pruned, cached or kept. The first is a low-level redundancy r from VAE latents, since a token whose latent barely changes over time or across its neighbors adds little new content. The second is a high-level importance g from the vision-language-model (VLM) conditioner, where the attention that the prompt and any first or last frames pay to a token tells whether the output needs to read it. Tokens that are not redundant are kept, and redundant tokens are cached if important and pruned otherwise. Pruned tokens leave the sequence before the first step with all rotary position embedding positions unchanged. Cached tokens have their keys and values computed once and reused, and kept tokens are processed as usual. On MiniMax-H3, already distilled to 8 timesteps and quantized to int8, PCRG is 1.57x faster end to end on our V2V-Bench and 1.49x faster on the public VACE-Benchmark, while the quality of VBench drops by only 0.24% and 0.17%, respectively. PCRG shortens the sequence rather than changing how attention is computed or which steps are run, so it also stacks on step caching and sparse attention. On V2V-Bench it reaches 1.86x with TeaCache, 1.82x with Spark at nearly unchanged fidelity and 2.15x with Veda.

[82] Practice in Text, Act in Pixels: Correcting Where Perception Fails cs.CVPDF

Zhongling Xu, Hairong Wang

TL;DR: 论文研究文本界面训练的GUI智能体在通过视觉语言屏幕阅读器执行截图任务时的性能下降问题，提出将少量真实截图预算用于对齐屏幕阅读器，而非直接训练策略。方法通过截图-文本视图配对微调阅读器，使其输出屏幕文本视图，从而在不改变策略的情况下纠正系统性误读。

Details

Motivation: 文本视图训练成本低，但低成本屏幕阅读器在截图上存在系统性误读，导致文本中能完成的任务在截图上失败；论文关注有限真实截图预算应花在哪里以缩小这一差距。

Result: 在模拟移动平台上，将截图预算用于阅读器对齐后，新任务实例的全任务成功率为80.9%，而用于训练策略时为55.6%；相同标注截图下，适配阅读器也优于适配策略，且单个对齐阅读器无需重训即可提升所有尝试的文本训练策略。

Insight: 创新点在于发现阅读器误读具有系统性和元素重复性，并提出从感知源头进行阅读器对齐，而非用噪声模拟或直接适配策略；可借鉴之处是把有限真实数据用于修复可复用的感知模块，从而低成本提升多个下游策略。

Abstract: GUI agents can learn cheaply from a text view of an interface and act on screenshots through a vision-language screen reader. Yet on a simulated mobile platform with a low-cost reader, policies that complete most tasks in text fail on many of the same tasks on screenshots. We ask where a small budget of real screenshots is best spent to close this gap. We find that the reader’s misreads are systematic and recur on the same interface elements. Noise matched to the reader’s error rates, a natural sim-to-real remedy, misses this structure, and in a pre-specified comparison it did not beat training the policy directly on screenshots. We therefore correct the misreads at their source with reader alignment, which fine-tunes the reader on screenshots paired with their text views. The reader learns to output each screen’s text view, as in text practice, and the policy stays unchanged. Spent on the reader, the screenshot budget gives 80.9% full-task success on new task instances, against 55.6% when spent on training the policy. Given the same labeled screenshots, adapting the reader also beats adapting the policy, and a single aligned reader improves every text-trained policy we tried without retraining any of them.

[83] LoopZip: Visual Token Compression for Efficient Looped Vision-Language Models cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Weizhong Huang, Zhicheng Liu, Yan Zhang, Fei Chao, Xiawu Zheng

TL;DR: 论文提出 LoopZip，一种面向循环视觉语言模型（Looped VLM）的免训练视觉 token 压缩算法。它通过在循环骨干前构建紧凑候选池、跨调用平均指令注意力选择 token，并用权重绑定的值校正减少不同循环状态间的输出偏差，从而降低长视觉序列重复处理的计算和显存开销。

Details

Motivation: 循环 VLM 通过复用共享层降低参数和部署负担，但重复处理长视觉序列仍带来显著计算与 GPU 显存成本。现有视觉 token 压缩方法在循环模型中未充分考虑跨调用 token 重要性变化和输出变化，可能丢失后续循环所需信息。

Result: 在两种开源循环 VLM、11 个 benchmark 和 3 种剪枝率上实验。LoopVL-1B 在 50% 剪枝率下保留原模型 97.16% 性能，在 87.5% 剪枝率下超过最强基线 5.36%；在 Hugging Face Transformers、vLLM 和 SGLang 上峰值 GPU 显存降低 58.36%-58.98%，端到端加速 2.01-2.47 倍。

Insight: 创新点在于针对循环 VLM 的跨调用特性设计免训练压缩：先用视觉聚合构建候选池，再平均同一共享注意力层前两次调用的指令注意力以兼顾不同状态所需 token，并通过权重绑定值校正联合拟合共享视觉值调整。客观来看，其价值在于将 token 选择从单次静态重要性扩展到循环调用间的一致性保持，同时兼顾压缩率与部署效率。

Abstract: Looped vision-language models (VLMs) deepen computation by reusing shared layers, approaching larger models’ capabilities with fewer parameters and easing the storage and deployment burden of stacking independent layers. However, repeatedly processing long visual sequences still incurs substantial compute and GPU memory costs. Visual token compression reduces these costs by shortening sequences. Existing methods mainly select original tokens directly or combine selection with aggregation. In looped models, the former may overlook changing token importance across calls and discard information needed later; the latter lack joint consideration of output changes across calls, limiting information preservation. We propose LoopZip, a training-free visual token compression algorithm addressing both issues. Visual aggregation builds a compact candidate pool before the looped backbone, and cross-invocation selection averages instruction attention from the first two existing calls of the same shared attention layer to retain tokens needed by both states. Weight-tied value correction then jointly fits shared visual-value adjustments to both pre-selection layer outputs, reducing output deviations across states. To validate LoopZip’s effectiveness, we conduct extensive experiments covering two open-source looped VLMs, eleven benchmarks, and three pruning ratios. On LoopVL-1B, LoopZip retains 97.16% of the original model’s performance at 50% pruning ratio and exceeds the strongest baseline by 5.36% at 87.5% pruning ratio. Across Hugging Face Transformers, vLLM, and SGLang, LoopVL-1B with LoopZip reduces peak allocated GPU memory by 58.36%-58.98% and achieves 2.01-2.47x end-to-end speedups over the original model.

Details

Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在相机转开再返回时，未观测场景缺乏持续演化的表示，常出现场景冻结、物体丢失或外观改变。论文希望让世界状态在任意视角下都持续演化，而不仅是当前视野内。

Result: 在WRBench的23个公开评估模型中取得最佳整体得分，并在STEVO-Bench上取得最高总得分。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新是用全景潜空间作为始终存在的全局世界状态进行演化，并将全景世界演化与透视视图渲染解耦；构建事件中心360°视频数据集训练图像到全景视频模型。客观看，关键创新在于用全景扩散潜空间作为持续更新的世界记忆，并通过方向受限交叉注意力从低分辨率全景状态中恢复高质量视角细节。

Abstract: Video world models should maintain a world that keeps evolving as the viewpoint changes. Yet when the camera turns away and returns, current models often freeze the unobserved scene, lose objects, or change their appearance, because content outside the view has no representation that keeps changing. We present PanoEvo, which uses a Panoramic Evolver to evolve the world state in the latent space of a panoramic video diffusion model, where every viewing direction is present at every time step, so the scene evolves whether or not it is observed. Existing panoramic video models are not trained to evolve a scene from a single image or the objects within it, so we build PanoEvo-Data, an event-centric dataset from real 360° videos, and train the Panoramic Evolver coarse-to-fine as an image-to-panoramic-video model. Since each view covers only a small part of the low-resolution panorama, directly projected views are blurry and often show low-quality details or structural errors. A Perspective Renderer, built on a perspective video diffusion model, reads this state via latent-space projection and direction-restricted cross-attention to render sharp, high-quality views. PanoEvo achieves the best overall score among 23 publicly evaluated models on WRBench and the highest total score on STEVO-Bench. Code, models, and data will be released.

Abstract: Closed-loop vision-language-action (VLA) policies rely continuously on multi-view cameras, yet existing visual attacks typically hijack sensor streams with 2D patches or alter target object meshes that require retraining when manipulation objects change. We introduce RefCloth, a framework that optimizes one persistent adversarial tablecloth on a frozen source policy and deploys it zero-shot across unseen tasks and target models without modifying task objects or cameras. Throughout manipulation, static third-person and moving wrist views observe this shared world-bound texture under evolving occlusions. To generalize across shifting surface views while bridging the gap between differentiable and native renderers, RefCloth combines bounded texture parameterization with shared surface UV transformations and a same-path clean reference that removes static rendering offsets at initialization. Across four LIBERO suites in native simulation, one frozen texture transfers from OpenVLA-OFT to $π_0$ and $π_{0.5}$, reducing success relative to an unoptimized cloth by 62.50 and 43.75 percentage points on average and far exceeding amplitude- and variation-matched random controls. Abstract: Detailed video captioning requires comprehensive and faithful descriptions of objects, actions, and temporal events. Abstract: Fine-grained questions about long videos ask how many times an action occurs, in what order events unfold, or exactly when a state changes. Agentic models that decide when and where to look promise to find these moments, yet this ability is hard both to measure and to learn. Sparse supporting evidence may establish that an action occurred without establishing its total count; verifying timeline-wide claims requires checking for missed occurrences and double counting. Answer-only supervision, in turn, does not explicitly supervise the search for evidence. We present NexSight, which builds verifiable tasks to both evaluate and train agentic long-video understanding. [89] Visual Harness Scales General VLMs to Medical Images cs.CVPDF

Shaowen Wan, Hanqi Jiang, Zihao Wu, Junhao Chen, Weihang You

TL;DR: 论文提出一种医疗视觉harness，为通用视觉语言模型配备交互式视觉工具和系统的诊断证据管理，使其能迭代探索医学图像、积累并检索诊断证据、根据遗漏与可靠性评估修订答案。该方法在11个任务、4种成像模态上提升了通用VLM表现，并显示强API模型也能从中获得互补增益，主张视觉harness是扩展通用VLM到医学影像的新范式。

Details

Motivation: 通用VLM在多样医学影像任务上直接应用仍受限，缺少交互式图像探索工具和可靠的诊断证据管理机制。论文旨在通过合适的视觉harness，将通用VLM有效扩展到医学影像分析与测量任务。

Result: 在11个任务和4种成像模态上，所提方法使Qwen3.8-27B在所有任务上提升，例如脾测量从63.3%到98.3%、肾脏搜索从57.5%到71.2%、肺结节检测从0.0%到28.2%；GPT-6.1 Sol在肺结节检测上从10.8%提升到69.2%，显示强模型能力与视觉harness的互补收益。

Insight: 创新点在于面向医学影像的交互式视觉工具、无损诊断证据记忆，以及基于潜在遗漏和发现可靠性评估的迭代agentic修订循环。客观看，其价值主要是推理阶段的通用VLM增强框架，而非训练医学专用模型，体现了工具调用、证据管理和迭代自检对医学视觉任务的通用增益。

Abstract: We show that an appropriate visual harness can substantially improve the performance of general vision-language models (VLMs) across diverse medical imaging tasks. We introduce \ours, a medical visual harness that equips general VLMs with interactive visual tools and systematic diagnostic evidence management. With \ours, general VLMs interactively explore medical images and progressively acquire diagnostic evidence, which is preserved in a lossless memory for subsequent retrieval and use. After submitting an initial answer, the VLM receives an assessment of potential omissions and the reliability of identified findings. This assessment guides further image inspection and measurement, followed by answer revision in an iterative agentic loop until sufficient evidence is obtained. Across 11 tasks and four imaging modalities, \ours improves performance on all tasks with Qwen3.8-27B, with notable gains in spleen measurement (63.3% to 98.3%), renal search (57.5% to 71.2%), and pulmonary nodule detection (0.0% to 28.2%). High-capability API-based VLMs achieve even higher performance with \ours. For example, GPT-6.1 Sol improves pulmonary nodule detection from 10.8% to 69.2% with \ours, demonstrating the complementary benefits of stronger model capabilities and an effective medical visual harness. Our results highlight the potential of visual harnesses as a new paradigm for scaling general VLMs to medical imaging tasks.

[90] Pixel is All You Need cs.CVPDF

Jie Chu, Haoting Shi, Fucheng Zhang, Jiawei Liu, Junwei Liao

TL;DR: 论文探讨视觉生成模型能否直接在像素空间中完成符号推理，并训练了无文本通路的纯像素生成模型。模型仅以图像为输入输出，可在画布上读题、写中间步骤并给出答案，表现出随规模幂律提升、逐帧写步骤类似思维链、强化学习后训练增强推理等大语言模型式行为。

Details

Motivation: 现有多模态/图像视频生成器的视觉生成模块无法直接在像素文本上推理，往往依赖隐含文本模块或提示重写器提供答案，论文希望厘清该失败源于像素本身还是训练范式。

Result: 摘要未提及具体benchmark或SOTA对比；报告的现象包括性能随规模呈幂律提升、逐帧写中间步骤显著优于单帧作答、强化学习后训练进一步增强推理，且同一画布支持跨模态推理。

Insight: 创新在于首次系统研究无文本token的纯像素空间生成模型，并展示其具备类LLM的缩放规律、思维链式中间步骤和RL后训练收益；客观看，其以像素作为统一多模态推理媒介的思路具有启发性。

Abstract: Text, images, audio, touch, and even robot actions can, in principle, all be rendered onto a single canvas, suggesting an ambitious goal of unifying all modalities within vision. Language provides the natural first test. To subsume it, a visual model must reason directly over text in pixels. Across a wide range of image, video, and unified multimodal generators, however, we find that their visual generation modules cannot perform symbolic reasoning over text on the canvas. They succeed only when an underlying text module or prompt rewriter already supplies the answer, effectively reducing the generator to a mere renderer, a limitation that post-training does little to resolve. To test whether this failure stems from pixels themselves or from current training paradigms, we train a family of purely pixel-space generative models from scratch without any text pathway. With images as their sole input and output, these models read questions, write intermediate steps, and produce answers directly on the canvas. Remarkably, they exhibit hallmark behaviors of large language models. Performance improves predictably with scale following a power law. Writing intermediate steps frame by frame substantially outperforms single-frame answering, echoing chain-of-thought, while reinforcement-learning post-training further strengthens reasoning. Moreover, sharing one canvas allows the same model to reason across modalities, a capability that text-only language models lack. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of these phenomena in purely pixel-space generative models free of text tokens.

[91] Keeping Parts Honest: Isolated Part Graphs for Faithful Vision Transformer Explanations cs.CVPDF

Bernard Tomczyk, Gail Collyer-Hoar, Bryan Williams

TL;DR: 论文指出ViT中自注意力会跨patch混合信息，导致基于部件/原型的解释所依据的区域特征吸收全局上下文，从而无法忠实反映决策。作者提出一种将图像表示为“孤立部件图”的可解释分类器：每个部件仅由自身patch编码，并通过层次化子部件细化；部件间边由部件对计算；最终将图像图与每类学习的原型图匹配，使预测和解释直接由节点与边相似度构成，从结构上保证解释忠实性。

Details

Motivation: 现有可解释ViT分类器依赖注意力造成的证据泄漏：孤立编码解释区域时准确率会崩溃，说明部件级证据并不忠实。论文旨在解决解释中的证据泄漏问题，使解释区域真正对应模型决策所用证据。

Result: 在11个细粒度与通用物体基准上，该方法超过了依赖证据泄漏的现有可解释方法，同时提供能反映模型实际使用证据的解释；摘要未给出具体数值，也未宣称绝对SOTA。

Insight: 创新点在于用孤立部件编码阻断ViT跨区域信息泄漏，并结合无标注层次化部件发现、部件对边证据和类原型图匹配，使预测成为节点与边相似度的直接组合，从而“按构造”保证解释忠实。客观看，其价值主要是将可解释性约束嵌入模型结构与匹配机制，而非仅对事后解释做正则。

Abstract: Part and prototype-based explanations for Vision Transformers (ViTs) present a decision as evidence from individual image regions. However, self-attention mixes information across patches, so part-level features absorb context from the whole image and consequently fail to faithfully reflect the decision. We show that existing interpretable ViT classifiers depend heavily on this leakage: when each explained region is encoded in isolation, their accuracy collapses, and retraining them under isolation recovers only part of the lost accuracy. We propose a classifier that represents each image as a graph of isolated parts. Each part is encoded only from its own patches by a ViT whose last blocks are fine-tuned under isolation, preventing evidence leakage from elsewhere in the image. Parts are discovered without annotation and refined hierarchically through their sub-parts, making them more consistent and stable across images than those of competing methods. Each edge is computed from part pairs, and recovers evidence that attention previously shared across the object without affecting the parts. Matching the image graph to a learned prototype graph per class makes the prediction a direct combination of node and edge similarities, each computed only from the patches it is credited to, so every explanation is faithful by construction. Across 11 fine-grained and general-object benchmarks, our method exceeds the accuracy of existing explainable methods that rely on leakage, while providing explanations that reflect what the model actually uses.

[92] Beyond Co-Generation: Rethinking Jointness in Joint Audio-Video Generation cs.CV | eess.ASPDF

Jihoo Jung, Ji-Hoon Kim, Suyeon Lee, Gia Thinh Pham, Joon Son Chung

TL;DR: 论文研究联合文本到音视频生成中真正的音视频协调问题，提出“内在联合性”概念，即在提示语义欠指定时音视频能否产生一致的具体实现。作者用语义欠指定提示评测四个近期模型，发现其内在联合性有限，并提出基于表示对齐的后训练方法JointREPA，同时构建UNSAIDBench基准来评估该能力。

Details

Motivation: 现有T2AV生成中，详细文本提示难以区分音视频一致性是来自真正的跨模态协调，还是仅来自各自独立遵循文本。论文希望隔离并评估真正的音视频语义协调能力。

Result: 实验覆盖两个不同模型和三个基准（包括UNSAIDBench），表明JointREPA能够持续提升音视频语义一致性；摘要未给出具体数值或SOTA结论。

Insight: 创新点在于提出“内在联合性”这一评估视角，用语义欠指定提示暴露模型跨模态协调不足；并提出简单的表示对齐后训练方法JointREPA及相应基准UNSAIDBench。

Abstract: Our aim is to advance the understanding of genuine audio-visual coordination in joint text-to-audio-video (T2AV) generation, with particular emphasis on semantic consistency. Evaluating audio-visual consistency in T2AV generation is nontrivial, as detailed text prompts obscure whether such consistency stems from genuine cross-modal coordination or merely from independent text following. To isolate the former, we introduce intrinsic jointness: the ability of audio and video to produce the same realization for details left underspecified by the prompt. Using semantically underspecified prompts (e.g., “musical instrument playing” rather than “electric guitar playing”), we investigate four recent models and reveal their limited intrinsic jointness. To address this limitation, we propose JointREPA, a simple representation alignment-based post-training method to facilitate cross-modal coordination. Since existing benchmarks use semantically detailed prompts, we introduce UNSAIDBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate intrinsic jointness under semantic underspecification. Experiments across two distinct models and three benchmarks, including UNSAIDBench, demonstrate that JointREPA consistently improves semantic consistency.

[93] LOCUS: Causal Support Windows for Attribute Control in Autoregressive Image Generation cs.CVPDF

Zijun Huang

TL;DR: 论文研究自回归图像生成中多属性约束难以同时满足的问题，提出因果支持窗口（causal support windows）来刻画不同属性对图像 token 序列中不同位置干预的响应规律。基于此提出 LOCUS，在测试时诊断失败属性，并将潜变量优化路由到对应的支持窗口。实验表明该方法在冻结生成器的情况下显著提升 Janus-Pro 等模型的属性控制和失败修复能力。

Details

Motivation: 统一自回归图像生成器难以在单个提示中同时满足多个属性约束；虽然属性信息可从图像 token 潜变量中解码，但测试时直接编辑潜变量仍很困难，固定属性方向难以覆盖冲突属性。

Result: 在 GenEval++ 上，支持窗口路由比晚窗口宏平均准确率高 23.6 个百分点；冻结生成器时，LOCUS 将 Janus-Pro 的 GenEval++ 总体准确率从 24.6% 提升到 46.8%，多属性标签从 19.5% 提升到 40.5%。在 GenEval 上，单次 rollout 修复 48.1% 的失败生成，高于前缀优化的 37.4%，且平均少用 14% 的轮次。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点是提出因果支持剖面，发现结构属性（数量、位置）更依赖早期窄窗口，外观属性（颜色、尺寸、材质）更依赖后期宽窗口，并据此将测试时潜变量优化路由到属性对应的支持窗口。客观来看，其创新在于把因果干预位置分析与免训练测试时优化结合，用属性类型和空间内容指导编辑位置，从而更高效地解决多属性冲突。

Abstract: Unified autoregressive (AR) image generators struggle to satisfy multiple attribute constraints in a single prompt. Although these attributes are decodable from image-token latents, editing those latents at test time remains difficult. Adding a fixed attribute direction to the prefix can reinforce a requested attribute but rarely overrides a conflicting one, such as turning a car red when the prompt specifies blue. We investigate how the effect of an intervention depends on where it acts in the image-token sequence. We introduce the causal support profile, which measures, for each attribute, how often patching image-token states within a given window flips the rendered attribute. Structural attributes (count and position) are most responsive in early, narrow windows, while appearance attributes (color, size, and material) respond over later, broader windows. A budget-matched comparison of early and middle windows shows that the effect of window location depends on the target attribute, and two-dimensional support maps show that the responsive regions track spatial content. Based on these profiles, we propose LOCUS (Latent Optimization in Causal Support windows), which diagnoses which attribute failed and routes test-time latent optimization to its corresponding support window. With the generator, optimizer, reward, verifier, and token budget held fixed, support-routed windows outperform late windows by 23.6 percentage points in GenEval++ macro accuracy. With the generator frozen, LOCUS raises Janus-Pro’s GenEval++ accuracy from 24.6% to 46.8% overall and from 19.5% to 40.5% on multi-attribute tags. On GenEval, LOCUS repairs 48.1% of failed generations in one rollout, compared with 37.4% for prefix optimization, and needs 14% fewer rounds on average to succeed.

Details

Motivation: 现有图像生成模型能生成照片级真实细节，但其随机细节合成会导致视频时间不一致；现有视频复原方法虽提升时序连贯性，却常牺牲视觉保真度。论文旨在弥合图像级质量与视频级稳定性之间的差距。

Result: 摘要称大量实验表明，该方法在视觉质量、细节丰富度和时间稳定性上显著优于现有视频复原方法，但未给出具体benchmark或定量指标。

Insight: 创新点在于从图像扩散先验中实现时间一致的细节合成，具体包括抑制低频随机性的噪声能量重平衡、沿物体运动传播细节的运动对齐噪声变换、缓解编码器时间不确定性的自监督去噪编码器，以及建模跨帧依赖的解耦时间注意力。

Abstract: Recent image generation models produce photo-realistic results with rich details, but transferring their powerful priors to video restoration remains difficult due to temporal inconsistency from stochastic detail synthesis. Existing video restoration methods improve temporal coherence but often sacrifice visual fidelity, leaving a gap between image-level quality and video-level stability. We propose a generative video restoration method that bridges this gap by enabling temporally consistent detail synthesis from image generation priors. Our method combines noise energy rebalancing to suppress structure-disturbing low-frequency stochasticity, motion-aligned noise warping to propagate details along object motion, a self-supervised denoising encoder to mitigate encoder-induced temporal uncertainty, and decoupled temporal attention to model cross-frame dependencies. Together, these components preserve fidelity, enhance fine-grained realism, and maintain temporal consistency. Extensive experiments show that our method substantially outperforms existing video restoration approaches in visual quality, detail richness, and temporal stability.

[95] SlideJev: Order-Stable and Tissue-Grounded Jev Decisions for Whole-Slide Pathology cs.CVPDF

Lei Wu, Jiashuai Liu, Di Zhang, Chunze Yang, Jiusong Ge

TL;DR: 论文提出 SlideJev，用于全切片病理视觉问答（VQA），将候选答案验证与组织证据 grounding 分开处理。方法采用 Jev 评分，用原语言模型头中支持与拒绝的对数似然差给候选打分，并以相同格式、无选项位置标签的并行提示计算后归一化选择答案；同时通过候选条件化阅读与比较，在解码器各层门控更新视觉 token。在三个全切片病理 VQA 基准上取得最高平均总体准确率，并在四种选项顺序下达到 100% 答案一致性。

Details

Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在全切片病理 VQA 中过度依赖语言先验，答案对候选选项顺序敏感，并可能通过绕过组织证据的捷径作答。论文旨在实现顺序稳定且基于组织证据的判断。

Result: 在三个全切片病理 VQA 基准上，SlideJev 取得最高平均总体准确率，并在四种选项顺序下实现 100% 答案一致性。图像控制实验显示，在三个数据集上，正确图像相对错配图像和无图像输入带来最大平均准确率增益。

Insight: 创新点在于将候选答案验证与视觉证据学习连接起来：用支持/拒绝对数似然差进行并行、无位置标签的候选评分以提升顺序稳定性，并用候选条件化的阅读与比较门控更新视觉 token，使判断 grounded 到组织证据；冻结主干并仅训练视觉模块也较有借鉴意义。

Abstract: Whole-slide visual question answering (VQA) offers a natural-language interface to pathology, allowing diagnostic and phenotypic questions to be answered directly from tissue slides. However, current vision-language models (VLMs) rely heavily on linguistic priors: their answers are sensitive to the order of the candidate answers, and can be reached through shortcuts that bypass the tissue evidence. To address these challenges, we introduce SlideJev, a framework that separately verifies candidate answers against the slide and learns to ground these judgments in tissue evidence. To connect parallel candidate evaluation with visual evidence learning, we adopt Jev and score each candidate by the difference between support and rejection log-likelihoods from the original language-model head. We compute these scores in parallel using separate prompts with the same format and no option-position labels, then normalize them for answer selection. To ground these scores in tissue evidence, candidate-conditioned reading and comparison guide gated updates to visual tokens across decoder layers. With the backbone frozen, we train these visual modules through the same scores using question-answer and paired-image supervision. Across three whole-slide pathology VQA benchmarks, SlideJev achieves the highest mean overall accuracy and 100% answer consistency across four option orders. Image-control experiments further show that SlideJev achieves the largest average accuracy gains from correct images over mismatched and image-free inputs across the three datasets. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/leowu-ai/SlideJev.

[96] Learning Agent-Centric Vision-Language-Action World Models for Autonomous Driving cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Guoqing Wang, Pin Tang, Xiangxuan Ren, Chao Ma

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentWorld，一种以智能体为中心的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）世界模型，用于自动驾驶。它摒弃像素级未来帧预测，转而使用离散的智能体token进行推理和轨迹规划，每个token动态地关联周围智能体的状态（如语义、几何、运动、交互）。通过显式几何接地、推理导向的监督微调和智能体导向的强化学习训练，实验表明该模型提升了规划能力并产生可解释的世界状态。

Details

Motivation: 现有世界VLA模型通常生成像素级未来帧，包含大量与驾驶无关的外观细节，只有少量token直接编码规划相关因素（如智能体几何、运动、交互），导致性能瓶颈。论文旨在探索支持规划的世界模型应推理何种世界状态，提出应聚焦于紧凑、结构化的智能体中心潜在状态。

Result: 摘要提到在多样驾驶场景下的开放环和闭环实验表明，AgentWorld增强了规划能力，充分利用视觉证据和智能体动态，并产生可解释的智能体中心世界状态。但未提供具体量化结果或对比基准。

Insight: 创新点在于提出以智能体为中心的VLA世界模型，使用离散智能体token联合推理和规划，每个token动态接地到周围智能体并关联其多模态状态；训练过程结合显式几何接地、监督微调和强化学习。客观分析认为，该工作抓住了像素级预测中信息冗余的痛点，转向紧凑结构化的潜在表示，有望提升规划效率和可解释性。

Abstract: Recent progress in integrating vision-language-action (VLA) models with world modeling has significantly advanced end-to-end autonomous driving by allowing systems to reason about imagined futures instead of merely reacting to current observations. However, most world VLA models generate future frames at the pixel level with plenty of dense visual tokens. Although such generated futures can be visually plausible, they preserve many driving-irrelevant appearance details, i.e., only a small portion of the tokens directly encode critical planning-relevant factors, such as agent geometry, motion dynamics, and interactions. This introduces a significant performance bottleneck. An important question thus arises: what world state should a world VLA model reason over to support planning beyond pixel-level prediction? We argue that planning-oriented world modeling should focus on a compact and structured agent-centric latent state that captures the interactions between objects in the driving scene over time. As such, we introduce AgentWorld, an agent-centric VLA world model that uses discrete agent tokens jointly for reasoning and trajectory planning. Each token is dynamically grounded to a surrounding agent associated with its object’s state, such as semantic identity, 3D geometry, motion, and interaction context. AgentWorld learns these tokens through three key processes: explicit geometric grounding, reasoning-oriented supervised fine-tuning, and agent-oriented reinforcement learning. Extensive open-loop and closed-loop experiments under diverse driving scenarios show that AgentWorld enhances planning capabilities, fully exploits visual evidence and agent dynamics, and yields interpretable agent-centric world states.

Abstract: Ensembles of diverse Large Vision Language Models have demonstrated performance beyond that of single models across multiple datasets and tasks. However, the necessary aggregation process usually relies on training, parameter fitting, or complex task-specific pipelines. Furthermore, evaluation is limited, making a fair comparison difficult. We create a unified pipeline where we perform candidate response selection: we build an ensemble by selecting the best answer from a set provided by different models. We comprehensively evaluate across 34 different means of confidence estimation, 24 ensemble configurations, 20 datasets, and two prompt specifications. We show that simple training and parameter-free approaches can be sufficient, even for ensembles of different architectures. These simple self-assessment approaches rely solely on each constituent model’s confidence, without explicit calibration, yet are able to perform better than said constituent models. They are also competitive with more involved alternatives on standard metrics such as accuracy, including on open-ended problems. However, we also discover that dataset, ensemble composition, and prompt wording are key confounding variables insufficiently explored by prior work that significantly affect ensemble performance, such that the ensemble can perform worse than its best constituent model. [105] When a Larger Guidance Scale Repairs Text-to-Video Failures and When It Does Not cs.CVPDF

Taewon Kang, Matthias Zwicker

TL;DR: 论文系统测量了文本到视频扩散模型中“提高无分类器引导尺度（CFG scale）”这一常见修复手段何时有效。作者在 Mochi 上发现：提高引导尺度能修复罕见组合失败，甚至优于重新生成；但对否定语义失败修复更差，对必须发生在视频后段的事件几乎无效。论文提出可用 prompt 条件和一个从去噪前四分之一阶段读取的廉价“demand”分数提前判断是否值得提高引导尺度。

Details

Motivation: 文本到视频模型常会忽略 prompt 的一部分，用户通常首先尝试提高 CFG scale；论文关注这一修复策略并非普遍有效，旨在刻画其适用边界并给出可提前判断的规则。

Result: 在 Mochi 上的测量显示：提高引导尺度对罕见组合（如“snowy beach”）的修复优于重新生成；对否定语义（如“no vehicles”）修复失败数少于重新生成；对特定时间后段事件（如海浪冲平沙堡）无改善。结合 prompt 中无否定、无特定时间事件以及 demand 超过拟合阈值时，修复率明显更高。

Insight: 创新点在于将“是否提高引导尺度”转化为可预测的条件判断：利用去噪早期、视频尚未生成时的 demand 分数衡量去噪器对缺失 prompt 部分的反应，并结合否定和时间事件条件形成简短决策规则，帮助用户在重新生成与调参之间做选择。

Abstract: When a text-to-video diffusion model ignores part of a prompt, the usual first fix is to raise the classifier-free guidance scale. This paper is a measurement study of when that fix works. On Mochi, the same increase repairs failures on rare compositions (“a snowy beach”) beyond what generating again does, does not repair negations (“a highway with no vehicles”), where it fixes fewer failures than generating again does, and changes nothing for events that must happen late in a clip (“a sandcastle that a wave washes flat”). Which of these happens can be known in advance, from the prompt and one inexpensive score. The score, which we call demand, measures how strongly the denoiser reacts to the part of the prompt that the failed video leaves out, and it is read from the first quarter of denoising, before any video exists. Our main finding is a condition: raising the scale is effective only when the prompt contains no negation and no event tied to a specific time, and when demand is sufficiently high, with recovery clearly higher above a fitted threshold than below it. Published corrections for the three failure types, used as fixed probes, behave as this account predicts. A short decision rule follows: before generating again, a user can tell whether the most common fix is worth trying, and what to use when it is not.

Abstract: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) enables a single student to absorb the skills of several domain teachers by learning from its own generated responses. We present Multimodal Model Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation Reasoner (M3OPD-Reasoner), a fully open recipe for post-training vision-language models (VLMs) over three reasoning domains: image STEM, image perception, and video. On these data, we compare post-training recipes that start from Qwen3.5-9B: training one model on the mixed data of all domains (MixSFT and MixRL), and training one teacher per domain with Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), directly or after an SFT warm-up, and distilling the teachers into one student with MOPD. We evaluate on 21 image STEM, image perception, and video reasoning benchmarks after we decontaminate the training data against them. The comparison establishes MOPD of teachers trained with GSPO directly from Qwen3.5-9B as the best recipe: the student closely matches each teacher on its own domain and is on average above the mixed-training baselines on the same prompts. Since MOPD already merges the domain capabilities that MixRL would otherwise have to learn, its student is a stronger starting point for RL, and we ask whether the same data can carry the model further from there: we add MixRL on top of the MOPD student, on the same prompts and with the same settings, which, to our knowledge, no prior VLM recipe has studied. The same prompts still improve the MOPD student, with no collapse, and give our main model, M3OPD-Reasoner-9B.
