[107] When Anchors Shift the Target: Advantage-Gated Adaptation for Diffusion Reinforcement Learning cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Pu Cao, Min Zhou, Ying Shu, Tiezheng Ge, Qing Song

TL;DR: 论文研究扩散与流匹配模型在前向过程强化学习中的目标锚点冲突：为稳定负优势训练而引入的移动锚点会扭曲正样本回归目标。作者提出优势门控自适应（AGA），将正负样本的更新角色解耦，并推导出无参数门控。实验在组合生成、文本渲染和偏好对齐上表明AGA能改善奖励与质量的权衡。

Details

Motivation: 负优势会带来凹更新和无界排斥梯度，现有方法依赖移动策略副本的缓存预测作为锚点稳定训练；但这些锚点同时改变正反馈应拟合的目标，形成锚点耦合冲突。论文希望在保留正回归目标的同时处理负优势的负曲率问题。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值或SOTA声明；在组合生成、文本渲染和偏好对齐任务上，实验显示移动正目标会降低视觉质量并提高对策略副本调度的敏感性，而AGA能持续改善奖励-质量权衡。

Insight: 论文将冲突形式化为锚点耦合，并论证仿射移动锚规则无法同时消除负曲率与保留所有正回归目标；其创新在于正样本保持标准目标拟合，负样本采用凹目标在移动策略预测处的一阶切线以保留局部排斥方向，且在二次锚族下唯一确定门控ω(a)=max(-a,0)。此外，移动副本被重新解释为局部线性化工具，长期行为保持交由冻结预训练参考并按运行CFG尺度正则，提供隐式奖励视角。

Abstract: Forward-process reinforcement learning adapts diffusion and flow-matching models by re-noising rewarded generations into regression targets. Because negative advantages induce concave updates with unbounded repulsive gradients, existing methods stabilize training using cached predictions from a moving policy copy, yet these anchors also alter what positive feedback teaches the model to fit. We formalize this conflict as anchor coupling: after separating frozen-reference regularizers, affine moving-anchor rules cannot eliminate negative curvature while preserving every positive regression target. We propose Advantage-Gated Adaptation (AGA) to decouple these roles. Positive samples retain standard target fitting, whose self-limiting update vanishes at the observed target independently of the moving anchor. Negative samples instead use the first-order tangent of the concave objective at the moving-policy prediction, preserving the local repulsion direction with a prediction-independent gradient. Within the quadratic-anchor family, these requirements uniquely determine the parameter-free gate $ω(a)=\max(-a,0)$. Because the moving copy provides local linearization rather than retention, AGA assigns long-horizon behavior preservation to a frozen pretrained reference regularized at its operating classifier-free guidance scale, interpreted through an implicit-reward lens. Across compositional generation, text rendering, and preference alignment, our experiments show that displacing positive targets degrades visual quality and heightens sensitivity to policy-copy schedules, whereas AGA consistently improves the reward–quality trade-off.

Abstract: Event-based cameras provide high temporal resolution, low power consumption, and a high dynamic range, but progress in event-based object detection remains constrained by the scarcity of annotated training data. Events are generated from per-pixel log-luminance differences between consecutive frames, with contrast thresholds sampled independently from a normal distribution to emulate the fabrication-induced threshold mismatch of real sensors. Abstract: Audio-visual question answering on lightweight user devices requires balancing speed, memory usage, privacy, and communication. One approach is to run audio-visual encoders on a server and a lightweight language decoder on the user device, keeping questions local while offloading media encoding. However, transferring and processing the full multimodal representation remains costly. We propose a training-free, query-agnostic token pruning framework that constructs a compact, reusable memory before transmission. We define video spatio-temporal blocks and aligned audio windows as players and estimate their importance through Monte Carlo Shapley valuation. We use an embedding coverage objective that combines query-independent utility with cross-modal redundancy calibration and rewards unimodal diversity through marginal coverage gains. Chengqian Ma, Yuanze Zeng, Hongyu Wang, Mingfeng Lin, YiCheng Xiao

TL;DR: 论文提出VIVID，用于视频多模态大模型在紧帧预算下的视频问答，突破现有模型将视频理解退化为静态多图推理的问题。方法通过关键帧与帧间Patch Diff保留局部运动变化，结合ADKP关键帧规划、DRCM双路变化记忆、主动运动ROI和实体状态链构建动态表示。仅增加0.22%可训练参数到冻结27B骨干，即以少量训练样本取得强结果。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频MLLM常丢弃未选帧并独立编码关键帧，导致空间上小运动目标被全帧池化稀释，时间上丢帧或平均token抹除方向、速度和顺序信息。论文希望在不增加大量帧或token的情况下保留局部帧间变化和事件时序。

Result: VIVID-Small仅用50个示例、约1.5 GPU小时训练，在8083个问题上总体准确率超过全部11个基线，并仅次于使用3600个示例的VIVID-Large；VIVID-Large在MotionBench、Video-MME和TempCompass上领先。

Insight: 创新点包括用帧间Patch Diff建模局部变化、ADKP自适应选择锚点、DRCM形成零额外anchor token的动态token，以及相机稳定运动ROI和时序实体状态链。客观看，其价值在于将帧间差异与文本实体动态结合，以较低训练成本缓解静态多图推理和运动信息丢失问题。

Abstract: Video question answering under tight frame budgets requires preserving localized motion and event order, yet current video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) reduce comprehension to static multi-image reasoning by discarding unselected frames and encoding keyframes independently: (1) Spatially, whole-frame pooling dilutes small moving targets into static backgrounds; (2) Temporally, dropping intermediate frames or averaging tokens erases inter-frame motion, confusing direction, speed, and order. We propose VIVID (Visual Inter-frame Variations and Individual Dynamics), which moves beyond static snapshots like human vision: keyframes capture core states while inter-frame Patch Diffs preserve neighboring changes within $K_f$ keyframes. Autonomous Delta-Driven Keyframe Planning (ADKP) selects anchors from a $3\text{ fps}$ pool and feeds surrounding patch differences into Dual-Road Change Memory (DRCM), forming Dynamic Tokens at zero extra anchor tokens alongside a camera-stabilized Active Motion Region of Interest (ROI) and chronological Entity State Chains. Adding $0.22%$ trainable parameters to a frozen $27\text{B}$ backbone, VIVID-Small ($50$ examples, $\sim 1.5$ GPU hours) beats all eleven baselines in overall accuracy across $8,083$ questions and trails only VIVID-Large ($3,600$ examples), which leads on MotionBench, Video-MME, and TempCompass.

[115] Say Less, Hallucinate Less? Diagnosing and Restoring Object Coverage in Preference-Optimized Vision-Language Models cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Hansang Lee

TL;DR: 论文诊断偏好优化（PO）在降低大视觉语言模型物体幻觉的同时会降低物体覆盖：四个LLaVA-1.5-7B的PO检查点命名的物体类别更少，CHAIR上集合级物体F1也无显著提升。作者用EOS logit bias做召回控制的早停分析，发现早停可复现相当部分PO增益，并提出无需训练的base-guided object-category restoration，将基础模型高置信的独有类别追加到PO描述中，以恢复召回并保持较低幻觉。

Details

Motivation: PO是减少大视觉语言模型物体幻觉的标准方法，但论文发现它可能牺牲物体覆盖和召回；因此需要厘清PO的幻觉降低有多少只是生成更短/早停带来的，并恢复被PO丢失的物体类别。

Result: 在COCO/CHAIR上，正EOS偏置早停可复现三个检查点58%至86%的CHAIRi增益；负EOS偏置仅恢复2%至9%的丢失CHAIR召回，94%至96%的新增物体提及是重复类别。所提方法在留出COCO集上使三个检查点召回接近基础模型1个百分点内，且不同物体幻觉率低于基础模型；相对召回匹配的早停曲线，在RLAIF-V及CHAIR-DPO的部分召回设置下仍有效。

Insight: 创新点在于揭示PO降低幻觉与降低物体覆盖的耦合，提出用EOS偏置进行召回受控诊断，并无需训练地利用基础模型独有高置信类别做恢复。客观看，核心贡献是诊断/评测协议和轻量后处理，而非新的PO训练目标。

Abstract: Preference optimization (PO) is the standard way to reduce object hallucination in large vision-language models. We find that four public PO checkpoints of LLaVA-1.5-7B also lower object coverage. They name fewer object categories, and their lower hallucination rates bring no significant gain in set-level object F1 on CHAIR. We therefore ask how much of the PO gain the base model can reproduce by stopping earlier. A positive end-of-sequence (EOS) logit bias lets the base model reach the recall of each PO model, and this early stopping alone reproduces 58 to 86 percent of the CHAIRi gain of three checkpoints on two disjoint COCO sets. Writing longer does not bring the lost categories back. Under greedy decoding, a negative EOS bias recovers only 2 to 9 percent of the lost CHAIR recall of these checkpoints, and 94 to 96 percent of the added object mentions repeat categories already named. On CHAIR, the base caption of the same image still names about half of the missed object categories. We therefore propose base-guided object-category restoration, which appends high-confidence base-only categories to the PO caption without training. With thresholds fixed on a development set, it brings three checkpoints within one point of the base recall on a held-out COCO set with a lower distinct-object hallucination rate than the base model. Against the recall-matched base stopping curve, its gain holds for RLAIF-V near the base recall and for RLAIF-V and CHAIR-DPO at an intermediate recall. We will release the code of the recall-controlled evaluation.

[116] Any2Building: Unifying Point Clouds and Images for Building Reconstruction via Shared Spatial Queries cs.CVPDF

Hongye Hou, Zhuoxiao Li, Liu Xiangyu, Tengxi Wang, Jing Ou

TL;DR: 论文提出Any2Building，一个统一的LoD2建筑重建框架，可从部分点云、俯视图像或二者联合输入中恢复建筑结构，并支持可选的粗几何先验。其核心是占据感知的共享空间查询表示，将多模态观测组织到共同3D网格中，并由完整建筑表面体素监督共享生成器学习跨任务结构先验。论文还构建了约100万配对样本、覆盖18个城市的BuildingWorld-Image数据集，并通过体素条件表面生成与低多边形重网格化流程输出紧凑LoD2模型。

Details

Motivation: 有限观测下的结构化建筑重建对3D城市建模和数字孪生很重要，但部分点云与俯视图像各自只提供不完整的几何或视觉线索。论文希望统一对齐几何与视觉信息，并支持从任一模态或双模态重建建筑，同时恢复缺失结构。

Result: 在多个benchmark上，Any2Building在单模态和多模态点云补全、单图像建筑生成以及先验辅助生成任务中，几何重建精度优于现有方法；在点云输入模态退化时仍能保持细节和鲁棒恢复。

Insight: 创新点在于用占据感知的共享空间查询在统一3D网格中连接多模态观测与目标几何，使共享生成器跨任务学习结构先验并指导缺失结构恢复。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是将多模态输入、多种重建任务和大规模配对数据集统一到共享空间表示与体素监督框架中。

Abstract: Abstracting structured buildings from limited observations is essential for 3D urban modeling and digital twins. Partial point clouds and overhead images of the same building provide incomplete 3D surface measurements and visual cues to its footprint, roof geometry, and rooftop structures, respectively. This motivates a unified framework that aligns geometric and visual cues in 3D and reconstructs buildings from either or both modalities. Building on this connection, we present Any2Building, a unified model for Level of Detail 2 (LoD2) building reconstruction from partial point clouds, overhead images, or both, with optional coarse geometric priors. At its core is an occupancy-grounded shared spatial queries representation that organizes available observations within a common 3D grid. Supervised by complete building surface voxels, these queries connect observational evidence to target geometry, enabling a shared generator to learn structural priors across tasks and providing spatial guidance for recovering missing structure. A trained voxel conditional surface-generation and low-poly remeshing pipeline then converts the predicted structure into compact LoD2 building models. To support this framework, we introduce BuildingWorld-Image, comprising approximately one million paired samples of partial point clouds, overhead images, and LoD2 building models across 18 cities. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that Any2Building outperforms existing methods in geometric reconstruction accuracy across unimodal and multimodal point-cloud completion, single-image building generation, and prior-assisted generation, while maintaining detailed and robust recovery when point cloud input modality is degraded.

Vincenzo Marcianò, Xiaoming Zhang, Michela Antonelli, Sebastien Ourselin, Maria A Zuluaga

TL;DR: 论文提出 GRACE，一种检索增强的医学图像分割掩码精修器。它利用已验证先验病例构成共享知识库，为输入掩码提供上下文和形状先验，从而用单个模型跨多器官、多模态修正分割残差。方法在多器官语料上训练一次，并通过冻结模型下的索引扩展适配未见解剖结构。

Details

Motivation: 现有自动化分割残差修正通常按解剖结构分别建模，导致修正器数量随分割目标数量增长；论文希望用通用、可复用的检索条件精修器替代解剖结构专用的修正模型。

Result: 在 18 个 CT/MRI/超声公开数据集、7 个弱中强上游分割器和 9 种精修配置上评估。弱分割器输入 DSC 为 0.386 时，GRACE 最多提升约 +0.35 DSC；强分割器输入 DSC 为 0.932 时平均变化较小。对训练和索引中均未出现的成像模态迁移仅部分有效且依赖解剖结构。

Insight: 创新点在于用检索增强方式构建通用掩码精修器，通过冻结多模态编码器上的轻量形状感知投影头，将嵌入相似度映射到掩码形状一致性，使知识库返回可作为形状先验的病例；同时支持冻结模型下的无参数索引扩展，避免为每个解剖结构训练专用修正器。

Abstract: Automated segmentation is routinely used as the first step in quantitative image-based clinical workflows, where predicted masks are subsequently reviewed and corrected by human operators. Automated correction of the residual structural errors is currently addressed one anatomy at a time, so the number of trained correctors scales with the number of segmentation targets. We present GRACE, a retrieval-augmented refiner that edits an input mask under context drawn from a shared knowledge base of verified prior cases. The refiner is trained once on a multi-organ corpus spanning compact organs, cavities, branching vessels, and tumour lesions. In the held-out dataset-transfer evaluation, three held-out datasets query a fixed knowledge base built from training data only. A separate leave-one-anatomy-out experiment tests parameter-free index extension: verified cases of an unseen solid organ enter the knowledge base, and the refiner stays frozen. A light shape-aware projection head on a frozen multimodal encoder maps embedding similarity to the shape agreement of the masks, so the knowledge base returns cases that the refiner can use as a shape prior. We evaluate GRACE on 18 public datasets spanning CT, MRI and ultrasound, using nine refinement configurations and seven upstream segmentors that span weak, mid, and strong operating regimes. We report each change with the DSC of its input mask. On the weak tier, the input DSC is 0.386 and GRACE adds up to +0.35 DSC. On a strong segmentor, the input DSC is 0.932 and the mean change is small. Transfer to an imaging modality that is absent from training and from the index is partial and depends on the anatomy, and remains an open research direction. Our results suggest retrieval-conditioned refinement is a practical alternative to anatomy-specific correction models. Code: https://github.com/robustml-eurecom/grace

[118] Reading Scene States: Uncovering Safety Failures in Vision-Language Models cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Tongxi Wu, Yuyang Cheng, Jiacheng Xian, Menglin Yang, Kai Chen

TL;DR: 论文揭示视觉语言模型存在“场景状态安全漏洞”：即使没有显式请求有害目标，模型也会通过解释视觉事件提供有害协助。作者构建SceneSafetyBench（375个图像-问题对，覆盖初始、中间、最终场景状态和五种视觉呈现），在8个VLM上取得45.6%–72.3%攻击成功率。进一步提出S3TA，将有害目标转化为场景状态、独立查询图像-问题对并组装回答，在MM-SafetyBench七个类别、8个开源和商用VLM上平均ASR达71.2%，超过最强基线6.1个百分点。

Details

Motivation: 现有VLM安全主要关注显式有害输入筛查，但模型可能通过场景 grounded 推理自行推断出有害目标并提供协助。论文旨在识别并系统化这种场景状态安全漏洞，说明仅过滤输入内容不足以保证安全。

Result: SceneSafetyBench在8个VLM上诱导有害协助的ASR为45.6%–72.3%；S3TA在MM-SafetyBench七个类别和8个模型上平均ASR为71.2%，比最强基线高6.1个百分点，且文本过滤和视觉审核提供的保护有限。

Insight: 论文的创新在于形式化“场景状态安全漏洞”并构建对应基准，同时从层级安全信号角度分析其与QR相关图像的相似性、较低的后层读出和不同生成动态。方法上，S3TA将有害目标分解为场景状态并独立查询后组装，把安全风险从输入内容筛查扩展到模型通过场景推理推断出的协助行为。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can provide harmful assistance by interpreting visual events, even without an explicit request for the underlying harmful objective. We identify this \textbf{scene-state safety vulnerability} and introduce \textbf{SceneSafetyBench}, comprising 375 image–question pairs spanning Initial, Medium, and Ultimate scene states and five visual presentations. Across eight VLMs, scene-state inputs elicit harmful assistance with attack success rates (ASRs) of 45.6–72.3%. Comparative analysis across three VLMs shows that scene-state inputs most closely resemble QR-related images in their layer-wise safety-related signal profiles, while exhibiting lower average late-layer readouts and distinct signal dynamics during generation. To turn this vulnerability into a systematic attack, we propose \textbf{S3TA}, which translates harmful objectives into scene states, queries image–question pairs independently, and assembles the responses. On seven MM-SafetyBench categories across eight open-weight and commercial VLMs, S3TA achieves a model-average ASR of \textbf{71.2%, exceeding the strongest baseline by 6.1 percentage points}. Text-side filters and visual moderation provide limited protection. These findings highlight the need to safeguard the assistance models infer through scene-grounded reasoning, beyond screening input content.

[119] Learning Grounded Reasoning from Human Gaze cs.CVPDF

Gabriel Sarch, Declan Campbell, Nicholas Budny, Kia Ghods, Katerina Fragkiadaki

TL;DR: 论文研究如何利用人类注视数据改进视觉语言模型（VLM）的视觉基础推理。作者收集了7,109个人类注视试验，发现将人类注视作为基础推理的先验（而非完整注视序列监督）效果更好，并据此提出GGR（注视引导推理器），在图表理解、视觉搜索和计数任务上实现了更高的人类对齐和更可靠的视觉基础，准确率匹配或超过主流基线。强化学习进一步提升了任务准确率、注视对齐和基础质量。

Details

Motivation: 人类注视能揭示视觉推理中的探索过程，但直接使用完整注视序列存在噪声和冗余，可能难以迁移到VLM。论文旨在探索如何有效利用人类注视数据来提升VLM的视觉推理能力，同时保持与人类行为对齐。

Result: 在图表理解、视觉搜索和计数三个任务上评估，使用人类注视作为先验的监督方式一致优于使用完整注视序列。GGR在准确率上匹配或超过领先的基础推理基线，同时具有更好的人类对齐和视觉基础质量。强化学习进一步提升了任务准确率、注视对齐和基础质量（无需注视监督）。

Insight: 创新点在于发现人类注视的价值不在于完整模仿，而在于指导空间探索和塑造推理行为；提出将注视作为先验而非完整序列监督，并设计GGR模型结合强化学习。行为分析表明注视监督模型在给出答案前表现出更多视觉探索。

Abstract: Human visual reasoning is driven by systematic exploration and extraction of task-relevant visual evidence. Human gaze provides a window into this process, making gaze a useful source of guidance for visually-grounded reasoning in vision-language models (VLMs). However, human gaze also includes noise and redundancies that may transfer poorly to VLMs. In this work, we investigate how human gaze data can be effectively leveraged to improve visual reasoning in VLMs, while maintaining alignment with human behavior. We collect 7,109 human gaze trials from a diverse and challenging set of visual reasoning tasks, and train grounded reasoning models with different forms of gaze guidance. Across evaluations in chart understanding, visual search, and counting, we find that using human gaze as a prior for grounded reasoning consistently outperforms supervision using the full gaze sequence. Based on these findings, we introduce GGR (Gaze-Guided Reasoner), which achieves greater human alignment and more reliable visual grounding while matching or outperforming leading grounded-reasoning baselines in accuracy. Reinforcement learning further improves task accuracy, gaze alignment, and grounding quality despite using no gaze supervision. Behavioral analyses show that gaze supervised models exhibit more visual exploration before arriving at an answer. Our results suggest that the value of human gaze lies in guiding spatial exploration and shaping reasoning behavior without requiring complete imitation of human behavior.

Motivation: 人类认知状态标签在规模化获取时成本高昂，而注视、车辆状态和场景视频等多模态数据较易采集；论文希望借助自监督重建目标提升损伤状态推断，并系统分析不同模态和损伤类型的影响。

Abstract: Human cognitive state (e.g., distraction, stress), gaze, and behavior interact with the driving environment in subtle ways that AI could use to assist drivers, but ground-truth labels for human cognitive state levels are costly to obtain at scale. We propose a multimodal masked-diffusion autoencoder, ID-MAE, that adds a self-supervised objective to impairment prediction: alongside predicting the impairment label as a masked token, it reconstructs masked gaze and trajectory. To probe the effects of different modalities and types of impairments on model inference, we introduce DIVERT, a driving-simulator dataset containing 103 hours of driving data (n = 109) with ten impairment inductions spanning cognitive, visual, and emotional states, recorded with gaze, vehicle state, and scene video. We demonstrate how ID-MAE affords significant improvements in impairment inference results on both the DIVERT dataset and existing datasets, and enables strong results in additional tasks, such as trajectory and gaze prediction. For location-matched pairs, our model achieves a paired AUC of 0.706 for impairment detection on DIVERT, compared to 0.590 without reconstruction loss, improving over existing baselines. We probe the design space in terms of input modalities, masking scheme, positional encoding, attention mechanism, and decoding. We also investigate the interaction between various impairments in training and inference, demonstrating that visually demanding distractions are separable from cognitive distractions. Code and data will be available upon acceptance.

Details

Motivation: WSI 弱监督分析中，新肿瘤亚型不断出现，MIL 模型需在有限历史数据下学习新类并保留旧类知识，同时推理时不能依赖任务标识；主要难点是特征聚合和预测模块更新导致旧类遗忘。

Result: 在 TCGA 的八种肿瘤亚型上进行实验，EviSlide-MIL 在多数增量学习设置下相比对比方法取得更高性能和更少遗忘，但摘要未明确给出具体指标或 SOTA 声明。

Abstract: Histopathological analysis of whole-slide images (WSIs) plays a critical role in cancer diagnosis, and multiple instance learning (MIL) is widely adopted for WSI analysis under weak supervision. As new tumor subtypes are introduced, extending existing MIL models requires learning new classes while retaining knowledge of previously learned ones. This presents a class-incremental learning challenge, where models must acquire new classes with limited access to historical training data and recognize all learned classes without task identifiers at inference. A major obstacle is forgetting, as updates to feature aggregation and prediction modules can degrade performance on previously learned classes. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{EviSlide-MIL}, a class-incremental WSI framework that integrates multiple learnable queries per class with a shared prediction module. The queries aggregate frozen patch features into class-specific representations, while the shared prediction module limits parameter growth across incremental stages. By freezing queries associated with previously learned classes, EviSlide-MIL preserves their attention patterns for identical inputs, while experience replay and knowledge distillation further mitigate forgetting as shared parameters are updated. Experiments on eight tumor subtypes from The Cancer Genome Atlas demonstrate that EviSlide-MIL achieves improved performance and reduced forgetting compared with competing methods across most learning settings, demonstrating its potential for scalable and knowledge-preserving WSI classification. Code and pretrained models will be released.

[122] FastFrames: Video Generation at Interactive Latency cs.CVPDF

Gene Chou, Guandao Yang, Fujun Luan, Lars Mescheder, Wei Dong

TL;DR: 论文提出 FastFrames 系列快速自回归视频扩散模型，通过分析推理流水线发现 VAE 解码和 KV cache 更新等常被忽视的组件占端到端延迟一半以上，并用轻量替代方案实现交互级延迟。1.3B 模型在单张 B200 上达到 36 ms/帧、28 RPS，质量与慢近 8 倍的 SOTA 相当；14B 模型在 8 张 B200 节点上达到 45 ms/帧、22 RPS，并显著提升视觉质量和真实感。

Details

Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型虽适合交互式视觉体验，但推理速度比流畅交互所需慢几个数量级；论文希望在保证生成质量的同时实现交互级延迟。

Result: 1.3B 变体在单 B200 上达到 36 ms/帧（28 RPS），与慢近 8 倍的 SOTA 模型生成质量相当；14B 变体在 8×B200 节点上达到 45 ms/帧（22 RPS），视觉质量和真实感显著更好，并支持相机条件与物理条件视频生成的实时交互。

Insight: 创新点在于重新审视被当作固定实现细节的 VAE 解码和 KV cache 更新等瓶颈，挑战其不可替代假设并用轻量组件替换；客观来看，端到端 profiling 驱动系统级优化，并将快速自回归视频扩散落地到实时交互应用，是主要可借鉴之处。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models generate frames based on new observations and inputs, making them a promising medium for interactive visual experiences. However, existing models are orders of magnitude too slow for fluid interaction. This paper introduces FastFrames, a family of fast autoregressive video diffusion models that achieves interactive latency without compromising quality. We profile the inference pipeline of state-of-the-art models and find that operations often treated as fixed implementation details, such as VAE decoding and KV cache updates, in fact account for over half of end-to-end inference latency. We challenge the assumption that these components are indispensable and replace them with lightweight alternatives. Our 1.3B variant runs at 36 ms per frame, or 28 responses per second (RPS), on a single B200 GPU while matching the generation quality of a state-of-the-art model that is nearly 8x slower. Our 14B variant runs at 45 ms per frame, or 22 RPS, on an 8x B200 GPU node and yields significantly better visual quality and realism. We show that FastFrames enables real-time interaction in applications such as camera-conditioned and physics-conditioned video generation.

[123] Spool: Low-Latency Streaming Inference for Hybrid Vision-Language Models cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Rui Ning, Om Chabra, Hari Balakrishnan

TL;DR: 论文提出 Spool，一种面向混合视觉语言模型（VLM）的低延迟流式推理框架。针对带线性循环层（如 Gated DeltaNet）的 VLM 在维护滑动窗口时需重放整个窗口的问题，Spool 利用循环状态更新的仿射可组合性，缓存每帧的状态变换并在窗口推进时高效组合，从而无需重跑骨干网络即可移除过期帧，并设计专用数据结构与 GPU 内核降低开销。

Details

Motivation: 视觉辅助代理和机器人等应用需要在有限的历史帧窗口内及时回答关于近期视频的问题。混合 VLM 虽适合此类场景，但线性循环层将所有历史帧压缩为共享状态，导致滑动窗口过期帧移除通常需要重新运行整个窗口，推理延迟高。

Result: 在 5 个开源混合 VLM 和 4 个视频推理基准的 20 组模型-基准测试中，Spool 均取得最低的 p95 首 token 时延，相比两个近期 vLLM 基线中更快者几何平均降低 6.6 倍，同时平均准确率提升 2.3 个百分点。

Insight: 创新点在于发现循环层状态更新对状态是仿射的且系数依赖输入，因此每帧影响可表示为可关联组合的变换；Spool 无需训练，通过缓存并组合逐帧状态变换，在每个循环层内精确移除过期帧而无需重放帧。客观来看，其价值在于利用混合模型中线性循环层的代数结构实现高效滑动窗口状态淘汰，并用专用数据结构和 GPU 内核将缓存与组合开销降到实用水平。

Abstract: Applications such as visual accessibility agents and robotics require answering questions about recent video content promptly, over a bounded window of past frames (e.g., the last two minutes). Hybrid vision-language models (VLMs), which interleave attention with linear recurrent layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), are attractive for this setting, but maintaining a sliding window is expensive. The reason is that recurrent layers combine all past frames into a shared state, so expiring old frames normally requires re-running the entire window through the model. We observe that the recurrent update is affine in the state with input-dependent coefficients, so the effect of each frame can be captured as a transformation that composes associatively. We present Spool, a training-free framework that caches per-frame state transformations and composes them efficiently as the window advances, removing expired frames from each recurrent layer’s state without replaying frames through the backbone. Removal is exact within each layer; as with key-value eviction in attention, expired frames can retain residual influence through the cached inputs computed by earlier layers. We design a dedicated data structure and GPU kernels to achieve low caching and composition overhead. Across five open-source hybrid VLMs and four video reasoning benchmarks, Spool achieves the lowest p95 time-to-first-token in all 20 model-benchmark tests, reducing it by a geometric mean of $6.6\times$ over the faster of two recent vLLM baselines, while improving accuracy by 2.3 percentage points on average.

[124] Conserved Verdicts, Drifting Commitments: Identifying Harmful Overthinking cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Jianghong Zhou, Hao Rong, Chen Lin

TL;DR: 论文研究推理模型「过度思考」导致已得正确答案丢失的现象，区分了「承诺」（若此刻停止所得答案正确的概率）与「裁决」（最终答案正确的概率），论证仅估计最终正确性的探针无法判断是否该停止推理，并给出两个恒等式：承诺-裁决差距等于剩余答案质量的期望变化与停止可获得的准确率；准确率-预算曲线可分解为链内答案变化与仍在运行的链的变化。作者在MATH-500和OlympiadBench上用六个推理模型验证了该框架。

Details

Motivation: 推理模型在已经得到正确答案后继续思考反而可能丢掉答案，因此需要判断「何时停止」。现有做法（最终正确性探针）只估计最终答案是否正确，论文要说明这种估计为何不足以支撑停止决策，并给出应当测量什么。

Result: 在MATH-500上，DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B处于正确答案的379个点上继续推理平均损失3.6分（95% CI [2.2, 5.2]），且损失集中在少数题目；在三个家族六个模型、预算至16,384 token下，下降的准确率曲线主要源于「哪些链仍在运行」（幸存者效应），仍在推理的链则保持或提升答案；理想oracle停止规则在1.5B和7B上最多仅增益+1.8与+2.5分。

Insight: 创新点在于提出commitment/verdict的区分与两个恒等式，指出最终正确性作为对固定结果的预测不存在可预测漂移、因而无法用于停止比较；客观上可借鉴之处是把准确率-预算曲线分解为链内答案变化与链存活变化，从而揭示「过度思考损失」与幸存者偏差，并主张在构建停止规则前先明确测量对象。

Abstract: A reasoning model that has reached a correct answer can lose it by continuing to think. Deciding whether to stop means comparing two probabilities at the same point of a chain: that the answer the model would give if stopped now is correct (its commitment), and that the answer it will finish with is correct (its verdict). We show that the verdict alone, which is what final-correctness probes estimate, cannot make this comparison. As a forecast of a fixed outcome it has no predictable drift, whether continuing helps or hurts. The commitment-verdict gap equals the expected remaining change in answer quality and the accuracy gained by stopping. A second identity splits any accuracy-versus-budget curve into answer change within chains and change in which chains are still running. We measure both on MATH-500. At all 379 points where DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B holds a correct answer, selected without regard to how its chain ends, continuing loses 3.6 points on average (95% CI [2.2, 5.2]), and the loss concentrates in a few problems. In six reasoning models from three families, on MATH-500 and OlympiadBench and with budgets up to 16,384 tokens, declining accuracy curves come from which chains remain, while the chains still reasoning keep or improve their answers. An oracle stopping rule gains at most +1.8 and +2.5 points at 1.5B and 7B. The framework says what to measure before building a stopping rule.

[125] Thinking Harder Doesn’t Lead to Better Choices: Reasoning Effort Increases the Confidence but Not the Correctness of LLM Shopping Agents cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CY | cs.HC | econ.GNPDF

Adi Krishnamoorthy, X. Y. Han

TL;DR: 论文研究将LLM用作多模态购物智能体时，增加推理努力（thinking effort）能否提升商品选择质量。作者在基于WebShop、Amazon SQID和Wayfair WANDS构建的三个商品选择benchmark上评测十个前沿模型，发现增加思考努力并不能统计显著地提升决策质量，却会显著提升模型自报的置信度，即额外思考带来的是信心而非正确性；此外多数模型校准性差，且错误在模型间高度共享。

Details

Motivation: 人们通常假设推理能力更强、思考更久的模型能做出更高质量的购买决策。论文旨在检验这一信念是否成立，并考察模型自报置信度能否预示其出错的可能性。

Result: 在三个benchmark上，提升思考努力未能统计显著地改善决策质量，但显著提高了报告的置信度；多数模型在各努力水平下校准性不佳；错误高度共享，多个模型常在同一任务出错，并在这些任务上大量选择同一个错误商品。

Insight: 创新点在于系统性地揭示“推理努力≠正确性”、置信度与正确性脱钩以及模型错误同质化现象。客观来看，其价值在于对多模态购物智能体可靠性、校准性和集体盲区的实证刻画，提示部署时不能把思考投入或自报置信度当作质量保证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous shopping agents that process multimodal product listings (consisting of visual and text data) and recommend purchases on behalf of consumers. Naturally, one might assume that models with more thinking power would make higher-quality purchasing decisions. To test this belief, we ask two questions about such agents: does asking a model to think harder make it choose better products, and does the confidence it reports tell us the likelihood it may be wrong? We benchmark ten frontier models on three product-choice benchmarks built from WebShop, Amazon’s SQID and Wayfair’s WANDS platforms to see if LLMs with more thinking power actually make better-quality product purchase decisions. Across all three benchmarks, we found that increasing the thinking effort does not increase decision quality in a statistically meaningful way. The same change in thinking effort raises stated confidence at a statistically significant level, so extra thinking buys confidence rather than correctness. We also measure the calibration of these models (how well their reported confidence percentage matches their true correctness percentage) in these tasks and show that most models are not well-calibrated at every effort level. We also find that errors are strongly shared: when one model errs in a task, several others tend to err on that same task. Furthermore, on tasks where several models all err, a large number of them pick the same wrong product.

Rahim Hossain, Dipanwita Saha, Ashirbad Mishra, Xinxin Shu, Svetlana Strunjas

TL;DR: 论文提出 LoCaR，一个无需训练的局部文本到图像检索框架，利用冻结的视觉语言模型和 SAM2 生成以对象为中心的独立候选区域，对每个区域分别编码以获得更局部的表示。该方法还研究了可选的 paired-support adaptation 作为残差，利用匹配的文本-区域样例而不更新底层编码器，并引入基于 Visual Genome 的 LOCFG 基准来评估更小、更具挑战性的指代目标检索。

Details

Motivation: 整图嵌入容易低估小对象和局部属性，而这些信息对局部化查询中的相关图像区分很重要；论文旨在解决无需训练情况下，冻结视觉语言模型对局部文本到图像检索表示不足的问题。

Result: 摘要在多个视觉语言骨干模型上进行了实验，定性表明区域表示本身的改进是主要增益来源；同时提出 LOCFG 基准用于评估更小、更困难的局部指代检索，但未给出具体数值或 SOTA 对比。

Insight: 创新点包括无需训练的局部检索框架、利用 SAM2 候选区域和冻结视觉编码器进行独立区域编码、可选的 paired-support adaptation 残差机制，以及面向局部检索的新基准 LOCFG；客观看，其核心价值在于强调区域级表示对冻结 VLM 检索性能的关键作用。

Abstract: Whole image embeddings can under represent small objects and local attributes that distinguish relevant images for localized queries. We present LoCaR, a training free framework for localized text to image retrieval with frozen vision language models. LoCaR constructs and utilizes independent proposals (SAM2) focusing on the object so as to get a more localized representation that independently encodes each region using the frozen visual encoder. We further study paired-support adaptation as an optional residual that uses matched text region exemplars without updating the underlying encoders. We also introduce LOCFG, a Visual Genome derived benchmark designed to evaluate localized retrieval for smaller and more challenging referents. Across several vision language backbones, our results show that improving the region representation itself is the main source of gain.

Allen G. Roush, Minh Nhat Nguyen, Ravid Shwartz-Ziv, Judah Goldfeder, Sanjay Basu

TL;DR: 论文研究开放权重语言模型在长文本生成和高温采样下性能退化的问题，认为关键不只是训练不足，而是采样器是否在温度升高时控制候选token池。作者系统测试29个1B至122B模型、10个基准及百万token检索任务，发现top-nsigma等保持小候选池的采样器能显著缓解退化，而top-p等在高温下候选池膨胀后性能崩溃。

Details

Motivation: 长上下文生成和agentic生成越长越差，现有修复多依赖更多训练；论文关注高温采样时采样规则对长生成稳定性的决定性作用，试图不额外训练地解决性能退化。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified编码agent等任务上，温度2时top-p/top-k/Top-H解决0/100个GitHub issue，top-nsigma在三个Qwen3.5模型上解决46至62个；所有基准上top-nsigma中位数保持温度1得分的100%，top-p仅26%。133种采样器设置与温度组合中，典型候选池大小与质量评分强负相关（Spearman -0.77）。

Insight: 创新点在于把长上下文生成退化归因于采样温度下候选池膨胀，提出用候选池大小诊断采样器稳定性，并引入top-nsigma；客观看，其价值是无需训练、仅改采样策略即可显著提升高温长生成鲁棒性，并给出跨模型跨任务的大规模实证。

Abstract: Open-weight language models are widely observed to get worse the longer they generate. Often, attempts to fix this involve more training. At high temperature, The sampler, i.e. the rule that picks the next token out of the model’s probabilities, decides whether long and agentic generation survives and/or thrives. We test 29 open-weight models from 1B to 122B parameters on ten established benchmarks plus needle retrieval out to one million tokens, covering SWE-bench Verified coding agents, long chain-of-thought reasoning, long-form writing, long structured output and long-context reading. Samplers that keep their pool of candidate tokens small and steady as temperature rises don’t lose accuracy. Samplers that let in low-probability tokens fall apart. At temperature 2, coding agents running top-p, top-k or Top-H resolve 0 of 100 GitHub issues, while the same agents running top-nsigma resolve 46 to 62 and win every issue the two samplers disagree on, on three Qwen3.5 models. Over all benchmarks top-nsigma keeps a median 100% of its temperature-1 score where top-p keeps 26%. Counting how many tokens each sampler lets through explains why. In one Llama-3.1-8B story, going from temperature 1 to 2 grows top-p’s pool from 4 tokens to 51,845 while top-nsigma stays at 3. Across 133 combinations of sampler setting and temperature, the typical pool size predicts judged quality (Spearman correlation of -0.77), and when we pick the pool at temperature 1 and only apply temperature afterwards, Top-H, min-p and p-less stop collapsing. At the default temperature of 1 most samplers perform about the same, We release all code and data to the public.

[128] Not All Evidence Is Admissible: Evidence Governance for Tool-Augmented Medical Agents cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Runxun Zhang, Onofrio Antonio Catalano, Jinqiao Wang, Jingwei Wei

TL;DR: 论文指出，工具增强的医疗智能体会默认把所有工具返回的内容都当作证据；但医疗工具会静默失败，而冻结的推理器无法忽略已进入上下文的内容，于是许多原本能答对的案例在加入工具输出后被翻转为错误，作者称之为“证据劫持”。作者用V-information理论解释该现象，并把工具增强推理重新表述为“证据治理”：把“什么算证据”的判定从推理器中分离出来，提出无需训练的CIEG框架——每个工具输出写成可与原始输入核对的卡片，独立核查器只根据输入和卡片决定哪些卡片可成为证据，推理器只依据被采纳的卡片作答。

Details

Motivation: 工具输出并非总是可靠，医疗工具会静默失败；而冻结的推理器缺乏“忽略”选项，错误上下文会污染原本正确的判断，造成证据劫持，因此需要一种机制来决定哪些工具输出可以作为证据。

Result: 在医疗问答、眼底青光眼筛查和胸部X光肺炎分类三项任务上，CIEG在同等规模模型中取得最高准确率，并在更低成本下显著优于基于同一骨干模型的SOTA工具使用智能体与多智能体系统。

Insight: 理论分析区分了过滤带来的两类收益：一类只是让推理器“看得更少”，另一类是通过基于内容的筛选让它“看得更准”；据此把证据准入决策外置给独立核查器，以恢复推理器“忽略”的选项。核查器全部免训练、仅依赖原始输入与卡片，这一模块化设计是可以借鉴的关键创新。

Abstract: Tool-augmented medical agents treat whatever their tools return as evidence. We show that this default is costly: medical tools fail silently, and a frozen reasoner cannot ignore what enters its context, so many cases it would have answered correctly are flipped to wrong once tool outputs are added. We call this evidence hijacking. $\mathcal{V}$-information explains the phenomenon: the reasoner has lost the option to ignore, so a tool output can carry negative usable information, and moving the decision of what to keep outside the reasoner restores that option. The theory further shows that part of any filtering gain comes merely from letting the reasoner see less, and only selection by content lets it see better. Building on this, we recast tool-augmented reasoning as evidence governance, which separates deciding what counts as evidence from the reasoner that answers, and propose context-independent evidence governance (CIEG), the first medical agent framework that rules, after acquisition, on whether each individual tool output may enter as evidence. Every tool output is written as a card that can be checked against the raw input; an independent checker that sees only the input and the cards decides which cards become evidence; and the reasoner answers from the admitted cards alone, with no training anywhere. On medical question answering, fundus glaucoma screening, and chest X-ray pneumonia classification, it achieves the highest accuracy among models of comparable scale, and significantly outperforms state-of-the-art tool-using agents and multi-agent systems built on the same backbone at lower cost.

[129] Fine-Grained Japanese Clinical Named Entity Recognition: A Controlled Comparison of BERT and Open-Weight Large Language Models cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Feng Han, Ayako Yagahara, Kenji Hirata, Atsushi Yoshida, Kohsuke Kudo

TL;DR: 论文在细粒度日语临床命名实体识别任务 iCorpus（113 类实体）上，系统比较了 BERT-CRF 与九个开放权重 LLM。结果显示零样本 LLM 效果远低于 BERT，但在足够输出长度下经 QLoRA 微调的大参数 LLM 可超过 BERT；输出 token 限制对比较结果影响显著，而仅用 JSON Schema 约束输出结构不能解决零样本低准确率问题。

Details

Motivation: 生成式 LLM 能否替代编码器模型用于细粒度日语临床 NER 尚不明确，尤其其对生成设置、输出约束和微调策略的敏感性缺乏受控评估。

Result: 零样本最佳 relaxed F1 仅 0.337，BERT 基线为 0.836-0.841；在更大输出限制下，八个至少 13B 的微调 LLM relaxed F1 达 0.882-0.914、exact-surface F1 达 0.840-0.880，超过两个 BERT 基线。提高输出限制使三 seed 平均 relaxed F1 提升 0.052-0.085，JSON Schema 约束影响较小。

Insight: 创新点在于对 BERT 与开放权重 LLM 进行受控比较，覆盖零样本、JSON Schema 约束、QLoRA 微调和输出 token 限制等因素；客观看，其价值主要是揭示细粒度临床 NER 中任务微调与足够生成长度的重要性，而非提出全新模型。

Abstract: Background: Generative large language models (LLMs) offer an alternative to encoder-based clinical named entity recognition (NER), but their performance on fine-grained Japanese extraction tasks and sensitivity to generation settings remain uncertain. Methods: We compared two fine-tuned BERT-CRF baselines with nine open-weight LLMs on iCorpus, which defines 113 entity categories. The evaluation used 433 sentences from 36 test documents; 1,672 sentences were used for training. LLMs were evaluated using zero-shot prompting with and without JSON Schema constraints and three-seed QLoRA fine-tuning. Fine-tuned models were assessed under different output-token limits, including 900 and larger model-specific limits of 2,600 or 4,096 tokens. Performance was measured using sentence-level label-surface matching with relaxed and exact-surface F1. Results: The highest zero-shot relaxed F1 was 0.337, compared with 0.836-0.841 for the BERT baselines. At the larger output limits, all eight fine-tuned LLMs with at least 13B total parameters exceeded both BERT baselines (relaxed F1, 0.882-0.914; exact-surface F1, 0.840-0.880). Across three-seed means, increasing the output limit improved relaxed F1 by 0.052-0.085, alongside higher recall and fewer length-limited outputs. JSON Schema constraints changed relaxed F1 by -0.014 to +0.008 among seven models outside the two reasoning-oriented configurations, while medical-general model pairs showed no consistent advantage for medical specialization. Conclusions: On this historical Japanese case-report benchmark, task-specific fine-tuning combined with adequate generation capacity was associated with stronger LLM extraction performance. Output-token limits materially affected comparisons with BERT, whereas enforcing output structure alone did not overcome low zero-shot accuracy.

[130] LMLineage: Content Lineage and Context Governance for LLM Agents cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Wei Hou, Jingxin Geng, Xin Chen, Zheqing Jiang

TL;DR: 论文提出 LMLineage，一个面向 LLM Agent 的带外内容溯源与上下文治理系统。它无需白盒访问模型或额外推理，就能将 Agent 会话中的每个输出句子归因到支撑它的输入片段，并给出校准置信度；基于溯源元数据，进一步实现概念漂移微压缩：当旧输入文档不再被引用时，仅精准压缩这些文档，同时逐字保留用户指令。

Details

Motivation: 现有 Agent 可解释性多关注模型内部权重，难以覆盖多源上下文下的推理与决策过程；同时上下文压缩通常等到长度上限后整体摘要，容易丢失关键指令或保留已无用内容。论文希望在不依赖模型内部信息的情况下实现输出到输入的内容溯源，并据此进行更精细的上下文治理。

Result: 在 10 个中文 Web-QA 案例的 71 条金标句子及 3 个生成模型、90 轮多轮会话上评估：窗口级嵌入相似度比整文档或词法匹配更符合 LLM 复述；贪心窗口快速路径平均更快且更准；单 GPU 下单轮归因约 1 秒，上下文累积至 40 轮仍稳定；概念漂移检测误压缩率为 0%，召回受归因准确率限制。

Insight: 创新点在于将 Agent 可解释性扩展到多源上下文的内容级溯源，并以带外、无额外推理的方式生成句子级归因；进一步把溯源元数据用于概念漂移感知的微压缩，实现只压缩已漂移文档并保留用户指令，兼顾可解释性与上下文治理。客观看，其可借鉴之处是轻量归因机制与保守压缩策略的结合，以及面向多轮 Agent 会话的实用化设计。

Abstract: Interpretability for AI agents should not be confined to understanding the model’s internal weights. It must also cover the agent’s reasoning and decision-making process over multi-source context. We present LMLineage, an out-of-band system that attributes every output sentence of an agent session to its supporting input spans with calibrated confidences, without requiring white-box access to the model or any additional inference passes. On top of the lineage metadata, we build concept-drift micro compaction: instead of waiting for the context-length limit and summarizing everything, the system detects when previously used input documents have stopped being referenced (“drifted”) and surgically compacts only those, preserving user instructions verbatim. We evaluate on a multi-tier corpus: 71 gold annotated sentences from 10 Chinese web-QA cases, plus 90 turns of independently collected multi-turn sessions across three generator models (gpt-4o-mini, qwen3.8-27b, deepseek-v4-flash). Results show that (i) window-level embedding similarity is far better aligned with LLM paraphrasing than whole document or lexical matching; (ii) the greedy-window fast path is both faster and more accurate on average than exhaustive line-matching (a multiple-comparisons bias in the latter inflates web recall at the cost of overall accuracy); (iii) per-turn attribution completes in 1s on a single GPU, remaining stable as context accumulates to 40 turns; and (iv) concept-drift detection is conservative (0% false compaction) with recall bounded by attribution accuracy. We further describe the concept-drift micro compaction design and an interactive incident-tracing/memory-cleaning case study. A companion patent application covers the lineage construction method.

[131] When Agent Skills Go Stale: Runtime Behavior and Selective Maintenance in Evolving Environments cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Ömer Faruk Akgül, Shuli Jiang, Yuting Zhang, Yi Zhang, Xiaohan Fei

TL;DR: 论文研究智能体技能在环境变化后失效的问题：技能状态复用虽能节省推理与环境交互成本，但环境变化会使部分状态过时。作者在四种任务设置和两个模型上对比环境变化前后的技能复用效果，并提出选择性维护方法，在不额外调用模型的情况下检查、修复并验证技能状态，必要时重建。

Details

Motivation: 智能体复用历史经验形成的技能状态可摊销推理和环境交互成本，但环境变化可能导致技能状态部分过时，从而损害任务表现或削弱执行效率，论文旨在理解和缓解这一“技能过期”问题。

Result: 实验表明，过时技能相对无技能可使任务性能下降超过10个百分点，或使执行成本增加近40%，且任务成功率可能不变但执行节省下降。相较复用过期状态，选择性维护最多提升67个百分点准确率，并减少最多37%的执行步骤。

Insight: 创新点在于系统分析环境变化下技能状态过期的运行时影响，并提出选择性维护：复用成功执行中的检查来识别冲突、局部修复受影响内容并验证更新，必要时重建，且无需额外模型调用或变化描述。客观看，其价值在于将技能维护从全量重建转为按需局部修复，并强调环境演化下保持技能推理时价值需选择性维护状态。

Abstract: Agent skills use past experience and current observations to guide decisions and actions on related tasks. This experience is retained in skill state, whose reuse can amortize inference-time reasoning and environment interaction. When environment changes, parts of this state can become outdated while others remain useful. Across four task settings and two models, we compare skill reuse before and after environment changes. Previously beneficial skills can reduce task performance by over 10 percentage points or increase execution cost by nearly 40% relative to no skill. Task success can also remain unchanged while execution savings decline. These effects depend on which environment-specific details become outdated and the agent’s ability to compensate using current observations. We introduce selective maintenance, which replays checks from successful executions without additional model calls. It verifies whether observed differences conflict with skill state, repairs affected content, and validates updates, rebuilding when local repair is insufficient. No description of the change is supplied. Relative to reusing stale state, maintenance improves task accuracy by up to 67pp and reduces execution steps by up to 37%. These results suggest that preserving a skill’s inference time value as environments evolve requires selectively maintaining its state.

The method decomposes this complex task into a chain of simple visual decisions about what to construct and what to correct. Geometric tools support precise construction, allowing the agent to focus on visual reasoning. Using the mid-sized Qwen3.8-27B model, CADMAGEDDON outperforms prior approaches, including systems based on frontier models, in reconstruction quality on all the main benchmarks. [133] PACT: Plan-Action Consistency Training by Preserving Correct Plans and Repairing Actions in GUI Agents cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Zhuohan Cai, Zhangxuan Gu, Runze Li, Beitong Zhou, Ming Wen

TL;DR: 论文研究了GUI智能体在推理中能给出正确的即时计划、却执行了与计划不符的动作这一”执行鸿沟”问题。为此提出PACT训练框架：利用带标注的参考动作识别执行错误，一方面在计划token区间屏蔽负向策略梯度以保护正确计划，另一方面在保持推理上下文不变的前提下，通过带stop-gradient配对权重的偏好代理，让参考动作优于错误的执行动作，并且不改变原有的标量动作奖励。

Details

Motivation: 在结果监督的强化学习中，响应级的负向优势会被广播到所有token上，导致正确的规划也错误地受到惩罚，从而产生信用分配冲突；而在多步任务中，单次执行错误就可能导致整个任务失败，因此需要解决规划与动作不一致的问题。

Result: 在AndroidWorld上，任务成功率从60.34%提升到68.97%（+8.63pp）；AndroidControl High上的规则化计划-动作一致性从83.71%提升到89.74%（+6.03pp）；迁移到桌面智能体后，在369个任务的OSWorld榜单上成功率从40.92%提升到44.17%（+3.25pp）。

Insight: 创新点在于把执行错误定位到具体token区间，并据此进行细粒度信用分配：通过屏蔽计划跨度内的负梯度来保护已验证的计划；同时用偏好代理修复动作，且不改变原有标量奖励，使方法可以广泛兼容现有的结果监督强化学习流程。

Abstract: GUI agents can formulate a correct immediate plan in their reasoning yet emit an action that fails to execute it, creating an execution gap. This recurring mismatch undermines reliable task completion, as a single execution error can derail a multi-step workflow. In outcome-supervised reinforcement learning, broadcasting a negative response-level advantage across all generated tokens risks penalizing valid planning together with erroneous execution. To address this credit-assignment conflict, we introduce PACT (Plan-Action Consistency Training), a training framework that combines reference-aligned plan protection and plan-aligned action repair without altering the original scalar action reward. Using annotated reference actions to identify execution gaps, PACT protects the verified plan by masking negative policy-gradient contributions across its token span. Keeping the generated reasoning unchanged as context, it then favors the reference action over the emitted erroneous action through a preference surrogate with stop-gradient pair weights. Compared with the outcome-only baseline, PACT raises AndroidWorld task success from 60.34% to 68.97% (+8.63 pp), while improving rule-based plan-action consistency on AndroidControl High from 83.71% to 89.74% (+6.03 pp). Adapting PACT to desktop agents also improves task success from 40.92% to 44.17% (+3.25 pp) on the full 369-task OSWorld benchmark. Together, these results establish PACT as an effective credit-assignment framework for bridging execution gaps and improving task completion across mobile and desktop GUI environments.

[134] The Between-Round Decision in Autonomous Research: An LLM Controller That Reads the Whole Trajectory cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Caoxin Wang, Shengkai Gan, Xiang Hu, Lili Pan

TL;DR: 论文研究自主研究流程中的“轮间决策”问题，即每轮实验后决定是继续优化当前最佳方案还是开启新方向、基于哪个方案继续以及设定什么目标。作者提出 BRED，一个基于 LLM 的控制器，维护当前最佳已验证方案 Champion 和历史轮次日志，每轮只做一次决策。实验表明，让 LLM 读取累积证据可提升多个研究代理基准和新任务表现。

Details

Motivation: 执行一个想法后，其前景可能发生变化，因此自主研究的下一步应基于实验结果而非初始提案。现有系统通常依赖规则、打分、搜索树和调度等机制，而让 LLM 做轮间决策时它需要读取哪些证据仍不明确。

Result: 在 ETTh1 上 MAE 降至 0.4172，较基线提升 4.80%；SST-2 准确率 95.07%，提升 3.44 点；CIFAR-100 准确率 84.63%，提升 3.43 点，均超过已发表结果。消融显示不读历史时 ETTh1 为 0.4299、CIFAR-100 为 83.71%，完整控制器 MAE 低 2.95%、准确率高 0.92 点。新任务语音抑郁检测在无泄漏协议下 macro-F1 达 0.6164，高于基线 5.5 点、高于最强已发表系统 4.0 点。

Insight: 创新点在于将自主研究中的轮间决策显式建模为基于累积证据的 LLM 控制问题，并用 Champion 加历史日志的简洁结构替代规则、搜索树等外部机制。客观看，其价值还在于通过消融证明“读取完整轨迹/历史证据”对决策质量有实质贡献，并展示该控制器可迁移到新研究任务。

Abstract: Executing an idea often changes how promising it looks, so the next step in autonomous research should rest on results, not on proposals. After each round of experiments, the system must decide whether to refine the best solution or start a new direction, which solution to build from, and what goal to set. We call this the between-round decision. Existing systems wrap it in machinery such as rules, scores, search trees, and schedules; where an LLM makes it, what the LLM needs to read is left open. We ask whether an LLM can make this decision from accumulated evidence, without a search structure around it, and how much of that evidence it needs to read. We present BRED (Between-Round Evidence-based Decision), an LLM-based controller that maintains the best verified solution so far (the Champion) and a log of past rounds, and makes one decision per round. On three widely used research-agent benchmark tasks, BRED improves on the task baselines by 4.80% in MAE on ETTh1 (to 0.4172) and by 3.44 and 3.43 points of accuracy on SST-2 (to 95.07%) and CIFAR-100 (to 84.63%), ahead of the published results. When the controller does not read the accumulated evidence, the same loop gains less: with no history it reaches 0.4299 on ETTh1 and 83.71% on CIFAR-100, whereas the full controller’s MAE is 2.95% lower and its accuracy 0.92 points higher. On a new task, speech-based depression detection under a leakage-free protocol, it reaches a macro-F1 of 0.6164 on seeds held out from selection, 5.5 points above its baseline and 4.0 above the strongest published system. Code is available at https://github.com/learninginvision/BRED.

[135] A Full-Ledger Counterfactual Audit of Adaptive Reasoning cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Xiaopeng Chu, Wenzheng Kong, Wei Liu, Wenyi Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出全账本反事实价值审计（FLCVA）框架，用于评估自适应推理中“立即作答”与“继续推理”的反事实价值，并核算策略使用所有信号的成本。在GSM8K和MATH-500上，作者比较即时答案与同一冻结前缀的固定长度续写，发现随机续写能修复部分错误答案，但也会破坏少量正确答案；离线回放表明利用续写正确性标签分配计算对DeepSeek有效、对Qwen3不确定，轻量短视控制器主要只是识别当前错误。

Details

Motivation: 自适应推理需要决定是否继续消耗计算，但“当前答案错误”并不等于“继续推理会改进答案”。论文旨在系统审计续写带来的真实反事实收益，并避免忽略策略所用信号的成本。

Result: 在GSM8K和MATH-500上，随机256 token续写可修复Qwen3-8B中26.6%、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B中18.9%的错误答案，同时仅破坏1.4%和1.9%的正确答案。离线回放中，利用独立续写正确性标签分配计算可提升DeepSeek表现，Qwen3提升不明确；轻量短视控制器在完整成本核算下额外价值有限。

Insight: 创新点在于提出全账本反事实审计，将局部续写价值测量、从可观测信号预测该价值、以及利用预测分配计算三者分开评估，并计入所有信号成本；客观上有助于揭示仅预测当前错误不足以指导自适应推理。

Abstract: Adaptive reasoning must decide whether to answer now or spend more compute. Predicting that the current answer is wrong does not establish whether further reasoning will improve it. We introduce the Full-Ledger Counterfactual Value Audit (FLCVA), which compares an immediate answer with four fixed-length continuations from the same frozen prefix under one answer procedure and accounts for the cost of every signal used by a policy. On GSM8K and MATH-500, a random 256-token continuation recovers a wrong answer in 26.6% of Qwen3-8B cases and 18.9% of DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B cases, while damaging a correct answer in 1.4% and 1.9%. Correctness labels from independently sampled continuations predict a separate continuation’s outcome in both primary models. In offline replay, using these labels to allocate compute improves performance on DeepSeek; the Qwen3 gain remains inconclusive. Under full cost accounting, the tested lightweight, myopic controllers mainly recognize current errors and add little continuation-specific value beyond that shortcut. A Llama-8B replication and action/cost ablations examine the robustness of these findings. FLCVA separates measuring local continuation value, predicting it from observable signals, and using those predictions to allocate computation.

Insight: 创新点在于构建可回放的受控基准，将证据获取与模型推理分离，并系统比较搜索API和记忆策略的影响。客观分析认为，其强调证据本身对预测性能的重要性不亚于模型，为评估LLM预测代理提供了更严谨的实验框架。

Abstract: Agents built on large language models (LLMs) increasingly forecast real-world events by searching the Web and reasoning over what they find. How well such an agent forecasts depends on both the retrieved evidence and the model, making control over that evidence essential for evaluation. Live evaluations can mix retrieval with reasoning when each system searches on its own, while retrospective search can leak later information through date filters or today’s ranking of historical pages. Comparing models, search sources, and memory strategies requires both fixed evidence and a record of how it evolved before resolution. We introduce ForecastGym, which records what four commercial web search APIs return for open prediction-market questions every two days and replays this frozen evidence offline once outcomes are known, comparing models, memory, and captured search sources under a fixed evidence-acquisition policy. Its first release covers 150 questions, 614 evidence states, one per question and collection time, and 131k search results. Rerunning the same queries after settlement returns results that all but state the outcome for up to 92% of the questions, despite the tested date filters. On the frozen evidence, all tested LLMs trail the market in forecasting accuracy. Our diagnostic analysis suggests that missed public information contributes more to the gap in high-gap states than misreading does. Switching the search API changes a model’s Brier score by up to 0.043, more than the spread across models, while persistent notes score similarly to rereading all evidence at half the input tokens. The evidence a forecaster sees can matter as much as the model that reads it.

[137] FrameRouter: Frame Budget Routing for Long-Video Understanding on Video-MME-v2 and Beyond cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Bingjun Luo, Jialin Guo, Siqi Li

TL;DR: 论文指出长视频问答基准通常对所有问题使用统一帧预算，但不同问题所需视觉证据差异很大。为此提出FrameRouter，一种无需训练的测试时框架，通过证据需求路由、预算约束分配和冻结的帧采样策略库，在保持平均帧预算不变的情况下为每个问题动态分配不同帧预算，从而提升长视频理解效果。

Details

Motivation: 现有长视频问答评测和推理通常采用统一帧预算，忽略了问题对视觉证据需求的不同；统一增加帧数也不一定能稳定提升所有指标，因此需要在相同平均预算下更合理地分配帧资源。

Result: 在Video-MME-v2及多个长视频理解基准上，FrameRouter通过更选择性地使用相同平均视觉预算提升了长视频问答表现；摘要未给出具体定量数值或SOTA结论。

Abstract: Long-video question answering benchmarks like Video-MME-v2 typically evaluate systems under a uniform frame budget for every question, despite stark variation in the evidence each query actually demands. We analyze this mismatch on Video-MME-v2 and find that different question subsets prefer different frozen frame-budget policies, while uniformly increasing the frame count does not reliably improve all metrics. These findings motivate fixed-mean frame-budget routing, a controlled inference setting in which each question may receive a different frame budget while the average budget matches a uniform reference. We introduce FrameRouter, a training-free test-time framework that combines an evidence-demand router, a budget-constrained allocation rule, and a frozen bank of frame-sampling policies. By controlling how much visual evidence each question receives while leaving the underlying samplers unchanged, FrameRouter is orthogonal to query-aware frame selection. Across Video-MME-v2 and several long-video understanding benchmarks, FrameRouter improves long-video question answering by using the same average visual budget more selectively across questions. Code is available at https://github.com/bingjunluo/FrameRouter.

Abstract: We introduce EJBench, a benchmark of engineering judgement: whether a model can predict how a design behaves at new conditions from a few earlier results, and decide on it. Unlike engineering benchmarks that test whether an agent can operate simulation or CAD software, EJBench is software-agnostic and asks what an engineer would conclude without running a simulation. Its tasks come from a conjugate heat transfer design campaign for battery cold plates, in three families: matching unlabelled test results to their operating points, choosing which stored runs to reveal before signing off a design, and ranking new duties by peak cell temperature. Every answer is taken from a converged simulation, not from an LLM judge. Hyojung Han

TL;DR: 论文研究在 NVIDIA B200 与 vLLM 部署约束下，对多语言 LLM Qwen3-8B 进行混合精度量化，覆盖 INT2/INT3、GPTQ、NVFP4 与混合配置。作者先验证 144 个融合线性模块的原生打包执行与精度，再用预注册门控评估韩语、英语、数值和推理质量。最终配置采用 16-bit 激活、98 个 NVFP4 仅权重模块、46 个 INT8 模块和 INT8 输出头，在未触碰的 FINAL-81 集上通过所有门控。

Details

Motivation: 低比特量化只有在保持服务相关精度、可由支持的 GPU kernel 执行，并满足实测显存和延迟限制时才有实用价值。论文关注部署约束下多语言 LLM 推理的混合精度量化，而非仅追求更低 bitwidth。

Result: 最终配置在 FINAL-81 的 5,168 项上通过所有门控：权重加载显存 5.917 GiB（限制 5.98），韩语教师正确保留率 0.904（门控 0.90），相对 BF16 的 TPOT 比为 0.939。相比 NVFP4 W4A4，配对准确率提升 +1.4 个百分点（Bonferroni 调整区间 [+0.2, +2.6]），但选择集上为 +3.4 个百分点；INT2 的七种网格均在生成上退化。

Insight: 创新点在于部署约束驱动的混合精度分配与严格评估流程：验证所有融合模块的原生打包执行、使用预注册门控和未触碰最终集，并揭示模块恢复效应强非加性、韩语与推理敏感度排序不一致。客观看，其价值更多来自系统级可部署性验证与可靠评测方法，而非单一 bitwidth 创新；最终 checkpoint 未保留、逐位重建可复现性未验证是主要局限。

Abstract: Low-bit LLM quantization is useful only if a model preserves service-relevant accuracy, executes through supported GPU kernels, and meets measured memory and latency limits. We study Qwen3-8B on NVIDIA B200 with vLLM, including INT2/INT3, GPTQ, NVFP4, and mixed-precision configurations. We verify native packed execution and precision on all 144 fused linear modules and evaluate Korean, English, numerical, and reasoning quality under preregistered gates. Packed INT2 and INT3 execute, but all seven tested INT2 grids degenerate on generation. Isolated module-restoration effects are strongly non-additive; Korean and reasoning sensitivity rankings disagree, and a promising INT4/INT8 development candidate fails independent final evaluation. A configuration with 16-bit activations, 98 NVFP4 weight-only modules, 46 INT8 modules, and an INT8 output head passes all gates on the untouched FINAL-81 set (5,168 items): 5.917 GiB of weight-loading memory (limit 5.98), Korean teacher-correct retention 0.904 (gate 0.90), and TPOT ratio 0.939 relative to BF16. It improves paired accuracy over NVFP4 W4A4 by +1.4 percentage points (Bonferroni-adjusted interval [+0.2, +2.6]), compared with +3.4 points on the selection set. This comparison changes both activation precision and INT8 protection; exploratory analyses suggest that the activation change contributes substantially, but the isolated allocation effect is not established. Final checkpoints were not retained, and bitwise rebuild reproducibility remains unverified.

[140] VisHead-Spec: Head-Routed Visual Attention for Multimodal Speculative Decoding cs.LG | cs.CVPDF

Xin Guo, Arushi Rai, Xiang Zhou, Qiang Zhang, Siyu Lin

TL;DR: 论文面向多模态视觉语言模型的高并发投机解码，发现草稿模型只需少量KV头直接关注目标模型顶层视觉特征，其余头可仅处理文本，从而提出VisHead-Spec：固定选择少量KV头读取图像特征，以降低草稿预测计算量并提升吞吐。

Details

Motivation: 现有投机解码在高并发批量下受计算瓶颈限制，多模态场景中长图像token进一步加重草稿模型预测开销，块扩散草稿器在高并发下的加速仍有限（<9%）。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B和MMSpec上，VisHead-Spec-8仅让40个KV头中的8个读取图像，接受长度与DSpark基线差距在0.3%以内；batch size 64时相对自回归解码吞吐提升16.0%，高于DSpark的9.5%，batch size 1时加速与DSpark相当。

Insight: 创新点在于识别并利用目标模型到草稿模型间视觉注意力的稀疏模式，通过KV头路由让少数头关注目标模型顶层视觉特征、其余头专注文本，从而在不明显损失接受长度的情况下降低多模态投机解码开销。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates vision-language models(VLMs) while preserving the target model’s outputs, but its speedup diminishes under high concurrency serving as the system becomes more compute bounded as the batch size increases. This problem is pronounced for multimodal speculative decoding as the long range of the image tokens in the prompt can significantly increase the computational cost during draft token prediction. The block-diffusion drafters (e.g. DFlash, DSpark) can effectively speed up the draft model efficiency by predicting multiple draft tokens in one forward pass, but their speedup for VLMs under high concurrency is still modest (<9%). In order to understand this gap, we examine the information flow between different modalities from target model to draft model and identify the sparsity pattern of visual attention between the target and draft models: only small set of key-value heads in the draft model need direct visual feature attention in the context and only visual features from top level layers in the target model need to be attended in the context. We therefore propose VisHead-Spec, which selects a small, fixed set of key-value (KV) heads to attend to all image features from top level layers in the target model and restricts the remaining heads to text features. On Qwen3-VL-8B and MMSpec, VisHead-Spec-8 lets 8 of the drafter’s 40 KV heads read the image while keeping accepted length within 0.3% of the block-diffusion draft model baseline (i.e. DSpark). At batch size 64, its throughput gain over autoregressive decoding increases to 16.0%, compared with 9.5% for DSpark; at batch size 1, its speedup remains comparable to DSpark.

[141] Solver to System: LLM Reasoning Across Representations and Execution in Scientific Simulation cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Eliza Berman, Rohan Subramanian, Shreya Jha, Lohit Jagarapu, Romain Lopez

TL;DR: 论文提出 Solver2System 数据集，用同一 PDE 系统的代码、方程、自然语言和数值轨迹等多表示形式，以及受控错误和词法扰动，评估 LLM 能否跨表示恢复物理系统并判断物理有效性。作者测试八个开源权重模型，发现模型在证据冲突时偏好特定表示，代码词法扰动会影响对其他未变表示的理解；执行能改善物理有效性判断，但正确判断不一定意味着能识别因果缺陷。

Details

Motivation: PDE 求解器代码的生成与解释对 LLM 仍很困难，而科学仿真代码既能以多种表示描述同一系统，又可能“代码可执行但物理错误”，因此是检验 LLM 科学推理与跨表示一致性的理想测试平台。

Result: 在八个开源权重模型上，LLM 在跨表示证据冲突时系统性偏向某些表示，代码的词法扰动会改变其对其他未变表示的解读；引入执行可提升物理有效性判断，但较弱智能体可能给出正确判断却无法定位因果缺陷。

Insight: 论文构建了带多表示、受控错误和词法扰动的 PDE 求解器数据集，并同时考察跨表示一致性与静态/智能体式物理无效检测；其价值在于揭示 LLM 的科学判断可能依赖表示偏好或表面线索，而非真正完成表示调和与因果诊断。

Abstract: Simulators based on partial differential equations (PDEs) are widely used to model physical systems, but generating and interpreting PDE solver code with large language models (LLMs) remains challenging. Scientific simulation code is a useful testbed for LLM scientific reasoning because (1) one system can be represented by code, equations, natural language, and numerical trajectories and (2) code can execute successfully while implementing invalid physics. We introduce Solver2System, a dataset of PDE solvers with multiple representations, controlled corruptions, and lexical perturbations, to evaluate whether LLMs can recover the underlying physical system through (1) cross-representation consistency and (2) static and agentic detection of physical invalidity. Across eight open-weight models, LLMs systematically favor some representations when evidence conflicts, and lexical perturbations to code alter interpretation of other unchanged representations. Execution improves physical-validity judgments, but weaker agents can judge correctly without identifying the causal defect. Thus, LLMs may reach correct judgments despite inconsistent representation reconciliation or causal misdiagnosis.

[142] Large Language Models as Nonlinear Dynamical Systems: The Three Clocks of Depth, Training, and Generation cs.LG | cs.CL | math.DS | nlin.CDPDF

Maor Farid

TL;DR: 论文提出用非线性动力系统视角统一理解大语言模型，将模型在深度（层迭代）、训练和生成三个“时钟”上的行为映射到不动点、分岔、同步、混沌和势阱逃逸等经典概念。作者构建“三时钟词典”汇总约200篇论文的15个概念对应关系，并用降阶模型解释softmax、梯度下降、loss spike、注意力与重复循环等现象。实验在GPT-2和Pythia等开放模型上验证重复循环逃逸符合Arrhenius律，并讨论用逃逸分析设计控制器，但尚未超过最佳固定温度。

Details

Motivation: 论文旨在弥合非线性动力学与大语言模型研究两个社区之间的隔阂，避免相同动力学现象被反复以新名称重新发现。其出发点是：Transformer在层数、训练步和生成token三个维度上迭代，天然构成动力系统，可用经典非线性动力学概念解释和预测LLM行为，并进一步解决重复循环等问题。

Result: 论文构建了三时钟词典和多种降阶模型，并在笔记本CPU上对开放语言模型进行验证：GPT-2和Pythia的重复循环逃逸遵循Arrhenius律，logit gap充当势垒，逃逸所需温度随生成长度N约按1/lnN下降、随模型规模上升；连贯文本存在于较窄温度窗口。训练使Pythia各层更“扩张”，小型Transformer在多模式稳定边缘训练；基于逃逸分析的控制器能消除循环，但尚未优于最佳固定温度。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新点在于提出“深度、训练、生成”三时钟统一框架，系统建立非线性动力学概念与LLM行为的可证明/可测量/可猜想对应，并给出softmax为玻尔兹曼分布、注意力为Kuramoto模型、重复循环为加深势阱等降阶解释。客观来看，其价值在于用动力系统语言统一多尺度LLM现象并提供可检验预测（如Arrhenius律和温度尺度），但控制器应用仍停留在初步验证阶段，尚未展示超越现有基线的实用优势。

Abstract: Language models are dynamical systems. A transformer is iterated over its layers, over the steps of its training and over the tokens it generates, and on each of these three clocks it shows behavior that nonlinear dynamics has studied for a century: fixed points, bifurcations, synchronization, chaos and noise-driven escape from potential wells. The two communities rarely meet, so this behavior is often rediscovered under new names. We build a bridge between them. To describe, we condense about 200 papers into the Three-Clock Dictionary, which maps fifteen concepts of nonlinear dynamics onto each clock and marks every correspondence as proved, measured or conjectured, and we show each concept in a mechanical system and in a language model side by side. To explain, we derive reduced-order models: the softmax is a Boltzmann distribution, gradient descent an Euler step on a nonlinear spring, a loss spike a ball thrown out of a stiff valley, attention on the circle a Kuramoto model, and a repetition loop a well that deepens while it is occupied. We then test these explanations on open language models, on a laptop CPU. Escape from repetition loops in GPT-2 and Pythia follows the Arrhenius law with the logit gap as the barrier, and the barrier deepens and then saturates, so the temperature needed to leave a loop falls like $1/\ln N$ with the generation length $N$ and rises with model size. Coherent text lives in a narrow window of temperatures, which a small reasoning model shares at its recommended temperature. Training makes each Pythia layer more expansive, and a small transformer trains at a multi-mode edge of stability. The third step, solving problems of language models, is still ahead: controllers derived from the escape analysis remove loops but do not yet beat the best fixed temperature. We state twelve open problems where nonlinear dynamics could get there.

[143] RiDEx: Reward-Informed Directional Exploration for Diffusion Reinforcement Learning cs.LG | cs.CVPDF

Song Yan, Chenfeng Wang, Wei Zhai, Xingchen Wang, Tao Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出RiDEx，一种面向扩散模型强化学习后训练的奖励引导方向性探索方法。它不再在高维潜空间中做各向同性随机扰动，而是将局部探索集中到去噪速度场的奖励相关子空间，并通过同一生成状态下的等大反向扰动测量终端奖励响应，从而提供带符号的局部监督，同时所有生成图像参与全局DiffusionNFT学习。

Details

Motivation: 现有基于奖励的扩散后训练方法虽然改进了采样和策略优化，但探索仍主要依赖高维潜空间中的各向同性随机扰动，扰动预算常被浪费在对目标奖励影响很小的方向上，导致探索效率低。论文关注扩散模型应在何处探索以及如何从探索反馈中有效学习。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium上，RiDEx在四个对齐与偏好任务中取得评估方法中最高的峰值奖励；其梯度方向实例在HPSv2和PickScore上相比DiffusionNFT分别实现15倍和29倍的目标达成加速（以H200 GPU小时计）。受控实验表明，相同扰动幅度下奖励对齐方向能引发更强响应，应用其测量信用可提升全部四个任务的曲线平均奖励。

Insight: 创新点在于将探索空间选择与反馈学习耦合：利用条件残差或可微奖励梯度提出奖励相关子空间，并在同一生成状态施加等大反向扰动以测量局部奖励响应，形成带符号监督并与全局DiffusionNFT学习结合。客观来看，其价值在于把探索方向约束到奖励敏感子空间，提升扰动预算利用率和样本效率，并通过反事实式局部信用估计增强扩散强化学习后训练的探索效率。

Abstract: Reward-based diffusion post-training updates model parameters from task rewards. While existing approaches improve sampling and policy optimization, they still rely largely on isotropic random perturbations in a high-dimensional latent space for exploration. Such variation often spends the perturbation budget on changes with little influence on the target reward, making exploration inefficient. We study where diffusion models should explore and how to learn from that exploration. We introduce Reward-Informed Directional Exploration (RiDEx), which concentrates local exploration in reward-relevant subspaces of the denoising velocity field. Conditional residuals or differentiable reward gradients propose the subspace, and equal-and-opposite perturbations at the same generation state measure its terminal reward response. The reward difference supplies signed local supervision in the explored coordinates, while all generated images contribute to global DiffusionNFT learning. RiDEx thereby couples exploration-space selection with feedback about the changes it produces. On Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, RiDEx attains the highest peak rewards among the evaluated methods on four alignment and preference tasks. Its gradient-direction instantiation achieves 15x and 29x target-attainment speedups over DiffusionNFT on HPSv2 and PickScore, measured in H200 GPU hours. Controlled experiments confirm that reward-aligned directions elicit stronger responses at equal perturbation magnitude, and that applying their measured credit improves curve-average reward across all four tasks.

Abstract: We post-train NVIDIA’s Nemotron 3.5 Super (120B total, 12B active parameters) with reinforcement learning (RL) on five expert-built task datasets, one at a time: real-world software engineering, complex constraints, long-horizon tool use, professional chart understanding and enterprise document reasoning. Each run trains a fresh rank-32 LoRA adapter with CISPO on Tinker, with no supervised stage, and each adapter is compared with the base model on the same tasks, harness and settings. On twelve headline benchmarks, eight built by other groups and four by Surge AI, the gains range from +4.5 points on IFBench to +13.8 on GDP_pdf, which rises from 10.4% to 24.2%; SWE-bench Pro V2 rises from 86.9% to 92.1%, Terminal-Bench 2.1 from 63.9% to 70.3%, MultiChallenge from 58.3% to 66.6%, AutomationBench from 14.8% to 27.5% and Chartography from 39.4% to 50.6%. The document adapter, trained without API tools, also gains +8.0 points on AutomationBench. In attempts read side by side with the base model’s on tasks each adapter newly solves, the base model’s failures recur in a few modes, and each adapter does more often what the base model missed: the tool-use adapter finds the data and the rules before acting, the coding adapter writes its own test or scratch files on SWE-bench Pro V2, the constraints adapter carries earlier turns into its answers, the chart adapter measures the marks in code, and the document adapter reads AutomationBench’s spreadsheets.

[145] What Does Weight-Space Programming Add to Diffusion Editing? Program Construction versus Score-Guided Refinement cs.LG | cs.CVPDF

Zitian Gao, Jingyi Xu, Ziqi Han, Chengkai Lei, Ye Liu

TL;DR: 论文研究了权重空间编程（WSP）与扩散概念编辑结合时，程序构建与分数引导修正两个阶段各自的贡献。WSP让语言模型为另一个网络构建可执行的权重更新，其中程序定义训练上下文、保持约束和更新方向，共享数值拟合器负责选择系数。作者在 Stable Diffusion 1.4 的 24 个 TIMED 测试编辑上，将生成的程序与已发表编辑器、共享面板手写基线和预算匹配搜索进行对比，发现预注册的改写流程相比强基线没有优势；而在探索性比较中，一次性 Astra 程序在全新场景的 VLM 联合成功率上提升 10 个百分点，但用探测和改写程序后，CLIP 提升 6.2 个百分点，联合成功率却下降 9.7 个百分点。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注 WSP 中程序构建相比传统分数引导编辑到底增加了什么价值，以及分数引导的修正是否能够保留程序构建带来的收益，并试图明确 WSP 流程中程序构建与修正应作为两个不同阶段分别评估。

Result: 在 SD 1.4 的 24 个 TIMED 测试编辑上，预注册改写流程未超过强基线；在探索性比较中，Astra 程序使全新场景 VLM 联合成功率提升 10.0 个百分点，而 CLIP 相对匹配没有显著变化。进一步探测并改写这些程序后，CLIP 提升 6.2 个百分点，但联合成功率下降 9.7 个百分点；在固定 144 张图像上，五个额外评估器在整图目标匹配下给出一致方向，但主体局部变化差异不够明确。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新在于将 WSP 流程拆分为“程序构建”和“分数引导修正”两个阶段分别评估，并发现完整生成程序能围绕受保护的文本状态子空间组织可调方向，从而带来实际收益，但分数引导修正不一定保留这些行为。从客观角度看，这属于对 WSP 与扩散编辑结合的系统性实证分析，其价值在于揭示两阶段评估的必要性，而非提出新的编辑算法。

Abstract: Weight-space programming (WSP) lets a language model construct executable weight updates for another network. We study diffusion concept editing through an interface in which programs define training contexts, preservation constraints and update directions, while a shared numerical fitter selects their coefficients. We compare generated programs with published editors, a shared-panel hand-written baseline and budget-matched search over conventional edits on 24 TIMED test edits for Stable Diffusion 1.4. The preregistered rewrite pipeline shows no advantage over the strong baseline. In an exploratory comparison, one-shot Astra programs improve fresh-scene VLM joint success, which requires subject presence together with whole-image target matching, by +10.0 points over the shared-panel baseline, while CLIP relative match shows no detectable change. These programs organise adjustable directions around protected text-state subspaces. Probing and rewriting the Astra programs then raises CLIP by +6.2 points while joint success changes by -9.7. On a fixed 144-image sample, five additional evaluators give point estimates in the same directions under whole-image target matching; checks of subject-local change give less decisive differences. Together, these results identify useful behaviour in complete generated programs and show that score-guided revision need not preserve it: program construction and revision should be evaluated as distinct stages of a WSP pipeline.

[146] Scaling Laws for Vision-Language Fusion cs.LG | cs.CVPDF

Seng Pei Liew, Teppei Suzuki, Seiya Tokui

TL;DR: 论文系统分析视觉语言模型多模态预训练的学习动态，将验证损失建模为基础学习轨迹与模态竞争导致的分布偏移，并推导出统一缩放定律，可跨数据混合比例、训练步数和模型规模预测损失，进而刻画早融合与晚融合的权衡。

Details

Motivation: 视觉语言模型通常采用晚融合，而早融合近期受到关注，但两种范式的根本权衡缺乏理解，导致训练配方昂贵且依赖启发式。论文旨在揭示多模态预训练动态并指导融合决策。

Result: 摘要未给出具体benchmark数值，但宣称缩放定律能准确预测不同混合比例、训练步数和模型规模下的损失；在固定数据预算下，早融合保持文本性能、晚融合偏向视觉性能，且模型规模增大时权衡更倾向早融合，实验验证了该定律可捕捉多模态学习动态并指导融合选择。

Insight: 创新点在于分离基础学习轨迹与模态竞争驱动的分布偏移，并用统一缩放定律描述视觉语言融合动态；客观上看，其可借鉴之处是将融合范式选择转化为可预测的缩放问题，为多模态预训练配方提供定量依据。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have emerged as central foundation models combining multimodal perception with text comprehension. While VLMs are traditionally built via late fusion by continued pre-training an off-the-shelf language model on multimodal (image-text) data, mixing modalities from the start of pre-training (early fusion) has recently gained traction. However, the fundamental trade-offs between these paradigms remain poorly understood, resulting in expensive and heuristic-driven training recipes. In this paper, we systematically analyze the learning dynamics of these multimodal pre-training processes, showing that validation losses during vision-language fusion can be modeled by separating the base learning trajectory, where different data types contribute at distinct rates, from a distribution shift driven by the competition between modalities. We derive a scaling law that unifiedly describes these dynamics, enabling accurate loss predictions across mixture ratios, training steps, and model sizes. Crucially, our law characterizes the trade-off between early and late fusion: under a fixed data budget, early fusion preserves text performance while late fusion favors vision performance, yielding a trade-off that increasingly tilts toward early fusion as model size scales. Extensive experiments confirm that our scaling law reliably captures multimodal learning dynamics and governs fusion decisions.

[147] When the tail is earned by its companions: measuring label dependence in long-tailed multi-label classification cs.LG | cs.CL | cs.IRPDF

Christoffer Löffler

TL;DR: 论文研究长尾多标签分类中稀有标签依赖共现频繁标签（companions）而被召回的现象，指出Macro-F1等聚合指标会掩盖这种标签依赖。作者提出标签内估计量、判读规则及基于训练标签的预训练上界，用于在训练前衡量报告召回可在多大程度上超过无companion召回。实验覆盖23个语料、7种训练目标和5类标签相关机制，发现无companion召回普遍不足，且现有长尾目标未能降低依赖，但模型架构会改变依赖程度。

Details

Motivation: 稀有标签常与频繁标签共现，分类器可能不学习稀有标签本身，而是借助companion标签的预测将其召回；现有诊断依赖已训练模型，且Macro-F1无法反映标签召回在有无companion情形间的分配，因此需要在训练前识别语料是否允许该失败并量化标准聚合指标隐藏的依赖。

Result: 在23个语料、7种训练目标和5类标签相关机制上，所有6个可定义估计量的语料中companion-free recall均不足；最多18.4%的尾部真阳性Macro-F1贡献会在无companion召回率下消失，且均处于训练标签上界内。没有长尾目标能降低该依赖，但架构会改变依赖，尾部得分由其他标签预测计算的模型依赖最强。

Insight: 创新点在于提出within-label estimand刻画标签召回在有无companion间的分配，给出判读规则和仅依赖训练标签的预训练上界，从而在训练前量化报告召回可能被companion虚高的程度。客观看，其价值在于揭示聚合指标掩盖的标签依赖、提供可预训练检验的统计量，并指出长尾目标不必然缓解依赖而架构选择更关键。

Abstract: A rare label seldom occurs alone, and a classifier can recover it from the frequent labels it co-occurs with, its companions, without having learned the label itself. Existing diagnostics of this need a trained model: none says before training whether a corpus admits the failure, or what the standard aggregate hides. Macro-F1, like any measure built from per-label confusion counts, is unchanged by how a label’s recall divides between occurrences with and without a companion. We introduce a within-label estimand of that division, a reading rule under which a fall in dependence counts only if companion-free recall rises, and a statistic of training labels that bounds, before training, how far reported recall can exceed companion-free recall. Across 23 corpora, 7 training objectives and five families of label-correlation mechanisms, companion-free recall falls short on all six corpora where the estimand is defined. Up to 18.4% of the tail true positives macro-F1 credits would vanish at the companion-free rate, within the training-label bound on every corpus. No long-tail objective reduces the dependence, but architecture moves it. Models whose tail score is computed from other labels’ predictions are strongly dependent.

[148] The Illusion of Grounding: Do Vision-Language Models Weigh Retrieved Text and Images Like an Ideal Observer? cs.LG | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Basavaraj R M, Harshal Chandel

TL;DR: 论文提出 GIB-200K 基准，基于 COCO 和 Visual Genome 构建 20 万图像-问题样本，并加入检索文本噪声、视觉模糊和跨模态冲突等受控压力条件，用于研究检索增强视觉语言模型如何权衡文本与图像证据。作者从理想贝叶斯线索组合出发定义 Knowledge Inertia 和 Sensory Capture，并用误差校正回归估计模态权重，评估 4 个开源模型。

Details

Motivation: 检索增强 VLM 同时依赖可能错误的检索文本和可能退化或矛盾的图像，仅测性能下降不足以说明模型如何融合两种证据，因此需要形式化衡量模型是否像理想观察者一样合理权衡文本与图像。

Result: 4 个开源模型在干净输入上准确率为 92.5%-94.2%，但在对抗条件下降至 41.3%-59.9%；两个 LLaVA 模型过度依赖文本（w_T/w_I=1.229 和 1.322），InternVL3 更偏向图像（0.680），且当文档撒谎时有 34.8%-71.4% 原本正确的干净答案被放弃。

Insight: 创新点在于构建带反事实翻转和跨模态冲突的受控基准，提出 Knowledge Inertia、Sensory Capture 和 Grounding Illusion Score 等可测试指标，并用测量误差校正回归估计模态权重，从而把“是否真正 grounding”转化为可量化的模态偏置分析。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented vision-language models (VLMs) answer from two sources of evidence that can each fail: the retrieved text can be wrong or noisy, and the image can be degraded or contradicted. We introduce GIB-200K, 200,000 image-question items built from COCO and Visual Genome, each expanded into controlled stress conditions: hard-negative retrieval noise (1-20 documents), visual ambiguity (Gaussian and motion blur, low-resource crops) and cross-modal conflict with counterfactual flip pairs. Instead of only measuring degradation, we ask how models combine the two modalities. From ideal Bayesian cue combination we derive a testable prediction that yields formal definitions of Knowledge Inertia (text over-weighted relative to the model’s own image evidence) and Sensory Capture (the reverse), and we estimate the weights with a measurement-error-corrected regression whose recovery of known weights we verify. A counterfactual flip decomposition separates modality bias from concept priors, and a Grounding Illusion Score measures how much clean accuracy is not grounded in the image. We evaluate 4 open models on one frozen, seeded evaluation split of 2,000 GIB-200K items. All models are accurate on clean inputs (92.5%-94.2%) but adverse accuracy falls to 41.3%-59.9%. The two LLaVA models over-weight the text (Knowledge Inertia, w_T/w_I = 1.229 and 1.322), whereas InternVL3 favours the image (w_T/w_I = 0.680); between 34.8% and 71.4% of the clean answers that are correct are abandoned when the document lies. A training-free consistency check that compares the answer with and without retrieval trades coverage for accuracy under misleading documents, while falling back to the image fails when the image is heavily degraded. All per-instance outputs, code, and an audit that recomputes every reported open-model number are released.

[149] Ambient LLMs: Data-Efficient Supervised Fine-tuning via Temperature Matching cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Giannis Daras, Marios Papachristou, Adam Klivans, Antonio Torralba, Constantinos Daskalakis

TL;DR: 论文提出 Ambient SFT，一种数据高效的监督微调框架：利用冻结的预训练模型作为含噪教师，要求微调模型在高温条件下的条件分布与预训练模型的高温分布粗粒度匹配，从而缓解小规模 SFT 数据导致的过拟合和灾难性遗忘。温度参数控制对预训练模型的信任程度，并统一了标准 SFT 与知识蒸馏。

Details

Motivation: 收集大规模、多样且高质量的后训练数据困难，SFT 数据通常远小于预训练数据，容易过拟合并遗忘数据分布尾部。论文希望在不依赖更多高质量标注数据的情况下，防止微调导致分布坍塌。

Result: 在领域适应、指令微调和推理任务上，该方法优于标准 SFT，平均相对提升最高达 21%；摘要未给出具体 benchmark 名称和更细粒度指标。

Insight: 创新点是用预训练模型的高温条件分布作为粗粒度正则/蒸馏目标，并通过温度调节在标准 SFT 与知识蒸馏之间连续权衡。客观看，其价值在于将不完美预测转化为数据高效微调信号，兼顾适配新任务与保留预训练知识，减少对大规模高质量后训练数据的依赖。

Abstract: Collecting large-scale, diverse, and high-quality post-training datasets for autoregressive language models is challenging. Consequently, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) is typically applied to datasets significantly smaller than those used for pre-training, which leads to overfitting and the catastrophic forgetting of the tails of the data distribution. To prevent this collapse, we introduce Ambient SFT, a principled framework for leveraging imperfect predictions to augment the fine-tuning set. In our method, a “frozen” version of the pre-trained model serves as a noisy teacher that the fine-tuned model must match at a coarse level. In particular, we require that the high-temperature versions of the conditional distributions produced by the post-training model should match the high-temperature versions of the pre-training ones. The temperature hyperparameter controls the level of trust in the pre-trained model, with the limit $T\to \infty$ reverting back to regular SFT and $T=1$ to knowledge distillation. We demonstrate that our approach outperforms standard SFT across domain adaptation, instruction-tuning, and reasoning, leading for up to $21%$ average relative improvement to standard SFT.
