[150] CodeReel: Accelerating Code-to-Video Agents with Exact Frame Reuse and Blocking-Aware Scheduling cs.MM | cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.DCPDF

Junyi Shen, Yao Lu

TL;DR: 论文针对代码生成视频的智能体（在“构建—检查—修改”循环中用程序生成影片），提出 CodeReel：一个无需训练、位于智能体已有命令背后的执行层。它用“精确帧复用”（按帧的绘制命令为其建立对应关系，不考虑该帧在时间轴上的位置，只要该绘制命令再次出现就直接复用）和“阻塞感知调度”（先跑检查，同时以空闲优先级并行启动渲染，除非最近的构建很可能无法通过）来缩短智能体的等待时间，并发布可重放的智能体会话基准 EditLoop。

Details

Motivation: 视频编码智能体单部影片的中位成本为 2.85 美元、耗时 11 分钟，其中超过三分之一的时间花在阻塞智能体继续执行的构建（渲染）上；作者发现这些时间大部分可以避免：会话后续影片有 37.5% 的帧此前已经画过（只是发生时间平移或位于被编辑的场景内），且一半构建会停在检查处，智能体只需要检查结论，但哪些构建会停无法事先知道。

Result: 在 EditLoop 基准上，CodeReel 相对带并行渲染的 warm pipeline “Rebuild” 将智能体等待缩短 36.3%，相对逐步执行的“Sequential”缩短 57.9%；在时间平移编辑中其复用渲染比例为 43.9%，接近像素为新的 43.0%，而 Manim 风格缓存渲染高达 99.8%；真实智能体上，Claude Code 中每次构建的中位等待为 Rebuild 的 0.62 倍（所用主题从未参与开发），Codex CLI 中为 0.67 倍，且增益在 5 个模型、2 个框架上成立。

Insight: 创新点在于将帧缓存键设计为绘制命令本身而非时间位置，从而支持跨版本、跨时间平移甚至复用失败版本已渲染的帧；并利用检查与渲染的依赖不确定性做阻塞感知调度（先检查、低优先级并行渲染）。无需训练、透明嵌入现有命令，同时提出可对照从头渲染验证的 EditLoop 基准，具有较强工程实用性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Coding agents make videos by writing and refining programs in a build–inspect–edit loop. A finished film costs a median of $2.85 in model calls and 11 minutes, over a third of that time in builds that block the agent. We find that much of this time is avoidable, for two reasons. First, 37.5% of the frames of a session’s later films were drawn before, many shifted in time or inside an edited scene. Second, half of the builds stop at a check, where the agent needs only the verdict, but which builds stop is unknown until the checks finish. We present CodeReel, a training-free execution layer behind agents’ existing commands. Exact frame reuse keys each frame by its drawing commands, regardless of its position in time, and reuses each frame whose key recurs. Blocking-aware scheduling runs the checks first and starts the render alongside them, at idle priority, unless the session’s recent builds make a pass unlikely. Scheduling spends CPU to answer sooner and reuse returns it; exact keys let later versions use frames rendered for a failed one as the agent thinks. We introduce EditLoop, a benchmark of replayable agent sessions whose films are checked against renders from scratch. On it, CodeReel cuts the agent’s wait by 36.3% against Rebuild, a warm pipeline with parallel rendering, and by 57.9% against Sequential, today’s step-by-step pipeline. On time-shifting edits its reuse renders 43.9%, close to the 43.0% whose pixels are new, while Manim-style caching renders 99.8%. With real agents, the median wait per build is 0.62x Rebuild’s in Claude Code, on topics never used in development, and 0.67x in Codex CLI. The gain holds for five models and two frameworks.

[151] HubEgo-100K: In-the-Wild Egocentric Video of Skilled Work for Robot Learning cs.RO | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Javad Amirian, Armin Kiani, Loïc Schaeffer, Tim Sprecher, Mohamed Taha

TL;DR: 论文提出HubEgo-100K，一个面向机器人学习、以多样性为目标的大规模野外第一视角人类视频数据集。v1.0包含10,124小时1080p视频、同步100Hz IMU数据，覆盖12,227名贡献者、15,548个环境和大量工作场景，并提供72,754个带机器生成动作与语言标注的片段。论文还测量了各维度多样性，并讨论其与策略性能相关数据属性的联系。

Details

Motivation: 模仿学习训练的机器人策略在训练环境外泛化较差，而数据扩展研究表明泛化更依赖环境和物体多样性而非单环境演示数量。机器人数据跨环境采集成本高，现有第一视角视频又多为日常生活，缺乏真实工作场所的技能工作数据。

Result: 摘要未报告策略性能benchmark或SOTA结果，主要报告数据集v1.0规模与多样性测量：10,124小时视频、72,754个片段、15,548个环境、83%时长来自工作场所，并显示随机顺序下新贡献者和词汇仍持续出现；后续版本将加入benchmarks。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点是构建面向机器人学习多样性的大规模野外第一视角技能工作视频数据集，包含同步IMU、多贡献者多环境、机器生成动作与语言标注及隐私/格式规范。客观看，其创新主要在数据资源采集与多样性量化，而非新算法，并将数据属性与已发表策略性能证据关联。

Abstract: Robot policies trained by imitation generalize poorly outside the environments and objects seen in training. Published data-scaling studies find that generalization grows with the diversity of training environments and objects rather than with more demonstrations per environment. Robot data is costly to collect across many environments, whereas egocentric human video can be recorded in the wild at scale; existing egocentric video, however, mostly shows daily-life activity and contains little skilled work in real workplaces. We present HubEgo-100K, a dataset built for diversity. This version of the paper covers the first release, v1.0: 10,124 hours of 1080p egocentric video with synchronized 100 Hz inertial (IMU) data from one head-mounted phone camera. In v1.0, 12,227 contributors record their own unscripted work in 15,548 environments (each one contributor’s own setting of one type, such as their kitchen or workshop) across 11 setting types, 30 work categories, 56 task types and 35 action families, with 83% of hours in workplace settings. The release comprises 72,754 episodes with machine-generated action and language annotations, in LeRobot and MCAP formats under CC BY 4.0. We measure diversity along each axis, show that inside v1.0, with episodes in random order, new contributors and words keep appearing, and relate the measurements to published evidence on six data properties tied to policy performance, including hand visibility and inertial sensing. We also document collection, curation, privacy measures and data format, and provide a datasheet. Later versions of this paper will add benchmarks and cover further releases toward 100,000 hours.

[152] CRA: Continuous Risk Assessment for Safety-critical Visual Navigation cs.RO | cs.CV | eess.SYPDF

Lihao Zheng, Ying Wu, Chen Jiang, Randy Freeman

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向安全关键视觉导航的连续风险评估框架CRA，利用RGB与LiDAR观测估计物体的动能、势能、接触严重性和热风险四类风险因子，并融合为连续风险分数，再结合Poisson安全函数与Laplace引导场构成安全过滤器，实现开放世界机器人导航中的在线监测与干预，并在仿真餐厅场景中演示。

Details

Motivation: 传统安全关键控制通常将状态划分为安全/不安全二值区域，但现实风险往往随物体或人的运动、物理属性和交互上下文连续变化，例如高速运动且携带重物或高温物体的人需要不同于静态障碍物的安全响应。

Result: 摘要仅说明在仿真餐厅场景中进行了演示，未提及定量实验结果、benchmark对比或SOTA水平。

Insight: 创新点在于将多模态感知估计的动能、势能、接触严重性和热风险融合为连续风险分数，并引入Poisson安全函数与Laplace引导场作为安全过滤器，实现开放世界视觉导航中的在线风险监测与干预。

Abstract: Traditional safety-critical control methods often rely on predefined constraints that separate states into safe and unsafe regions. While recent studies have extended geometric safety toward semantic safety, real-world safety is rarely purely binary. The risk associated with an object or human can vary continuously with its physical properties, like motion, and interaction context; for example, a person carrying a heavy object or a hot object moving at high speed may require a different safety response from an ordinary static obstacle. In this paper, we propose a vision-based continuous risk assessment framework for safety-critical visual navigation. Our method uses RGB and LiDAR observations to estimate four risk factors (kinetic, potential, contact-severity, and thermal) and combines them into a continuous risk score for each assessed object or person. These estimates are integrated into a Poisson safety function with a Laplace guidance field as a safety filter, enabling online monitoring and intervention in open-world robot navigation. We demonstrate the proposed method in a simulated restaurant scene.

[153] Heave-Ho: Decentralized Long-Horizon Multi-Humanoid Collaboration cs.RO | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Ting-Hsuan Chen, Siheng Zhao, Han Jiang, Qi Pan, Louison Lu

TL;DR: 论文提出Heave-Ho，一个面向多 humanoid 长时程协作的去中心化框架，涵盖低层多智能体后训练控制与高层视觉语言规划及语音协调机制。系统仅用两个相同 humanoid 训练，却能扩展到最多十个机器人并泛化到异构团队。作者还构建了HH-Bench基准，并在仿真评估与真实实验中验证了方法的有效性。

Details

Motivation: 多 humanoid 协作有望突破单机器人能力上限，但面临全身协调交互、长时程规划、机器人间精确同步以及去中心化执行等挑战。论文旨在实现无需网络通信、仅依赖本地观测的长时程多 humanoid 移动操作协作。

Result: 论文提出HH-Bench，包含12个涉及2至10个 humanoid（含异构团队）的任务。系统评估显示Heave-Ho持续优于基线方法，真实世界实验也展示了其在挑战性任务中的鲁棒去中心化协作能力。

Insight: 创新点包括：低层用多智能体后训练将人形基础跟踪模型适配到机器人间物理耦合交互；高层结合视觉语言规划器与基于语音的领导者-跟随者协调机制，实现无网络通信同步；仅用双机器人训练即可扩展到十机器人并泛化到异构团队。客观看，其去中心化、语音协调与可扩展泛化设计以及HH-Bench构建具有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Collaboration promises to extend humanoid capabilities beyond what individual robots can achieve. However, realizing this potential requires coordinated whole-body interactions, coherent long-horizon planning, and precise synchronization across robots. We introduce Heave-Ho, a decentralized framework for long-horizon collaborative multi-humanoid loco-manipulation. At the low level, we develop a multi-agent post-training framework that adapts a humanoid foundation tracking model to physically coupled interactions between humanoids, enabling coordinated decentralized control from local observations alone. At the high level, we train a vision-language planner and design a speech-based leader-follower coordination mechanism to synchronize decentralized execution without network communication. Trained with two identical humanoids, our system scales to teams of up to ten robots and generalizes to heterogeneous teams without retraining. To benchmark these capabilities, we introduce HH-Bench, comprising 12 tasks involving teams of 2 to 10 humanoids, including heterogeneous teams. Systematic evaluations show that Heave-Ho consistently outperforms baseline methods, while real-world experiments demonstrate robust decentralized collaboration across challenging tasks.

[154] TCP4D: A Unified Tool-Center-Point Tracking and Generation Model for Robot Manipulation cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Haosong Peng, Xiangyang Liu, Zhengshen Zhang, Yunfeng Fan, Tianyu Qi

TL;DR: 论文提出TCP4D，一个以工具中心点（TCP）位姿为动作空间的统一模型，利用共享4D场景表示同时跟踪观测到的TCP运动并生成未来TCP轨迹。跟踪器从单目视频和初始2D TCP查询中恢复SE(3)度量TCP位姿、夹爪状态、场景几何与相机参数；生成器沿恢复轨迹采样4D特征，结合机器人观测和语言指令预测未来TCP状态。论文还提出跨本体增强管线，将RGB视频重定向并合成新本体演示，无需源机器人状态。

Details

Motivation: 机器人操作视频蕴含丰富视觉经验，但从RGB观测中恢复机器人动作和夹爪状态标注仍很困难，且动作表示与机器人本体耦合，限制跨本体利用。论文希望以与本体无关的TCP位姿统一动作空间，降低标注依赖并支持跨本体学习。

Result: 跟踪器在DROID和RoboLab上绝对轨迹误差分别为7.60 mm和16.07 mm，仅用10条演示即可适应新本体；生成器在RoboTwin上平均成功率为69.19%，并在两个真实任务上优于成熟策略模型；源数据与增强演示联合训练使目标机器人成功率相比仅用增强数据提升41.5%。

Insight: 创新点在于用共享4D场景表示统一TCP运动跟踪与未来轨迹生成，并以TCP位姿作为跨本体动作空间。客观来看，将单目视频恢复、4D特征采样和跨本体运动重定向结合，为无源机器人状态下的演示增强和策略学习提供了可借鉴范式。

Abstract: Robot manipulation videos provide a rich visual experience, yet recovering robot action and gripper-state annotations from RGB observations still remains challenging. Using the embodiment-agnostic tool-center-point (TCP) pose as the action space, we present TCP4D, a unified model in which a shared 4D scene representation both tracks observed TCP motion and generates future TCP trajectories. Given a monocular video and initial 2D TCP queries, the tracker directly recovers metric TCP poses in SE(3) and gripper states alongside scene geometry and camera parameters. The generator samples local features from the same 4D representation along the recovered TCP tracks, then combines them with robot observations and language instructions to predict future TCP states. Our new cross-embodiment augmentation pipeline converts existing RGB videos into demonstrations for alternative embodiments by retargeting the recovered motion and synthesizing the corresponding observations, without recorded source-robot states. Experiments show that the tracker achieves absolute trajectory errors of 7.60 and 16.07 mm on DROID and RoboLab and adapts to a new embodiment with only ten demonstrations. The generator achieves 69.19% mean success on RoboTwin and outperforms well-established policy models on two real-world tasks, while joint training on source and augmented demonstrations improves target-robot success by 41.5% over augmented-only training.
